# American Express Membership Rewards® Credit Card

Welcome Gift of 4,000 Bonus Membership Rewards® Points

We are temporarily pausing the acceptance of new proprietary Card applications in India as we integrate new platforms for our domestic Card issuing operations. We apologize for any inconvenience and appreciate your understanding.

Compare Card

First Year Fee: Rs. 1,000 plus applicable taxes (Second Year onwards: Rs. 4,500 plus applicable taxes)

Card Type

[Credit Card](https://www.americanexpress.com/in/credit-cards/)

Annual fee

First Year fee : Rs. 1,000 plus applicable taxes

Second Year onwards : Rs. 4,500 plus applicable taxes

Reward Earn Rate

Earn 1 Membership Rewards® Point for every2 Rs. 50 spent except for spend on Fuel, Insurance, Utilities, Cash Transactions and EMI conversion at Point of Sale2.

Fee Waiver

The Annual Membership Renewal fee of Rs.4500 will be 100% waived off if total spends on American Express Credit Card in the immediately preceding membership year is Rs.1,50,000 and above; else 50% annual membership renewal fee will be waived off if total spends on American Express Credit Card in the immediately preceding membership year is Rs.90,000 to Rs.1,49,999.

## Key Benefits

### BONUS MEMBERSHIP REWARDS POINTS

Earn 1,000 Bonus Membership Rewards Points for simply using your card 4 times on transactions of Rs. 1,500 or above in a calendar month.

### Monthly Spend Milestone

Earn an additional 1,000 Membership Rewards points simply by spending INR 20,000 or more in a calendar month. This is an enrollment based benefit. Click [here](https://amex.co/mrccenroll) to enroll

### ADDITIONAL REWARDS ON CARD RENEWAL

Earn 5,000 Membership Rewards Points upon First Year Card Renewal, on payment of annual membership fee. Points to be credited within 90 days of Card renewal

### AMERICAN EXPRESS EMI

Split the payment and not the joy. Use your American Express Credit Card for your next big purchase & pay in [EMI](https://www.americanexpress.com/india/membershiprewards/cardmember_offers/pay_in_part.html)

Earn 1 Membership Rewards® Point for every2 Rs. 50 spent except for spend on Fuel, Insurance, Utilities, Cash Transactions and EMI conversion at Point of Sale2.

Contactless Payment

Your American Express Card may be enabled for contactless payments. To see if your Card is contactless, look for the contactless symbol network on the front and back of your Card.

To save time before you apply for your Card, it’s best to make sure you can say yes to the following:

- I/We confirm that I / supplementary card applicant/s is a Resident Indian as defined under the Income Tax Act of India and hereby declare that I/We will notify American Express Banking Corp. if there is a change in the residential status.

- I understand American Express Cards are currently issued to residents of Delhi/NCR, Mumbai, Bangalore, Chennai, Pune, Hyderabad, Jaipur, Indore, Coimbatore, Chandigarh, Ahmedabad, Surat, Vadodra, Lucknow, Ludhiana, Nagpur, Nasik, Trivandrum and Mysuru subject to condition.

- I have a Personal Annual Income of Rs. 6 Lakhs and above (in case of Self Employed or Salaried).

- I am over 18 years of age

- I have an Indian or multinational bank’s savings or current account in India

- I have a good credit history and no payment defaults

- I have a current/permanent residential address in India

- If self-employed: My company has been trading for more than 12 months


Most Important Terms & Conditions

Footnotes

1\. Welcome Gift of 4,000 Membership Rewards Points will be applicable on payment of joining fee and on spending INR 15,000 within first 90 days of Cardmembership.

2.You will not earn Membership Rewards Points on your American Express Card for all your spending on fuel, insurance and utility (electricity, water and gas bills) payments. Fuel includes petrol, diesel, CNG from Oil Marketing Companies (OMCs). Utility services includes providers of household/domestic electricity, gas and water. These providers can be government departments and agencies including local, state, municipal organizations, public housing societies and apartment associations.

Effective March 15, 2020, you will not earn Membership Rewards Points on any EMI option selected from a merchant’s website e.g. online shopping with Amazon or Flipkart etc. and on any EMI option selected at Merchant’s terminal e.g. a Chip and PIN transaction at Croma retail store etc. At the same time, you will continue to earn Membership Rewards Points on all EMI transactions through American Express® SafeKey and on all EMI conversions done post purchase.

3\. 0% Convenience fee on fuel purchase at HPCL for transactions less than Rs. 5,000 and 1% Convenience fee on fuel purchases per transaction is applicable for all transactions of Rs. 5,000 and above

4\. For latest options available under Gold collection please [check here](https://www.americanexpress.com/in/benefits/membership-rewards-card/index.html).

# Swiggy HDFC Bank Credit Card

By [Ankur Mittal](https://cardinsider.com/author/ankur/ "Posts by Ankur Mittal")

Updated On
16 April, 2025

_The Swiggy HDFC Credit Card has become one of the most popular cashback credit cards in India, even surpassing the success of the veteran HDFC Millennia card._

Swiggy, a leading online food ordering platform in India, has launched this co-branded credit card in collaboration with HDFC. It is a cashback card that comes with a very low annual fee of ₹500 + GST (~₹590), considering the numerous benefits it offers. Even this fee is not wasted as cardholders would get a free Swiggy One membership for three months.

With the Swiggy HDFC Credit Card, users can earn 10% cashback on purchases made through Swiggy, including Dineout, Genie, and Instamart. This means that you can not only order food but also buy groceries and enjoy significant discounts on dining out. If you are a frequent Swiggy customer who regularly orders food online, this card could be an excellent option for you, potentially saving you up to ₹42,000 annually.

Swiggy is the only online food ordering service currently offering a co-branded credit card. Continue reading to learn more about the Swiggy HDFC co-branded credit card, including its features, fees, and other important details.

## Swiggy HDFC Bank Credit Card

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/swiggy-hdfc-bank-credit-card/#)

- Complimentary 3-Month Swiggy One Membership as a welcome benefit.
- 10% Cashback across the Swiggy app.
- 5% Cashback on online spends across top brands.
- 1% Cashback on other spends.

[Pros/Cons](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/swiggy-hdfc-bank-credit-card/#)

#### Pros

- Get 10% cashback on all your Swiggy food delivery orders, including Genie and Instamart.
- Up to ₹1,500 cashback per month across major online shopping platforms.
- For food enthusiasts, a complimentary Swiggy One membership for three months is extremely beneficial.

#### Cons

- Although this card offers great benefits on food, it could have included additional perks such as free airport lounge access.

## Welcome Benefit

As a welcome benefit on card activation, you can receive a complimentary 3-month Swiggy One membership. If you already have the membership, you can extend it for 3 months. You must activate your card as per the latest RBI regulation in one of the following ways –

- Make a successful transaction with the credit card
- Use the card via OTP or IVR, set up the card PIN, and enable controls like online transactions, international transactions, etc.

## Up to 10% Cashback With Swiggy HDFC

All cashback earned with the Swiggy HDFC Credit Card will be applied directly to the credit card statement in the following month.

- Get **10% cashback across the** [**Swiggy**](https://www.swiggy.com/) **application**, including food orders, Instamart, Dineout, and Genie. You can earn a **maximum of ₹1,500 per billing cycle**, and the cashback is not applicable to spends made on Swiggy Money Wallet, Swiggy Minis, and Swiggy Liquor. Also, you do not earn cashback on transactions of less than ₹100.
- Get **5% cashback on online spends** made with the card across top brands like Nykaa, BookMyShow, Ola, Myntra, Amazon, Uber, Adidas, H&M, and more. The cashback is applicable on Apparel, Electronics, Entertainment, Home Décor, Department Store, Personal Care, Medical Store, Local Cabs, Pet Stores, and Discount Stores. The **maximum cashback you earn per billing cycle is ₹1,500**. Please remember that 5% cashback does not apply to gift cards and grocery purchases.
- **1% cashback on all other eligible categories**, with the **maximum benefit capped at ₹500 per billing cycle**.

_The following categories are excluded for cashback – fuel, rent, wallet, EMI, jewellery, and government transactions._

## Mastercard World Tier Benefits

As the Swiggy HDFC Bank Card is a MasterCard World Tier card, you get access to many benefits, including complimentary loyalty memberships, dine-and-stay, and more from top brands. You also get various privileges in sports, shopping, travel, arts and culture, and more in many countries worldwide.

- Swiggy HDFC offers four complimentary rounds of green fees each calendar year, with a limit of one round per month. Additionally, members receive twelve complimentary golf lessons annually, one lesson per month. In addition to utilizing the complimentary sessions, golfers can avail of discounted golf services at 50% off the regular green fee. To book these services, please contact 1800-102-6263.
- Up to 12% off on Agoda.
- Enjoy a complimentary stay and dinner for one person at designated Mastercard partner locations worldwide.

![Swiggy HDFC Credit Card Benefits](https://cd9941cc.delivery.rocketcdn.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/Swiggy-HDFC-Credit-Card-Benefits.webp)

## Earn IndiGo BluChips With Swiggy Spends

Discount coupons and cashback with Swiggy HDFC are just the beginning of the benefits. Customers will also [earn IndiGo BluChips](https://cardinsider.com/blog/swiggy-adds-indigo-rewards-partner-earn-bluchips-food-orders/) when they make purchases through the Swiggy app. **This offer is in addition to the advantages of using the Swiggy HDFC Credit Card**. Cardholders will not only receive cashback on their spending but will also be eligible to earn **1 IndiGo BluChip for every ₹100 spent on Swiggy’s platforms**, including Swiggy Food Delivery, Dineout, and Instamart.

## Eligibility Criteria

You must meet certain eligibility requirements to apply for the Swiggy HDFC Bank credit card.

- The applicant must be 21 to 60 years of age in the case of salaried and 21 to 65 years of age in the case of self-employed people.
- The applicant must be an Indian citizen.
- The applicant must have a minimum monthly salary of ₹15,000, or in the case of self-employed people, an ITR return of ₹6 Lakhs or more per annum.
- Existing HDFC Credit Card holders can also apply for this card.

## Swiggy HDFC Bank Credit Card Review

For the first time, Swiggy has launched a co-branded card, and you can get the Swiggy HDFC Bank credit card for a joining fee of ₹500. It is perfect for loyal Swiggy users as you can get 10% cashback on spends across the Swiggy platform, along with 5% cashback on other popular brands. Also, you get a complimentary 3-month Swiggy One membership worth ₹1199 as a welcome benefit.

![Swiggy HDFC Credit Card](https://cd9941cc.delivery.rocketcdn.me/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/Swiggy-HDFC-Credit-Card.webp)

You can apply for the card either through the Swiggy app or via HDFC Bank. Moreover, the card is on the Mastercard network, and you even get World Tier Mastercard benefits with the card on top brands and multiple locations. If you frequently order food from Swiggy and want to enjoy some additional savings on your dining expenses, consider getting the Swiggy HDFC Bank credit card. This card also offers savings on other well-known brands. Feel free to share your thoughts on this newly released credit card in the comments section.

## FAQs:

[What is the joining and renewal fee on the Swiggy HDFC Bank credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/swiggy-hdfc-bank-credit-card/#chck0)


The joining and renewal fee on the Swiggy HDFC Bank credit card is ₹500 plus taxes. You can apply for the card either through the Swiggy app or through the HDFC Bank website.


[Does the credit card offer Swiggy One membership?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/swiggy-hdfc-bank-credit-card/#chck1)


Yes, upon activation, you can receive a complimentary Swiggy One membership as a welcome benefit. Make sure to activate your card according to the latest RBI guidelines to receive the welcome benefit. If you are already a Swiggy One member, your membership will be extended by three months.


[What is the cashback rate offered by the credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/swiggy-hdfc-bank-credit-card/#chck2)


With the Swiggy HDFC Bank credit card, you can earn 10% cashback on your spends across Swiggy, including food orders, Dineout, Genie, and Instamart. Also, you can earn 5% cashback on online spends at many top brands, and 1% cashback on spends elsewhere. There is a ₹1500 capping per cycle on the 10% and 5% cashback categories, and ₹500 capping on the 1% category per billing cycle.


[How to use the cashback earned through the credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/swiggy-hdfc-bank-credit-card/#chck3)


Cashback earned through this card is directly adjusted in the next credit card statement.

# HDFC Bank Regalia Gold Credit Card

By [Ankur Mittal](https://cardinsider.com/author/ankur/ "Posts by Ankur Mittal")

Updated On
28 July, 2025

_The Regalia credit card range is one of HDFC Bank’s most sought-after offerings. It offers cardholders some of the best-in-class travel and lifestyle privileges._

The Regalia Gold card comes at an annual fee of ₹2,500. It offers a complimentary MMTBLACK Gold and Swiggy One membership on spending over ₹1 Lakh within 90 days of getting the card issued. Individuals get 4 Reward Points on every retail spend of ₹150 and accelerated 5X Reward Points on every ₹150 spent in-store and online at Nykaa, Reliance Digital, Myntra, and Marks & Spencer.

The reward points you earn can be redeemed through Netbanking or Smartbuy on flight/hotel bookings, transfer to Airmiles, products, vouchers on the Exclusive Gold Catalogue, and even cashback. Other than this, the Regalia Gold card also offers premium travel and insurance benefits to the cardholder. Keep reading our HDFC Bank Regalia Gold credit card review to know more about this premium lifestyle card by HDFC Bank.

#### Pros

- Enjoy the exclusive 24/7 concierge service that comes with the Regalia Gold Credit Card.
- You can enjoy access to both domestic and international lounges, with 12 and 6 visits per year, respectively.

#### Cons

- A fee of ₹99 is applicable for reward redemption.
- Card renewal fee waiver is at a high cost of ₹4 lakhs.

## HDFC Bank Regalia Gold Credit Card – Features & Benefits

The new Regalia Gold card by HDFC Bank is loaded with exclusive travel and shopping benefits. It offers a great reward rate on all retail spends and 5X reward points on purchasing from partner brands and merchants. Let’s know more about the features and benefits of this newly launched credit card in detail.

### Welcome Benefits

After successfully paying joining fees, you will receive a gift voucher worth ₹2500. You will also receive a complimentary MMTBLACK Gold and Swiggy One membership upon spending over ₹1 Lakh within 90 days of getting the credit card issued. The welcome benefits must be claimed within 60 days from the benefit end date, and the complimentary memberships are valid for one year.

#### Swiggy One Membership-

- Unlimited free delivery
- Member-only discount
- No surge charges
- Discount on Swiggy Genie

#### MMTBLACK Gold Membership –

- 10% extra discount on selected hotels
- Early check-ins, free room upgrades, discounts on beverages, food, etc., at specific hotels
- Make travel spends on [MakeMyTrip](https://www.makemytrip.com/) and get My Cash. You can use the My Cash for a booking.

### Milestone Benefits

- You get complimentary flight tickets and gift vouchers for achieving annual and quarterly spend-based milestones.
- On spending ₹1.5 Lakhs in a calendar quarter, you get ₹1500 worth of vouchers from M&S, Myntra, Marriott, and Reliance Digital. You can redeem just one voucher from any merchant per quarter.
- If you spend ₹5 Lakhs, you will get ₹5,000 worth of flight vouchers.
- Spending ₹7.5 Lakhs in the card anniversary year earns you additional flight vouchers worth ₹5000.

### Domestic Lounge Access

- Get 12 complimentary domestic lounge access at Indian airports every year for both international and domestic terminals.
- Use your Regalia Gold card to get complimentary lounge access—the airport lounge charges for all visits after the complimentary quota is reached.

### International Lounge Access

- Get six complimentary international lounge access every year through the Priority Pass for both the primary and add-on cardholders.
- After you complete at least four transactions with your Regalia Gold credit card, you can apply for Priority Pass membership. After you finish the complimentary quota, all visits cost USD 27 plus GST.

### Annual Spend-Based Waiver

You can get the card renewal fee waived on spending ₹4 Lakh in the card anniversary year.

### Insurance Benefits

- You get an accidental air death cover up to ₹1 Crore
- You can get an emergency hospitalization cover up to ₹15 Lakhs
- Get credit liability cover worth ₹9 Lakhs

![HDFC Regalia Gold Features](https://cd9941cc.delivery.rocketcdn.me/wp-content/uploads/2023/03/HDFC-Regalia-Gold-Features.webp)

## HDFC Bank Regalia Gold Credit Card Reward Structure

- Get **4 Reward Points** on every **spend of ₹150 on retail**, including utilities, insurance, and education, with a maximum cap of 2000 RPs per month on grocery transactions and 2000 RPs per day on insurance transactions.
- Get **20 Reward Points** on every **spend of ₹150 on Myntra, Marks and Spencers, Nykaa, and Reliance Digital** with a maximum cap of 5000 RPs across all brands.

### Reward Points Redemption

- You can redeem RPs for flight/hotel bookings via the [Smartbuy portal](https://offers.smartbuy.hdfcbank.com/v1/foryou) at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.50
- You can convert RPs into Airmiles through Netbanking at the rate of 1 RP = 0.50 Airmiles
- Redeem RPs for products and gift vouchers on the Smartbuy portal at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.35
- Redeem RPs against the Exclusive Gold Catalogue on Smartbuy at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.65
- You can also redeem the RPs as cashback against your card statement balance at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.20. The maximum cashback redemption cap is 50,000 RPs per month.

## HDFC Bank Regalia Gold Credit Card Eligibility

You should thoroughly review the eligibility requirements of any credit card before applying for it. The following are the age and income requirements for the Regalia Gold credit card.

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Particulars** | **Self-Employed People** | **Salaried** |
| **Age** | 21 to 65 Years | 21 to 60 Years |
| **Income** | Annual Income ₹18 Lakh | Minimum ₹1 Lakh Monthly |

_In addition to these requirements, you must have a good credit score and credit history to be approved for the credit card._

## Who Should Apply for the Credit Card?

This is one of the best HDFC Bank credit cards in the rewards and travel categories. It suits high-spenders who use their credit card for most of their spending. This allows them to make the most of the reward points earned, which can be redeemed against various options like products/vouchers, converted to air miles, and against the Exclusive Gold Catalog.

You should get the card if you plan to spend a lot of money with it and collect reward points. The rewards can be redeemed for premium brands, travel, etc. Also, as a milestone benefit, the card offers travel vouchers instead of bonus points, and you should definitely go for the card if you travel frequently.

## HDFC Bank Regalia Gold Credit Card Review

The HDFC Regalia range of cards is one of the most popular by HDFC Bank, and you get some of the best lifestyle and travel benefits with the Regalia Gold credit card. You get exciting welcome and milestone benefits with this card and reward points on every retail spend.

There are partner merchants on which you can earn accelerated reward points and redeem your points against cashback, flight/hotel bookings, Airmiles, or products from the Exclusive Gold Catalogue. For frequent travelers, the card also offers flight vouchers on reaching spend milestones and complimentary domestic and [international airport lounge](https://cardinsider.com/credit-cards-free-international-airport-lounge-access/) access.

If you are a responsible credit card user, the Regalia Gold credit card is worth getting because of its excellent benefits and reward program. In the comments, let us know your thoughts about the new Regalia Gold credit card and whether you are willing to get it.

## FAQs:

[Do I get a welcome benefit with the Regalia Gold credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/#chck0)


Yes, as a welcome benefit, you will receive a complimentary membership of MMTBLACK Gold and Swiggy One membership. To avail of the offer, you must spend ₹1 Lakh or above within 90 days of the card issuance date.


[What is the reward rate on this credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/#chck1)

- You get 4 Reward Points on every spend of ₹150 on retail, including insurance, utilities, and education.
- You get accelerated 20 reward points on every spend of ₹150 on Mark and Spencers, Myntra, Nykaa, and Reliance Digital.

[Can I get a complimentary flight ticket with this credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/#chck2)


Yes, you can get a complimentary flight ticket voucher to achieve a spend-based milestone. On spending ₹7.5 Lakhs or more in a card anniversary year, you get a complimentary flight ticket worth ₹5000. When you reach the spend milestone, the benefit will be unlocked, and you can generate the voucher code.


[What are the annual fees of the Regalia Gold Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/#chck3)


The HDFC Regalia Gold credit card has a joining and annual fee of ₹2500 plus taxes. However, you can get an annual fee waiver if you spend ₹4 Lakhs or above in the card's anniversary year.


[How can I redeem my Regalia Gold credit card reward points?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/#chck4)

- You can redeem RPs for flight/hotel bookings via the Smartbuy portal at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.50
- You can convert RPs into Airmiles through Netbanking at the rate of 1 RP = 0.50 Airmiles
- Redeem RPs for products and gift vouchers on the Smartbuy portal at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.35
- Redeem RPs against the Exclusive Gold Catalogue on Smartbuy at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.65
- You can also redeem the RPs as cashback against your card statement balance at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.20. The maximum cashback redemption cap is 50,000 RPs per month.

# HDFC Bank INFINIA Metal Credit Card

By [Ankur Mittal](https://cardinsider.com/author/ankur/ "Posts by Ankur Mittal")

Updated On
22 July, 2025

The Infinia Credit Card is the most premium product offered by HDFC Bank. It is an invite-only card with a target audience of high-net individuals who want luxurious benefits and high rewards. The card offers exclusive privileges like 24\*7 concierge services, access to premium golf destinations, and attractive dining benefits at some of the country’s most premium restaurant chains. All these benefits put this card in direct competition with the [Times Black ICICI Bank](https://cardinsider.com/icici-bank/times-black-icici-bank-credit-card-review/) Credit Card. However, none come close to the popularity of Infinia.

Keeping the international traveling needs of its premium customers in consideration, HDFC Infinia offers a lower foreign currency exchange rate of 2% and a complimentary membership to the Priority Pass program with this card. HDFC Infinia Metal is a Reward Points-based card: you earn 5 Reward Points for every ₹150 spent using this card. Although the membership fee for this card is slightly on the higher side, ₹12,500/annum, it is most certainly worth it, and it can be the card of preference for you if you’re someone who is financially stable and can not compromise with your lifestyle needs. To understand the features of this card in more detail, go through the article.Get 10% off upto ₹1,000 while paying your dining bills with HDFC Infinia Metal via Swiggy Dineout. This discount is available twice each month on a minimum bill amount of ₹8,000.

#### Pros

- Unlimited lounge access at domestic and select foreign airports.
- Unlimited complimentary golf games at premier golf courses.

#### Cons

- Infinia Credit Card is a highly rewarding card that benefits everyone. Although it is considered to be the ultimate card, only those who fulfill the strict eligibility criteria can get it. This can be termed the only drawback of this card.

## HDFC Infinia Credit Card Metal Edition Features and Benefits

The Infinia Card issued by the HDFC Bank is the most premium and exclusive card offered by the issuer. This credit card offers some exceptional world-class privileges over different categories, including dining, travel, insurance, and many more. Following is detailed information about the features and benefits of the HDFC Infinia Metal credit card:

### Welcome Benefits

- As a Welcome gift, HDFC gives you a **complimentary Club Marriott membership** for one year. With this card, you can get up to 20% off restaurant bills for a maximum of 10 guests and 20% off the best available rates at participating Marriott hotels in India and on Weekend Rates in the Asia Pacific. This membership is only valid for a period of one year for the primary cardholder.
- Another welcome benefit that you get is **12,500 Bonus Reward Points** after successfully paying the joining fee.

### Travel Benefits

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Benefit** | **Description** |
| **Airport Lounge Access** | With the Priority Pass membership, you can get unlimited complimentary domestic and international lounge access (for primary and add-on Infinia cardholders) worldwide. To avail of the complimentary lounge access, always carry your Priority Pass card with you and show it to the lounge staff. |
| **Club Marriott Membership** | You also get a one-year membership in the Club Marriott loyalty program, which entitles you to a 20% discount on the best available rates at some of the most luxurious hotels across India and on Weekend Rates in the Asia Pacific. |
| **ITC Hotels Complimentary Stay Benefits** | Book a 2-night stay at partner ITC hotels and receive a complimentary one-night stay. Stay for 3 nights and pay for only 2 at participating ITC Hotels with the HDFC Bank Infinia credit card. This offer is only applicable for advance booking. |

### Flight Bookings

The Smartbuy portal of HDFC Bank allows the HDFC Bank Infinia Credit Cardholders to book flights using the Reward points they have earned on their credit card. The value of 1 Reward Point for redemption against the flight booking is ₹1. In addition to this, you can also get exciting discounts and deals.

### Dining Benefits

#### Club Mariott Membership

Enjoy a complimentary Club Mariott membership, which you get as a welcome Benefit for the first year with the INFINIA Credit Card Metal Edition. With this, you are entitled to enjoy the following privileges:

- Get up to 20% off on Food & Beverage bills at top restaurants.
- Get up to 15% off select hotels for a maximum of 10 guests.
- Enjoy 20% off on the Best Available Rates on rooms at participating Marriott hotels in India and on Weekend Rates in Asia Pacific.
- Get 20% off on Spa services at select participating Marriott hotels in India between Monday and Friday.
- Apply for membership with the Club Marriott South Asia App. Enter your membership number and verify your mobile number and email to create your login credentials. Then, unlock your Membership to access a range of benefits at Marriott hotels.

#### ITC Complimentary Buffet

Enjoy exclusive offers on Travel and Dining with ITC Hotels on the HDFC Bank INFINIA Credit Card.

- Get a 1+1 complimentary buffet at participating ITC Hotels with the HDFC Bank Infinia Credit Card (one complimentary buffet per booking and one booking per month). To avail of this offer, Call HDFC INFINIA 24×7 concierge services and book your reservation.

### Swiggy Dineout

Get upto ₹750 off while paying your dining bills with HDFC Infinia Metal via Swiggy Dineout. This discount is available twice each month on a minimum bill amount of ₹8,000. Cardholders shall also be eligible for a 10% discount up to ₹500 on a minimum bill of ₹3,500.

### Golf Privileges

With INFINIA Credit Card, you are entitled to enjoy unlimited Complimentary golf games at leading courses across India and select courses across the world. Each golf lesson will span a minimum of 30 minutes and will include:

- Cost of instructor’s fees
- Range golf balls at 50 golf balls per lesson.

Also, enjoy the benefit of Unlimited complimentary Golf coaching at select golf courses across India. In order to avail of this benefit, you need to book your slot through HDFC INFINIA 24×7 concierge services.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

A 1% fuel surcharge is waived on all fuel purchases between ₹400 and ₹1,00,000 at all fuel stations across the country (maximum waiver is capped at ₹1,000 every month) done with the HDFC Bank Infinia credit card. Do note that no RPs can be earned for any purchases at fuel stations.

### Spend-Based Renewal Fee Benefits

Your card’s renewal fee of ₹12,500 is waived if ₹10,00,000 was spent in the previous year. Those who must pay the annual fees will receive 12,500 Reward Points as a renewal benefit.

### HDFC Infinia Concierge Services

The HDFC Infinia Credit Card also offers 24\*7 concierge services, some of which are as follows:

- Golf booking
- Private dining assistance
- Airport VIP service
- Itinerary planning and reservation assistance
- International gift delivery
- Event planning and referrals

![HDFC Infinia Credit Card Features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## HDFC Infinia Reward Point Earnings

One of the best Rewards Credit Cards in the country, the Infinia metal edition card earns you Reward Points, which can be used to buy shopping vouchers, hotel bookings, and flight tickets and can be adjusted against the card’s statement balance.

- For every ₹150 spent using the Infinia HDFC Card (except fuel spends), you earn 5 Reward Points. These rewards are also earned for insurance, utilities, and education spends.
- You earn accelerated 10X RPs on travel and shopping spending on the SmartBuy platform.
- The accelerated Rewards that you can earn with Infinia are capped at 15,000 points per month.
- Transactions such as fuel, rent, property management, government spending, EasyEMI, and e-wallet reloading will not be eligible for RPs.

## HDFC Infinia Reward Redemption

- The Rewards earned using the Infinia HDFC credit card are redeemable on [HDFC’s SmartBuy platform](https://offers.smartbuy.hdfcbank.com/v1/foryou) or via Netbanking.
- You can redeem the RPs against hotel/flight bookings, DTH recharges, etc., on SmartBuy (1 RP = ₹1).
- Reward Points can also be converted into AirMiles via Netbanking (1 RP = 1 AirMile).
- You can also purchase products and vouchers via Netbanking or on SmartBuy using your RPs (1 RP = ₹0.50).
- Reward Points can also be redeemed against Tanishq and Apple vouchers on SmartBuy (1 RP = ₹1).
- The RPs can also earn you cashback (i.e., they can be redeemed against the card’s statement balance). However, the rate of redemption is lower (1 RP = ₹0.30) in this case. It should be noted that for flight and hotel bookings and Apple and Tanishq vouchers, only 70% of the total payment can be made through the RPs.
- Reward point redemption on Smartbuy against hotel and flight bookings is capped at 1,50,000 RPs per month.
- Reward point redemption on Smartbuy against Tanishq vouchers is capped at 50,000 RPs per quarter. Calendar Quarters – April to June, July to September, October to December, and January to March.
- In August 2024, two new partners, Club ITC and SpiceClub, were added to the list of Reward Point Transfer Partners.
- In March 2025, HDFC Bank added the Maharaja Club as a transfer partner for Infinia. Cardholders can now transfer their reward points to the loyalty program of Air India at a 2:1 ratio.

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Partner** | **Transfer Ratio** |
| Air Canada Aeroplan | 1 Reward Point = 0.5 Aeroplan Miles |
| Club ITC | 1 Reward Point = 0.5 Green Points |
| SpiceClub | 1 Reward Point = 1 SC Point |
| Avianca LifeMiles | 1 Reward Point = 0.5 LifeMile |
| Turkish Airlines Miles & Smiles | 1 Reward Point = 0.5 Turkish Airlines Miles & Smiles |
| United MileagePlus Miles | 1 Reward Point = 0.5 MileagePlus Mile |
| British Airways Avios | 1 Reward Point = 0.5 Avios |
| Finnair Plus | 1 Reward Point = 1 Finnair Plus Point |
| AirAsia Rewards | 1 Reward Point = 1 AirAsia Point |
| Etihad Guest | 1 Reward Point = 0.5 Etihad Guest Points |
| Hainan Airlines Fortune Wings Club | 1 Reward Point = 1 Fortune Wings Club Point |
| Accor ALL | 1 Reward Point = 0.5 ALL Point |
| IHG One | 1 Reward Point = 1 IHG One Point |
| Wyndham Rewards | 1 Reward Point = 1 Wyndham Rewards Point |

## HDFC Infinia SmartBuy

SmartBuy is a rewards portal exclusively made for HDFC bank customers, where they can access exciting offers and deals in different categories. You get accelerated reward points on your Infinia Card when you make purchases via HDFC SmartBuy.

As discussed above, you can also redeem the reward points you earned using the HDFC Infinia Card on SmartBuy. Moreover, HDFC Bank keeps launching different offers on its credit/debit cards via the SmartBuy portal. To be updated on all HDFC SmartBuy offers and deals on Infinia Card, you can [check this page](https://cardinsider.com/blog/hdfc-bank-smartbuy-rewards-program-offers/) regularly.

## HDFC Infinia Metal Card Fees and Charges

The fees associated with the HDFC Bank Infinia Credit Card are as follows:

- **Joining Fee:** HDFC Bank charges ₹12,500 (plus applicable taxes) as the joining fee for the HDFC Bank Infinia Credit Card.
- **Annual Fee:** HDFC Bank charges an annual membership fee of ₹12,500 (plus applicable taxes) for HDFC Bank Infinia Credit Card.
- **Interest Rate:** An interest of 1.99% per month (23.8% annually), which is only applicable on the due amount. This is incredibly low compared to a lot of other credit cards, which have an interest of 3.5% per month.
- **Forex Markup Fee:** The HDFC Infinia Metal Edition Credit Card’s foreign currency markup fee is 2% of the transaction amount, which is very low. This fee applies when you transact in a different currency using your Infinia Card.

## HDFC Infinia Credit Card Eligibility

The Infinia Card is an invite-only credit card, so there are no pre-defined eligibility criteria for this card. However, there are a few basic eligibility requirements that you will need to fulfill if you want to get invited for this card:

- Your age should be above 21 years and below 65 years.
- You should have a high annual income, preferably ₹45 lakh or more.
- You should have a very good credit history and an existing relationship with HDFC Bank. The Total Relationship Value (TRV) should exceed ₹25 lakh.
- Those who wish to obtain the super-premium Infinia Credit Card should already have an HDFC Credit Card. They should have a high credit limit on it or high spends above ₹8 lakh in the past six months.

## Who Should Opt for the Infinia Credit Card?

The HDFC Bank Infinia credit card is an invite-only offering available only to clients who receive an official invitation from HDFC Bank. You might be eligible for the card based on your credit score, credit history, and other factors that the bank considers.

The card does charge a high joining and annual fee, but it is perfect for high-net-worth individuals who desire unlimited lounge access, bonus reward points, high reward rates, and more privileges. If you get an invitation for this card, you should definitely take advantage of it and use it to the maximum.

## Who Can Get Approved For HDFC Infinia Credit Card Metal Edition?

One can be invited for the HDFC Bank Infinia Credit Card if they already have an HDFC Credit Card that they have used responsibly with no payment defaults. Other requirements for this card are an excellent credit score and a high annual income.

## Review of HDFC Infinia Credit Card

HDFC Bank Infinia credit card offers a plethora of benefits to its customers, including lifestyle benefits like a 24×7 concierge service, complimentary access to the country’s leading golf destinations, dining and travel benefits like unlimited complimentary lounge access to all around the world with Priority Pass membership, flight/hotel bookings using Reward Points and, obviously, shopping benefit of 5 Reward Points per ₹150 spent with the card.

Considering all these benefits and privileges, it’s safe to say that this card fits the needs of almost all types of customers. Of course, since it’s a premium credit card, although the bank has not mentioned it explicitly in their terms and conditions, your net worth must be fairly high to be eligible for this card.

_This was our HDFC Infinia Credit Card Review. What do you think of this card? We’d love to hear your opinion, so feel free to leave a comment. If you’re an existing cardholder, do let us know about your experience with the card._

## FAQs:

[What is the validity of the Reward Points?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck0)


The Reward Points expire after 3 years from the date of accumulation. Also, if the card is not used for 365 days, the Points are nullified.


[How to redeem HDFC Infinia Card reward points?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck1)


To redeem the RPs, follow these simple steps:

- Login to your HDFC Net banking account.
- Click on the tab 'Credit Cards.'
- Register your credit card if not already done.
- Click on the option 'Redeem reward points' on the left navigation bar.
- Select your card and click on continue.
- Select the required category/products and redeem your points.

You can also redeem your points on the HDFC SmartBuy portal. The monetary value of RPs varies depending upon where the points are redeemed.


[Do I get the Club Marriott membership every year the card membership is renewed?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck2)


No, the Club Marriott membership is offered as a Welcome Benefit at the time of joining and is valid only for a period of 1 year.


[Do add-on cardholders get the insurance benefits?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck3)


No, only the primary cardholder gets the insurance benefits.


[What is HDFC Infinia credit card customer care number?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck4)


The dedicated customer care numbers for HDFC bank Infinia Credit cardholders are: 1800 266 3310, 1800 102 3310, and 022-61717606 (available 24\*7).


[How to apply for HDFC Infinia Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck5)


The HDFC bank Infinia Credit Card is an invite-only card, i.e., you can get the card only if the bank invites you for the same. Following are some key points that should be kept in mind if you want to get the card:

- You should have a long relationship with the HDFC Bank.
- You should have a high net relationship value with the bank (in the form of loans, life insurance, FDs, mutual funds, etc).
- Regular and responsible use of the existing HDFC credit card.

[What is the joining fee of the HDFC Infinia Card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck6)


The joining fee of the Infinia Credit Card is Rs. 12,500 (plus applicable taxes).


[What is the limit of the Infinia credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck7)


The HDFC Bank Infinia Credit Card comes with a credit limit of a minimum of Rs. 8 to 10 lakhs. However, the card has the NPSL (No pre-set credit limit) feature that allows you to get your credit limit increased by informing the Infinia HDFC team.


[What is HDFC Infinia Credit Card reward points value in rupees?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck8)


For redemption against flight bookings and hotel reservations on SmartBuy, 1 RP = Re. 1. Conversion rate of RPs to AitMiles is 1 RP = 1 AirMile. Redemption of RPs for purchasing shopping vouchers on SmartBuy or via Netbanking, 1 RP = Rs. 0.50, and if you want to use the RPs to settle your card’s statement balance, 1 RP = Rs. 0.30.


[Is HDFC Infinia card lifetime free?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-infinia-credit-card/#chck9)


No, the HDFC Infinia Metal Card is not free. It charges an annual fee of Rs. 12,500.

# HDFC Bank MoneyBack Plus Credit Card

By [Bharti Bisht](https://cardinsider.com/author/bharti/ "Posts by Bharti Bisht")

Updated On
3 April, 2025

The HDFC Bank MoneyBack Plus Credit Card is regarded as a superior version of the HDFC MoneyBack Credit Card that was issued earlier by the HDFC Bank. Although both the MoneyBack cards come with a similar joining & annual fee (₹500), the MoneyBack+ card provides a higher reward rate. With the MoneyBack Plus Card, for every spend of ₹150 on partner brands, you can earn up to 10X (20) CashPoints. These CashPoints are redeemable against the card’s outstanding balance, product catalog, Airmiles, and several more options. Not only cashback, but you can also get gift vouchers that are worth up to ₹2,000 on reaching the stipulated spending milestones.

Talking about the dining benefits that the card offers, you can enjoy a discount of ₹200 on dining across over 2,000 premium restaurants in the country with Swiggy Dineout. The HDFC MoneyBack Plus card allows its customers to save in a lot more ways, such as fuel surcharge waiver, spend-based annual fee waiver, etc. To understand the card’s features in detail, go through the complete HDFC MoneyBack Plus Credit Card Review given below:

## HDFC Bank MoneyBack Plus Credit Card

[Apply Now](https://cardinsider.com/go/hdfcbank)

- 500 CashPoints as a welcome gift.
- 2 CashPoints for every ₹50 you spend with the card (except fuel, wallet reloads, prepaid card reloads, and voucher purchases).
- 10X CashPoints on partner brands, including Amazon, Flipkart, Reliance Smart Store, BigBasket, and Swiggy.
- Gift voucher worth ₹500 on spends of ₹50,000 in a calendar quarter (max benefit of ₹2,000 in a year).
- Up to₹200 off on dining at over 2,000 premium restaurants in select cities.
- Get a waiver on the annual fee for an annual expenditure of a minimum of ₹50,000.
- A 1% waiver on fuel surcharge.

## HDFC Bank MoneyBack Plus Credit Card Features and Benefits

HDFC MoneyBack+ Card, just like the original MoneyBack Card, is among the most popular [cashback credit cards](https://cardinsider.com/best-cashback-credit-cards-india/) available in the Indian market. You earn cashback as CashPoints on all categories of spends with the card. Apart from earning CashPoints, the card also offers a few other benefits like gift vouchers (on achieving the stipulated spending milestones), dining discounts, fuel surcharge waivers, etc.

### Welcome Benefits

- You get 500 CashPoints when you join the card as a welcome benefit.
- Each year, on card renewal, cardholders shall get 500 cash points.

### Milestone Benefits

Earn a gift voucher worth ₹500 on expenditure of ₹50,000 or more in a quarter. This means that you can earn gift vouchers worth ₹2,000 in a year.

### Dining Benefits

Paying through your MoneyBack Plus Credit Card can earn you a discount of ₹200 (Minimum Order Value ₹2,000) on dining at more than 2,000 premium restaurants. This discount is only available if the dining bill payment is made via [Swiggy](https://www.swiggy.com/) Dineout.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

When you pay using your HDFC MoneyBack+ Credit Card, you can get a 1% waiver on the fuel surcharge for fuel purchases. The maximum waiver that can be claimed is capped at ₹250 per billing cycle.

### Spend-Based Renewal Fee Waiver

On spending ₹50,000 or more in the last year, get a waiver on the renewal fee of ₹500 for HDFC MoneyBack+ Credit Card.

### UPI Enabled

HDFC MoneyBack Plus Credit Card is available on multiple card networks, including Visa and RuPay. Those who opt for the RuPay variant can link their cards to UPI and make payments through their UPI applications. Earn 2 Reward Points/₹150 on UPI spends, capped at 500 Reward Points each month.

![HDFC Bank MoneyBack Plus Credit Card Benefits](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## HDFC Bank MoneyBack+ Credit Card Reward Points/Cashback

HDFC MoneyBack+ Credit card offers cashback in the form of CashPoints for every transaction done with the card. The following are the cashback on different transactions:

### 10X CashPoints/₹150 Spent on Popular Brands

Get 10x Cash Points (20 CPs/₹150) on your spending on Amazon, Flipkart, Big Basket, Reliance Smart Superstore, and Swiggy. The bonus cash points under this category are capped at 2,500 points per month.

### 2 CashPoints/₹150 Spent on Other Spends

Get 2 CashPoints/₹150 spent. SmartPay transactions will also be considered under the 2 CashPoints category.

CashPoints will not be provided for spending or transactions such as fuel spending, wallet loads, gift or prepaid card loads, rent, government transactions, Smart EMI, voucher purchases, cash advances, payment of outstanding amounts, and payment of card fees.

### Reward Points/Cashback Redemption

- 1 Cash Point = ₹0.25 for redemption against statement cash. The points are redeemable against cash credit by logging into your net banking account, and a minimum of 500 Cash Points are required.
- 1 Cash Point = ₹0.25 for redemption against flight/hotel bookings on [Smartbuy](https://offers.smartbuy.hdfcbank.com/v1/foryou). The cash points can only be used for up to 50% of the booking, and the rest must be paid through the cardholder’s account.
- 1 Cash Point = ₹0.25 when redeemed against the product catalog.
- 1 Cash Point = 0.25 AirMile for redemption against Airmiles.

## Eligibility Criteria for HDFC MoneyBack Plus Credit Card

The following table illustrates the income-based and age-based eligibility criteria for the HDFC MoneyBack+ Credit card-

- Salary employees should be between 21 and 60 and earn at least ₹20,000 per month.
- The self-employed individuals should be between 21 and 65 years old and have a minimum annual income of ₹6 lakhs.
- The applicant should have a decent credit score.

![HDFC MoneyBack Plus Credit Card Review](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20333'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## Money Back Plus Credit Card Limit

The credit limit assigned to the HDFC MoneyBack Plus Credit Cardholders has yet to be disclosed by the HDFC Bank as there is no fixed limit for everyone, and it varies for different cardholders. The factors that affect your credit card limit include your credit score, past payment history, income, etc. If you are a high-income individual or you have an excellent credit score, you will get a higher credit limit than those with a low score. If you have a lower credit limit, you should build a good score and apply for a higher credit limit. You can do this by contacting your bank’s customer care, filling out the online application form for card up-gradation, or visiting the branch.

## HDFC MoneyBack Plus Credit Card Bill Payment

To make the bill payment of your HDFC Bank MoneyBack Plus Credit Card, you can follow the below-mentioned procedures:

- Visit the [HDFC Bank’s Bill Desk](https://www.billdesk.com/pgidsk/pgmerc/hdfccard/index.jsp) page, enter the required credit card information, and proceed further to pay the bill.
- Log in to your HDFC Bank internet banking account and pay your credit card bill under the section ‘Credit Card Payment.’
- If you have a savings account in a bank other than HDFC Bank, you can settle your card bill via NEFT.
- You can also use digital payment services apps like Paytm, CRED, PhonePe, and CheQ to pay your credit card bills.

## HDFC MoneyBack+ Credit Card Customer Care

- If you need emergency assistance related to your HDFC Bank MoneyBack Plus Credit Card, you can call the 24\*7 helpline number, 1800 266 4332.
- To send your queries via email, contact HDFC Bank credit card customer care at customerservices.cards@hdfcbank.com.

## HDFC MoneyBack+ Credit Card Review

With its great reward offers and redemption options, the HDFC MoneyBack+ Credit Card can be viewed as a great card for regular spending. Being one of the basic yet popular HDFC Credit Cards, it provides you with an amazing reward rate (up to 3.33%), which will help you save a lot. Moreover, you can earn exciting gift vouchers worth ₹500 every quarter on the basis of your spending; the annual fee for the card can also be waived. All these basic but amazing features of this card make it worth trying. If you need a card that would help you manage your everyday expenses, this card would be a significant choice. After going through this HDFC MoneyBack Plus review given above, let us know your views on the card below in the comment area.

## FAQs:

[Is HDFC MoneyBack Plus credit card lifetime free?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-moneyback-plus-credit-card/#chck0)


No, the HDFC MoneyBack Plus Card is not lifetime free. It comes with an annual fee of ₹500, which is waived on spending ₹50,000 or more in the previous year.


[What is the maximum limit of an HDFC MoneyBack Plus Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-moneyback-plus-credit-card/#chck1)


As the HDFC MoneyBack Plus Credit Card is a basic card, cardholders don't get a very high credit limit. The maximum limit of this card can go up to around ₹1.5 lakhs.


[What is the difference between MoneyBack and MoneyBack Plus Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-moneyback-plus-credit-card/#chck2)


The MoneyBack Plus Card is an updated version of the MoneyBack Credit Card, which the bank no longer issues.


[What is HDFC Moneyback Plus Credit Card Reward Points value in rupees?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-moneyback-plus-credit-card/#chck3)


For redemption against statement balance, flight/hotel bookings/product catalog, the value of 1 Cash Point = ₹0.25 for the HDFC Bank MoneyBack Plus Credit Card.


[Is there any maximum cap on the fuel surcharge waiver?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-moneyback-plus-credit-card/#chck4)


Yes, a maximum waiver of ₹250 can be claimed with this card every month.


[Do I get any renewal benefits with the HDFC Bank MoneyBack+ Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-moneyback-plus-credit-card/#chck5)


Yes, every time you renew your Moneyback Plus card, you will get 500 bonus cash points.


[What are the ATM withdrawal charges for this credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-moneyback-plus-credit-card/#chck6)


A cash advance fee of 2.5% (minimum ₹500) would be applicable towards any cash withdrawn with this card.

# Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card

By [Gunjan Bharara](https://cardinsider.com/author/gunjan/ "Posts by Gunjan Bharara")

Updated On
31 August, 2025

The Axis Atlas Credit Card is a premium travel category card offering exclusive EDGE Miles to users who frequently travel. The Atlas Credit Card is not a co-branded card like other travel cards offered by Axis Bank; it is a generic card. With a joining fee of ₹5,000, this card offers exclusive travel, lounge, and dining benefits. For every purchase made with the card, you earn EDGE Miles, redeemable for Air Miles of partner airlines’ flyer programs like KrisFlyer or Air India Maharaja Club, or for booking flights/hotels via the bank’s Travel Edge portal. Atlas Credit Card offers accelerated 5 EDGE Reward Points/₹100 on travel spends and 2 EDGE Miles/₹100 for spends on all other eligible purchases.

The Atlas Card offers travel benefits, including complimentary access to international and domestic airport lounges, and higher reward rates on foreign currency transactions. For detailed information about Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card, refer to the details given below:

## Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card

![Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

(4.8/5)


#### Joining Fee


₹5,000 + GST


#### Renewal Fee


₹5,000 + GST


#### Best Suited For

Travel \|

#### Reward Type

AirMiles
\|

#### Welcome Benefits

2,500 Edge Miles on Completing 1 Transaction Within 37 Days of Card Issuance

[Rewards and Benefits](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-atlas-credit-card/#)

#### Movie & Dining

Get Up to 25% off (Max ₹800) Twice a Month at Partner Restaurants via EazyDiner on a Minimum Spend of ₹2,500


#### Rewards Rate

5 Edge Miles for every Rs. 100 spent on travel spends, 2 Edge Miles for every Rs. 100 spent on all other transactions excluding spends on Hotels, Airlines, and TRAVEL EDGE Portal


#### Reward Redemption


EDGE Miles Can be Transferred to Partner Airlines and Hotel Loyalty Programs. 1 EDGE Mile = 2 Partner Miles (Except Marriott)


#### Travel


Accelerated EDGE Mile Earning


#### Domestic Lounge Access


8 With Silver Tier, 12 With Gold Tier, and 18 With Platinum Tier (Each Year)


#### International Lounge Access


4 per annum under Silver Tier, 6 per annum under Gold Tier and 12 per annum under Platinum Tier


#### Golf

N/A


#### Insurance Benefits


4 With Silver Tier, 6 With Gold Tier, and 12 With Platinum Tier (Each Year)


[Fees & Charges](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-atlas-credit-card/#)

#### Spend-Based Waiver


N/A


#### Rewards Redemption Fee

₹99 + GST for Redemption of EDGE Miles and ₹199 + GST for Conversion of EDGE Miles to Partner Points


#### Foreign Currency Markup


3.5% of the transaction amount

#### Interest Rates

3.75% Per Month (55.55% Annually)


#### Fuel Surcharge

1% Waiver for Fuel Transactions Between ₹400 and ₹4,000 (Capped at ₹400 Per Statement Cycle)


#### Cash Advance Charges


2.5% of the withdrawal amount (subject to min charge of Rs. 500)

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-atlas-credit-card/#)

- Reward Points are earned as Axis Bank EDGE Miles.
- 2,500 EDGE Miles as a joining benefit.
- 5 EDGE Miles/₹100 on all travel spends with the card.
- Cards are available in three tiers of membership: silver, gold, and platinum.
- Bonus Edge Miles on reaching specified spending milestones with the card.
- 25% off up to ₹800 via EazyDiner at partner restaurants.
- Complimentary access to domestic and international airport lounges.

[Pros/Cons](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-atlas-credit-card/#)

#### Pros

- You will receive 2,500 EDGE Miles as a welcome bonus, which can be redeemed for travel and other benefits later.

#### Cons

- The Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card doesn’t include any golf benefits.
- Unfortunately, this credit card does not offer any insurance benefits.

## Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card Rewards

- **5 EDGE Miles/₹100** on transactions made on **Axis Bank Travel Edge, hotels, and flight bookings**. These accelerated EDGE Miles can only be earned on cumulative spends of up to ₹2,00,000 per month.
- **2 EDGE Miles/₹100** on **all other transactions** made with the Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card including spends made on travel after reaching the cumulative spend threshold of ₹2 lakh per month.
- No EDGE Miles shall be awarded for transactions made on Gold/Jewellery, Rent, Wallet, Government Institution, Insurance, Fuel, and Utilities.

## Atlas Credit Card Reward Redemption/Conversion

The EDGE Miles earned on the Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card can be redeemed in the following two ways-

- Booking flight tickets or making hotel reservations through the Axis Bank Travel Edge Portal. Please note that the maximum number of EDGE MILES that can be transferred to Partner Points within a calendar year is 150,000 EDGE MILES per customer ID.
- Transfer the Edge Miles on the card to partner airlines and hotel frequent flyer programs. The following table illustrates the value of Edge Miles for redemption against the Miles of different partner airlines’ frequent flyer programs-

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Group A (Maximum 30,000 EDGE Miles)** | **Group B (Maximum 1,20,000 EDGE Miles)** |
| Accor Hotels | Air France – KLM (Flying Blue) |
| Air Canada | Air India (Maharaja Club) |
| Ethiopian Airlines | Air Asia |
| Etihad | ITC |
| Japan Airlines | IHG Hotels & Resorts |
| Marriott International | Qantas Airways |
| Qatar Airways | SpiceJet |
| Singapore Airlines |  |
| Turkish Airlines |  |
| Thai Airways |  |
| United Airlines |  |
| Wyndham Hotels |  |

## Welcome Benefit

You get 2,500 EDGE Miles as a welcome gift on successfully completing 1 transaction within 37 days of the card set-up date.

## Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card Tiers

Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card membership comes in three tiers- Silver Tier, Gold Tier, and Platinum Tier. A particular customer’s membership tier depends upon their annual spending with the card. If a cardholder is spending ₹7.5 lacs or less in an annual year, he will be falling under Silver Tier Membership, but if he is spending between ₹7.5 lacs to ₹15 lacs, he will be under Gold Tier Membership, and if his annual spends of the year exceeds ₹15 lacs, he will be considered under Platinum Tier Membership. The same is depicted in the table below-

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Annual Spends** | **Membership Tier** |
| Less Than ₹7.5 Lakh | Silver |
| Between ₹7.5 Lakh and ₹15 Lakh | Gold |
| More Than ₹15 Lakh | Platinum |

The membership tiers are different in terms of the number of Edge Miles that you get as a renewal benefit and the number of domestic/international airport lounge access you get annually.

Your membership Tier will be downgraded from next year onwards if you are not able to maintain the minimum spend with your Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card in the current year according to the tier in which you fall. You will remain in the same tier, i.e., Silver Tier if you spend ₹7.5 lacs or less in the current year. Now, if you were in the Gold Tier but in the current year, your annual expenditures are less than ₹7.5 lacs, you will be downgraded to Silver Tier Membership. Adding to this, if based on your annual spending, you were a part of Platinum Tier Membership, but in the current year, your annual spending falls below ₹15 lacs, You will be downgraded from Platinum Tier Membership to Silver or Gold Tier Membership. This can be explained in a tabular form in the following way-

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Existing Tier** | **Tier After Downgradation** |
| Silver | N/A |
| Gold | Silver |
| Platinum | Silver (for Spends Less Than ₹7.5 Lakh)<br>Gold (for Spend Between ₹7.5 Lakh & ₹15 Lakh ) |

## Annual Benefit

You get Bonus Edge Miles on your Atlas Credit Card annually once the renewal fee is paid. The number of Bonus Miles received as the annual benefit is different for each of the tiers of the card membership.

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Membership Tier** | **Edge Miles Credited Annually** |
| Silver | N/A |
| Gold | 2,500 |
| Platinum | 5,000 |

## Milestone Benefits

A particular spend amount is pre-decided by the bank for each tier. Upon achieving that milestone spend the amount of your respective tier, and you will be credited with Bonus Edge Miles of your tier. You get Bonus Edge Miles on achieving the spend targets with your Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card.

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Spend Milestone** | **Edge Miles** |
| ₹3 Lakh | 2,500 |
| ₹7.5 Lakh | Additional 2,500 |
| ₹15 Lakh | Additional 5,000 |

_Transactions made for Gold/Jewellery, Rent, Wallet, Government Institutions, Insurance, Fuel, and Utilities merchants will not be counted towards Milestone achievement and Tier evaluation thresholds._

![Axis Atlas Credit Card Features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## Free Airport Lounge Access With Axis Atlas Credit Card

The lounge access benefit is different for card members in different tiers of membership. The following table illustrates the complimentary domestic as well as international airport lounge visit privilege that you get on your Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card-

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Membership Tier** | **Domestic Airport Lounge Visits** | **International Airport Lounge Visits** |
| Silver | 8 | 4 |
| Gold | 12 | 6 |
| Platinum | 18 | 12 |

## Dining Benefits

You can avail of discounts of up to ₹800 at partner restaurants via [EazyDiner](https://www.eazydiner.com/). Offer valid twice per month on a minimum order value of ₹2,500.

## Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

The table given below summarizes the eligibility criteria for the Axis Bank Atlas credit card:

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Age** | 18 Years – 70 Years |
| **Income** | Salaried – ₹12 Lakhs and above<br>Self-Employed – ₹15 Lakhs and Above |
| **Credit Score** | Decent credit score (750 or above) |

## How To Apply For The Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card?

If you want to apply for the Atlas credit card offline, then you can do the same by visiting the Axis Bank branch in your city and filling out the application form. Or you can apply for the card online by following the steps below:

- Click on the Apply Now button.
- Fill in your particulars.
- Submit your application.

## Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card Review

![Axis Bank Atlas Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

The Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card is an excellent choice for individuals who spend a significant amount on domestic and international travel each year. You get a welcome benefit of 2,500 Edge Miles and bonus miles on spending the milestone amount based on your tier. This card is considered the best for travel, as it offers a great transfer ratio of 1 EDGE Mile to 2 Partner Points for several airline and hotel partners.

The card even offers complimentary lounge access at domestic and international airport lounges, dining benefits via EazyDiner.

## FAQs:

[What are the tiers of Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-atlas-credit-card/#chck0)


Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card is offered in three tiers- Silver Tier, Gold Tier, and Platinum Tier. Initially, the cardholder is in the Silver Tier and can be upgraded to a higher tier on achieving certain spend milestones with the card (more details on this above).


[How many domestic and international airport lounge access do I get with Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-atlas-credit-card/#chck1)


Silver Tier members get 8 domestic and 4 international airport lounge visits, Gold Tier members get 12 domestic and 6 international airport lounge visits and Platinum members get 18 domestic and 12 international airport lounge visits.


[Can Edge Miles be redeemed for cashback?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-atlas-credit-card/#chck2)


No, Edge Miles can either be transferred to a partner airline's frequent flyer program or can be redeemed for booking flights/hotel rooms via Axis Bank's Travel Edge Portal.


[What is the reward rate of Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-atlas-credit-card/#chck3)


You earn 5 Edge Miles per ₹100 on flight/hotel bookings via the Travel Edge portal and 2 Edge Miles for every ₹100 spent on all other transactions with Axis Bank Atlas Credit Card.

# Axis Bank SELECT Credit Card

By [Bharti Bisht](https://cardinsider.com/author/bharti/ "Posts by Bharti Bisht")

Updated On
9 July, 2025

The Axis Bank SELECT Credit Card has a joining fee of ₹3,000 and offers benefits across travel, shopping, dining, movies, etc. Those with the Axis Burgundy account shall get this card as lifetime free but they shall not be eligible for the welcome benefit of 2,000 EDGE Reward Points. It covers almost all categories where people spend, making it a sought-after lifestyle credit card. In addition to free domestic airport lounge access, this card includes a Priority Pass membership that provides complimentary access to international airport lounges.

This card offers discounts at various popular restaurants through the Axis Dining Delights program and monthly discounts on Swiggy orders as well. The SELECT credit card offers accelerated 2X (20 Points) EDGE Reward Points on retail shopping with the card. Also, users can get bonus Reward Points on reaching spending milestones and get a renewal fee waiver on spending ₹8 lakh in a year. Keep reading to know if this card is the perfect fit for your wallet or not.

## Axis Bank SELECT Credit Card

[Apply Now](https://cardinsider.com/go/axisbank)

BOGO on Movie Tickets Via District by Zomato App (Up to ₹250, Twice Each Month). ₹200 off on Swiggy Orders (Twice Each Month) and Up to ₹800 Off With EazyDiner on Dining Bills

- Earn 20 EDGE Points/₹200 on spends up to ₹20,000 cumulatively per month on the card and then earn 10 EDGE Points/₹200 spent after that.
- Flat ₹500 on a minimum purchase of ₹2,500 on the BigBasket mobile app and website.
- Get a flat discount of ₹200 on your Swiggy orders when you make a minimum transaction of ₹1,000.
- 12 complimentary accesses to international airport lounges annually with a Priority Pass membership.

- Bonus EDGE Points on spending ₹3 lakh in an anniversary year.

#### Pros

- Complimentary priority pass membership is also included with the Axis Bank Select Card.

#### Cons

## Axis Select Credit Card Features and Benefits

The Axis Bank Select Credit Card is one of the most popular sub-premium credit cards offered by Axis Bank. It comes with a lot of exciting privileges in different categories, including travel, entertainment, dining, golf, insurance, and many more. The features and benefits of the Axis Bank Select Credit Card have been discussed in detail below:

### Welcome Benefits

You get 10,000 EDGE Reward Points as a welcome benefit on your first transaction within 30 days of issuance. These 10,000 Points carry a value of ₹2,000 and shall be only awarded upon the realization of the joining fee (not valid for lifetime free cards).

### Milestone Benefits

- 5,000 bonus EDGE Points on a minimum annual expenditure of ₹3 lakh.
- Complete annual fee waiver on spending ₹8 lakh in an anniversary year.
- Auto-renewal of the complimentary Priority Pass membership on a minimum annual expenditure of ₹3 lakh.

### Travel Benefits

- With the Axis Bank Select Credit Card, you get 12 complimentary international airport lounge access every year with [Priority Pass](https://cardinsider.com/blog/priority-pass-program-guide/) membership. Renewal of Priority Pass membership is subject to achieving the annual expenditure milestone of ₹3 lakh.
- 2 complimentary domestic airport lounge access every quarter. You would need to spend ₹50,000 in the previous three months to be eligible for free domestic lounge access.

### ₹500 Off With Big Basket

With the Axis Bank Select Credit Card, you can get a flat discount of ₹500 on a minimum purchase of ₹2,500 on the [BigBasket](https://www.bigbasket.com/) mobile app and website. This offer can be applied once each month per user and can help you save up to ₹6,000 in a year on your grocery shopping. However, it is essential to note that you need to make the payment with the Select Card only to avail of this offer.

**Use code “AXISSELECTCC” to avail of this offer.**

### Movie & Dining Benefits

- Up to 25% discount up to ₹800 on dining transactions made at partner restaurants with Axis Bank Dining Delights when paying via Select Credit Card.
- Flat ₹200 on Swiggy. Offer valid twice a month on a minimum transaction amount of ₹1,000 per order. Coupon code “AXIS200” has to be used to avail of the discount.
- Enjoy a 100% discount on your second movie ticket when booked through the District app using your Select credit card. This offer is valid for up to ₹250 off twice a month on movie ticket bookings. To qualify, a minimum of two movie tickets must be purchased via the District app.

### Golf Benefits

- Get 6 complimentary golf rounds each year.
- Get 6 additional rounds if you spend ₹3 Lakh or more in a year.

Those looking to book their golf games/lessons can do so by clicking [here](https://www.extraordinaryweekends.com/).

### Insurance Benefits

The following are the insurance benefits that you get with the Axis Bank Select Credit Card:

- Purchase Protection cover worth up to ₹1 lakh.
- Cumulative insurance covers up to USD 500 for loss of travel documents and delay/loss of check-in baggage.

## Axis SELECT Credit Card EDGE Rewards Earning and Redemption

- Earn **20 EDGE Points per ₹200 on spends up to ₹20,000** cumulatively per month on the card and earn **10 EDGE points per ₹200 spent after that.**
- No EDGE reward points are earned on fuel, insurance, gold, government services, education, utilities, and wallet spends made with the card.

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Monthly Spends** | **Rewards** | **Reward Rate** |
| ₹20,000 | 20 EDGE Points/₹200 | 2% |
| Above ₹20,000 | 10 EDGE Points/₹200 | 1% |

### Redemption

EDGE Points earned on the card can be redeemed against shopping/travel vouchers or for purchasing products (from the given catalog) from the [EDGE Rewards](https://edgerewards.axisbank.co.in/lms/rewardscatalogue) portal. You can even transfer the EDGE reward points earned by you to partner airlines/hotel points at a ratio of 5:1 for ITC, SpiceJet, and AirAsia; for all other partners, a 10:1 ratio is applicable.

The total number of reward points you can convert in a calendar year is capped at 5,00,000 RPs for a single Customer ID. You can just link one Customer ID to a partner program loyalty membership at a given time. If you want to link another ID, you will first have to delete the existing primary ID and add the new one. Following is a detailed list of airline and hotel loyalty partners with Axis Bank, classified into two groups:

## Eligibility Criteria for Axis SELECT Card

The following are the eligibility criteria that one needs to fulfill to get approved for the SELECT card by Axis Bank:

- The primary cardholder must be between 18 and & 70 years of age.
- Should be a resident of India.
- The net annual income of the applicant must be ₹6 lakhs or more.

## Axis Bank Select Credit Card Fees & Charges

The following table summarizes the fee structure for the Axis Bank Select Credit Card:

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Fee** | **Charges** |
| **Joining/Annual fee** | ₹3,000 + GST (~₹3,540) |
| **Interest Charges** | 3.75% Monthly (55.55% Annually) |
| **Foreign Markup Fee** | 3.5% |
| **Cash Advance Fee** | 2.5% (Minimum ₹500) |
| **Cash Payment Fee** | ₹175 |
| **Skill-Based Online Gaming Fee** | 1% If Transactions Go Over ₹10,000 in a Statement Cycle |

## Axis Bank Select Credit Card Limit

The bank has not clearly disclosed the credit limit provided by the Axis Bank Select Credit Card, but generally, it is higher than ₹2 lakhs. However, the credit limit may vary for different cardholders as it depends on their credit history, past payment behavior, income, and a few more factors. If you have a lower credit limit with your Select Credit Card, you can always request a higher credit limit by contacting the Axis Bank credit card customer care or by visiting your nearest branch. Before increasing your credit limit, make sure that you have a good credit score and no pending payments so that your card issuer can trust you and provide you with a higher credit limit.

## Select Credit Card Activation & PIN Generation

To activate your Axis Bank Select Credit Card or for its PIN generation, you may choose and follow any one of the below-mentioned methods:

- You can generate the PIN of your credit card via Internet banking. Log in to your net banking account, and under the option My Credit Card, go to ‘More Services’ and then ‘Credit card PIN.’ Proceed further by entering the PIN and then confirming.
- You can also generate your credit card PIN by contacting customer care for your credit card. Ask them to help you activate your credit card. They’ll ask you for a few details, and you will provide them with all the required information. Then, an OTP will be sent to your registered mobile number for the verification process. Provide them with the OTP and generate the PIN.
- You can also do the same via the Axis Bank mobile banking app. Register yourself on the app and look for an option for credit card PIN generation.
- Generating your credit card PIN via an ATM is another easy method to activate your credit card. Visit your nearest ATM and find an option to generate a credit card PIN from the main menu.

## Axis Bank SELECT Credit Card Review

Axis Bank SELECT Credit Card is a versatile credit card with benefits for almost all spends categories. From discounts on dining to complimentary access to domestic and international airport lounges and comprehensive insurance benefits, the card comes packed with all kinds of benefits and privileges that one expects from a lifestyle credit card. [HDFC Regalia Gold Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/) is one of its closest competitors.

The Axis Select Credit Card is designed for individuals who can maintain a monthly spending of ₹20,000. With this spending level, cardholders can earn 20 EDGE Reward Points for every ₹200 spent. People with such spending habits might also want to consider the [American Express Membership Rewards](https://cardinsider.com/american-express/american-express-membership-rewards-credit-card/) Credit Card. Individuals with high spending levels would benefit more from opting for a premium and rewarding credit card. Although this card may not seem impressive at first glance, it is ideal for those seeking a modest rewards rate alongside excellent lifestyle benefits, such as access to domestic and international airport lounges.

## FAQs:

[How many total complimentary golf rounds do I get on spending over Rs. 3 lakhs in a year?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-select-credit-card/#chck0)


You get a total of 12 complimentary golf rounds if you spend Rs. 3 lakh in a year with your Axis Bank Select Credit Card.


[How can I get the renewal fee waived for my Axis Bank SELECT Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-select-credit-card/#chck1)


The annual fee for the SELECT card can be waived if you spend Rs. 8 Lakhs in an anniversary year.


[Do I earn any EDGE Points on fuel purchases through this card?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-select-credit-card/#chck2)


No, you don’t earn any EDGE Points for fuel purchases using your Axis Bank SELECT Credit Card.


[How can I block my Axis Bank Select credit card in the case of theft or loss?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-select-credit-card/#chck3)


In case of theft or loss, you can easily get your card blocked by contacting Axis Bank Credit Card customer care. Make sure to report the loss to the bank as soon as possible as you will get zero liability protection by reporting the loss in a timely manner.


[How to convert my Axis Bank Select credit card purchases into EMIs?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-select-credit-card/#chck4)


To convert your credit card purchases into EMIs, you can contact customer care or you can also do it easily via internet banking.


[Will I need to submit a separate request to get my credit card's e-statement?](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-select-credit-card/#chck5)


No, you don't need to request the e-statement, but it will be sent to your registered email Id every month.

# Axis Bank Magnus Credit Card

By [Rajat Gaur](https://cardinsider.com/author/rajat/ "Posts by Rajat Gaur")

Updated On
12 September, 2025

The Magnus Credit Card is one of the most rewarding and sought-after cards in Axis Bank’s portfolio. This super-premium card issued by the bank targets high-income individuals who want to enjoy the privilege of a luxurious lifestyle. It has a joining fee of ₹12,500 but offers exciting benefits across various categories, including travel, rewards, and many more.

Cardholders get domestic and international lounge access at airports and premium customers can also make international transactions at a relatively low 2% Forex Markup fee. Also, there are no cash withdrawal charges on the card (Interest charges applicable). Although the annual fee of the card is relatively high, you can get it waived by spending 25 lakhs or above in a card anniversary year. Keep reading our review of the super-premium Magnus credit card to learn about its features and benefits.

## Axis Bank Magnus Credit Card

[Apply Now](https://cardinsider.com/go/axisbank)

![Axis Bank Magnus Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

(4.6/5)

15% Discount at 4,000+ Restaurants Across India Under the Axis Dining Delights Program

12 Axis EDGE REWARDS Points for every Rs. 200 spent with the card, 5X EDGE REWARDS on travel spends via TRAVEL EDGE.


#### Reward Redemption
