[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/sbi-card/sbi-simplyclick-credit-card/#)

- Gift vouchers worth ₹500 from Amazon as a welcome benefit.
- E-vouchers from ClearTrip/Yatra worth ₹2,000 on net annual spends of ₹1,00,000 and ₹2,00,000 each.
- 10X Reward Points on online spending at partner merchants, including popular websites like Apollo 24×7, BookMyShow, Cleartrip, Dominos, IGP, Myntra, Netmeds, and Yatra.
- 5X Reward Points on all other online spends using your SBI SimplyClick Credit Card.
- 1 Reward Point per ₹100 on all offline spends except fuel.
- Annual fee waiver of ₹499 on yearly spending of ₹1,00,000.
- 1% fuel surcharge waived for all purchases of fuel between ₹500 and ₹3,000.

[Pros/Cons](https://cardinsider.com/sbi-card/sbi-simplyclick-credit-card/#)

#### Pros

- One of the most beneficial pros of the SBI SimplyClick Credit Card is the welcome benefit of a voucher worth Rs. 500 from Amazon.
- You can also get coupons worth Rs. 4000 on achieving milestone spends.

#### Cons

- This card has a significant drawback for frequent travellers as it offers no domestic or international lounge access.
- Along with no travel benefits, there aren’t any insurance benefits with the SBI SimplyClick Credit Card.

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Features & Benefits

The SBI Simply Click Credit Card is among the most basic yet most desirable credit cards in the market. It comes with several exciting benefits, including a welcome bonus, reward programs, milestone benefits, etc. The following are the benefits and features of this credit card:

### Welcome Benefits

- You get an Amazon gift voucher worth ₹500 in the form of a welcome gift on the realization of the card membership fee.
- The voucher code will be sent to you on your registered mobile number within 30 days of the payment of the joining fee.
- The gift voucher will be valid for 12 months from the accrual date. Complete details regarding the welcome voucher can be found [here](https://www.sbicard.com/sbi-card-en/assets/docs/pdf/personal/credit-cards/value-savings/simplyclick/simplyclick-welcome.pdf).

### ClearTrip/Yatra Vouchers As Milestone Benefits

- You get an e-voucher valued at ₹2,000 from ClearTrip/Yatra on achieving annual spends of ₹1 lakh with the card.
- Another e-voucher valued at ₹2,000 from ClearTrip/Yatra on achieving annual spends of ₹2 lakh with the card.
- The renewal membership fee is waived if the cardholder has achieved annual spends of ₹1 lakh in the previous year.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

A 1% fuel surcharge is waived at all fuel filling stations across India for transaction values between ₹500 and ₹3,000 (max benefit of ₹100 per month).

### Zero Liability Protection

The SBI Card provides zero liability protection with the Simply Click Card. Accordingly, the cardholder will not be liable for any unauthorized/fraudulent transactions made through the card if the card has been reported lost/stolen to the card issuer in a timely manner.

![SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Benefits](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Rewards

- **1 Reward Point/₹100** spent with the card **on offline spends** (reward rate of 0.25%).
- **10X Reward Points/₹100 on** online purchases at **partner merchants, including Apollo 24×7, BookMyShow, Cleartrip, Swiggy, Dominos, IGP, Myntra, Gyftr, Netmeds, and Yatra** (reward rate of 2.5%).
- **5X Reward Points** on all other **online spends** with the card (reward rate of 1.25%).
- A max cap of 10,000 Reward Points per month is applicable on the Bonus (at 5X or 10X rate).
- Cleartrip and Yatra transactions done from affiliate channels will not be eligible for 10X Reward Points.

_No Reward Points shall be awarded for rent, cash advance, balance transfer, fuel, government transactions, wallet loading, digital gaming, and utility bill payments._

### Reward Points Redemption:

The Reward Points you earn using your SimplyCLICK SBI Credit Card can be redeemed by logging into your [SBI Card](https://www.sbicard.com/). You can redeem your earned points against a lot of products and gift vouchers available at the SBI Card’s reward catalog at a rate of 1 Reward Point = ₹0.25. You can also use your reward points against your card’s statement balance. Following is the stepwise procedure to redeem SimplyClick SBI Card Reward Points:

- Visit the official website of SBI Card.
- Click on the option ‘Rewards.’
- You will see a lot of products on your screen.
- Click on a product against which you want to redeem your points.
- If you are using only points to buy it, choose the option ‘Points Only, and if you are buying it with partial points and paying the remaining amount, choose the option ‘Points  + Pay.’
- Log in using your registered mobile number or card details, and proceed to redeem your points.

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Fees and Charges

Every credit card comes with different features & terms and conditions, which include its reward programs, charges like annual fees, interest rates, cash advance fees, and many more things. The following are the fees of the Simply Click SBI Card:

- The annual fee of the Simply Click Credit Card is ₹499 plus applicable taxes. You can get the yearly fee waived from the second year by spending ₹1 lakh or more in the previous anniversary year.
- The interest rate on this credit card is 3.75% per month, which is 45% annually.
- The foreign currency markup fee on the SimplyCLICK SBI Credit Card is 3.5% of the total transaction amount. This fee is charged when you make a transaction in a different currency using your SBI Credit Card.
- The cash advance fee on this credit card is 2.5% of the transaction amount, subject to a minimum of ₹500. This fee is chargeable when you withdraw cash using your SBI Simply Click Credit Card.

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

Every credit card has its own eligibility criteria that need to be fulfilled by an applicant in order to get approved for that card. The significant factors that affect your eligibility for a credit card include your age, income, and credit score. The following are the eligibility criteria for the SimplyCLICK SBI Credit Card:

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Criteria** | **Age** | **Income** |
| Salaried | 18 – 65 Years | ₹20,000 Monthly |
| Self-employed | 18 – 65 Years | ₹20,000 Monthly |

## How to Apply for SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Online?

- Add your details,
- Complete your e-KYC,
- Submit required documents,
- Your application will be submitted for approval.

_As the SBI SimplyClick Credit Card is an entry-level card, you can get it quickly if you don’t have an excellent score. But, it will definitely help you build a perfect credit score with time if you use your card responsibly. If you have the SimplyClick SBI Card, make sure that you always make your credit card bill payments on time. Moreover, try not to use more than 30% of your credit limit, as more than 30% of your credit utilization might affect your credit score badly._

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Limit

The card issuer has not disclosed the minimum or maximum credit card limit provided with the SimplyCLICK SBI Credit Card, as there is no such fixed limit. The credit limit that is assigned to you depends on your past payment behavior and credit history. Individuals with a good credit score may get a higher limit, while others with an average score may get a comparatively lower limit. However, if you are not satisfied with the limit that you got with the SimplyCLICK SBI Card, you can request a higher limit by contacting customer care or by visiting the branch.

But, you should make sure to use your card wisely for some time before applying for a credit limit increase. There are a lot of [ways to increase your credit limit](https://cardinsider.com/sbi-card/credit-card-limit-check-increase/), and you can choose any one of them.

## SBI SimplyCLICK Card Customer Care

If you need any assistance related to your credit card or you want to report an unauthorized transaction on it, you can contact the SBI SimplyClick Cadd customer care via the following contact details:

- You can write your queries or complaints regarding your credit card and send them via email to customercare@sbicard.com.
- To contact the customer via a call, you can dial any of these helpline numbers: 1800 180 1290 (toll-free), 1860 500 1290, 1860 180 1290, or 39 02 02 02.

## Benefits and Drawbacks of SBI SimplyClick Credit Card

All the pros and cons of having the SBI SimplyClick Credit Card are mentioned below:

### Pros

- It offers an excellent reward rate on several online shopping brands and hence is a great option for those who shop online very often.
- It comes with an affordable annual fee of ₹499.
- The annual fee can be waived easily by spending ₹1 lakh in the previous year.

### Cons

- It doesn’t offer complimentary lounge access. Therefore, it is not suitable for someone who travels very often.
- There are limited options for reward redemption.

## Is This The Right Card For You?

The SBI SimplyCLICK Credit card is targeted at average-income individuals who want to save more and more on their spending using a low annual fee credit card. It will also be a great choice for those who spend online very often, as the card provides accelerated reward points on online shopping.

However, the card doesn’t offer any exclusive travel or entertainment benefits, it can be considered the best for those who are frequent shoppers. This card would be the right choice for you if you love online shopping and are looking for a low annual fee credit card with maximum benefits.

## SBI SimplyClick Credit Card Review

Being a highly rewarding credit card for online shoppers (particularly at specific merchants like Apollo 24×7, BookMyShow, Cleartrip, Dominos, IGP, Myntra, Netmeds, and Yatra), the SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card is one of the best offerings for young millennials who love shopping online. Since it is an entry-level credit card with a low membership fee of just ₹499 per year, it is also an excellent card for first-time cardholders.

Other than the regular rewards, it offers you accelerated reward points on online shopping and hence is very beneficial for frequent online shoppers. If you love shopping and want to have a credit card to make your shopping experiences more rewarding, you should definitely apply for the SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card. Make sure to check the eligibility criteria before proceeding further.

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Rewards

- **1 Reward Point/₹100** spent with the card **on offline spends** (reward rate of 0.25%).
- **10X Reward Points/₹100 on** online purchases at **partner merchants, including Apollo 24×7, BookMyShow, Cleartrip, Swiggy, Dominos, IGP, Myntra, Gyftr, Netmeds, and Yatra** (reward rate of 2.5%).
- **5X Reward Points** on all other **online spends** with the card (reward rate of 1.25%).
- A max cap of 10,000 Reward Points per month is applicable on the Bonus (at 5X or 10X rate).
- Cleartrip and Yatra transactions done from affiliate channels will not be eligible for 10X Reward Points.

_No Reward Points shall be awarded for rent, cash advance, balance transfer, fuel, government transactions, wallet loading, digital gaming, and utility bill payments._

### Reward Points Redemption:

The Reward Points you earn using your SimplyCLICK SBI Credit Card can be redeemed by logging into your [SBI Card](https://www.sbicard.com/). You can redeem your earned points against a lot of products and gift vouchers available at the SBI Card’s reward catalog at a rate of 1 Reward Point = ₹0.25. You can also use your reward points against your card’s statement balance. Following is the stepwise procedure to redeem SimplyClick SBI Card Reward Points:

- Visit the official website of SBI Card.
- Click on the option ‘Rewards.’
- You will see a lot of products on your screen.
- Click on a product against which you want to redeem your points.
- If you are using only points to buy it, choose the option ‘Points Only, and if you are buying it with partial points and paying the remaining amount, choose the option ‘Points  + Pay.’
- Log in using your registered mobile number or card details, and proceed to redeem your points.

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Fees and Charges

Every credit card comes with different features & terms and conditions, which include its reward programs, charges like annual fees, interest rates, cash advance fees, and many more things. The following are the fees of the Simply Click SBI Card:

- The annual fee of the Simply Click Credit Card is ₹499 plus applicable taxes. You can get the yearly fee waived from the second year by spending ₹1 lakh or more in the previous anniversary year.
- The interest rate on this credit card is 3.75% per month, which is 45% annually.
- The foreign currency markup fee on the SimplyCLICK SBI Credit Card is 3.5% of the total transaction amount. This fee is charged when you make a transaction in a different currency using your SBI Credit Card.
- The cash advance fee on this credit card is 2.5% of the transaction amount, subject to a minimum of ₹500. This fee is chargeable when you withdraw cash using your SBI Simply Click Credit Card.

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

Every credit card has its own eligibility criteria that need to be fulfilled by an applicant in order to get approved for that card. The significant factors that affect your eligibility for a credit card include your age, income, and credit score. The following are the eligibility criteria for the SimplyCLICK SBI Credit Card:

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Criteria** | **Age** | **Income** |
| Salaried | 18 – 65 Years | ₹20,000 Monthly |
| Self-employed | 18 – 65 Years | ₹20,000 Monthly |

## How to Apply for SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Online?

You can apply for the Simply Click SBI Card online at your convenience with Card Insider. To apply online for the same, you can follow a few simple steps as mentioned below:

- Click on the Apply Now button on this page,
- Add your details,
- Complete your e-KYC,
- Submit required documents,
- Your application will be submitted for approval.

_As the SBI SimplyClick Credit Card is an entry-level card, you can get it quickly if you don’t have an excellent score. But, it will definitely help you build a perfect credit score with time if you use your card responsibly. If you have the SimplyClick SBI Card, make sure that you always make your credit card bill payments on time. Moreover, try not to use more than 30% of your credit limit, as more than 30% of your credit utilization might affect your credit score badly._

## SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card Limit

The card issuer has not disclosed the minimum or maximum credit card limit provided with the SimplyCLICK SBI Credit Card, as there is no such fixed limit. The credit limit that is assigned to you depends on your past payment behavior and credit history. Individuals with a good credit score may get a higher limit, while others with an average score may get a comparatively lower limit. However, if you are not satisfied with the limit that you got with the SimplyCLICK SBI Card, you can request a higher limit by contacting customer care or by visiting the branch.

But, you should make sure to use your card wisely for some time before applying for a credit limit increase. There are a lot of [ways to increase your credit limit](https://cardinsider.com/sbi-card/credit-card-limit-check-increase/), and you can choose any one of them.

## SBI SimplyCLICK Card Customer Care

- You can write your queries or complaints regarding your credit card and send them via email to customercare@sbicard.com.
- To contact the customer via a call, you can dial any of these helpline numbers: 1800 180 1290 (toll-free), 1860 500 1290, 1860 180 1290, or 39 02 02 02.

## Benefits and Drawbacks of SBI SimplyClick Credit Card

All the pros and cons of having the SBI SimplyClick Credit Card are mentioned below:

### Pros

- It offers an excellent reward rate on several online shopping brands and hence is a great option for those who shop online very often.
- It comes with an affordable annual fee of ₹499.
- The annual fee can be waived easily by spending ₹1 lakh in the previous year.

### Cons

- It doesn’t offer complimentary lounge access. Therefore, it is not suitable for someone who travels very often.
- There are limited options for reward redemption.

## Is This The Right Card For You?

The SBI SimplyCLICK Credit card is targeted at average-income individuals who want to save more and more on their spending using a low annual fee credit card. It will also be a great choice for those who spend online very often, as the card provides accelerated reward points on online shopping.

However, the card doesn’t offer any exclusive travel or entertainment benefits, it can be considered the best for those who are frequent shoppers. This card would be the right choice for you if you love online shopping and are looking for a low annual fee credit card with maximum benefits.

## SBI SimplyClick Credit Card Review

Being a highly rewarding credit card for online shoppers (particularly at specific merchants like Apollo 24×7, BookMyShow, Cleartrip, Dominos, IGP, Myntra, Netmeds, and Yatra), the SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card is one of the best offerings for young millennials who love shopping online. Since it is an entry-level credit card with a low membership fee of just ₹499 per year, it is also an excellent card for first-time cardholders.

Other than the regular rewards, it offers you accelerated reward points on online shopping and hence is very beneficial for frequent online shoppers. If you love shopping and want to have a credit card to make your shopping experiences more rewarding, you should definitely apply for the SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card. Make sure to check the eligibility criteria before proceeding further.

# SBI SimplySAVE UPI Rupay Credit Card

By [Bharti Bisht](https://cardinsider.com/author/bharti/ "Posts by Bharti Bisht")

Updated On
1 July, 2025

A great entry-level credit card, the SBI SimplySAVE charges an annual fee of Rs. 499 and offers a welcome benefit of 2000 bonus reward points. You earn accelerated reward points on grocery, movies, and departmental store purchases with the card, making it perfect for individuals who are new to the credit scene and are seeking to save money on essential everyday expenses. This card can also be linked to your UPI applications.

In addition to rewards, you receive an annual fee waiver on spending Rs. 1 lakh or more with the card. This can be easily achieved, hence making this card free from any annual charges. There are limited travel, dining, or entertainment privileges associated with the card, as it is primarily designed for beginners seeking an entry-level credit card. To learn more about the card, its benefits and features, as well as its fees and charges, continue reading.

## SBI Simply SAVE Credit Card Features & Benefits

SBI SimplySAVE Card is a basic yet popular credit card offered by SBI Card. As the name suggests, the card comes with exclusive saving opportunities, as you can save almost every time you spend using it. The following explained features and benefits of the card will make you understand how you can save more and more using this card:

### Welcome Benefits

2,000 Bonus RPs that are worth Rs. 500 on the expenditure of Rs. 2,000 in the first 60 days of card activation.

### UPI Enabled

The SBI Simply SAVE Credit Card is available on the RuPay network, meaning you can link this card to popular UPI applications like Google Pay, PhonePe, Paytm, etc.

### Renewal Fee Waiver

The annual renewal fee of the card for the card can be waived on spending Rs. 1 Lakh or more in the previous year.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

For every fuel transaction between Rs. 500 and Rs. 3,000, the cardholder can get a 1% waiver on fuel surcharge. Note that a maximum of Rs. 100 per month can be waived.

### Add-On Cards

You can avail yourself of add-on SimplySave Credit Cards at no additional cost to your family. Your parents, siblings, and spouse can avail of these add-on credit cards and make their spends. Many times, SBI Card comes up with exciting offers for add-on Simply Save RuPay Credit Cards.

![SBI SimplySAVE Credit Card features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## Simply SAVE SBI Credit Card Rewards:

- 10X Reward Points for every Rs. 150 purchase made on dining, departmental stores, movies, and grocery spending (reward rate = 1.7%). You can earn up to 5,000 accelerated points in a month; after reaching this cap, the normal reward rate shall be applicable.
- 1 Reward Point for every Rs. 150 spent on other purchases (Reward rate = 0.17%).
- No Reward Points shall be awarded for rent, government, and fuel transactions.

### Reward Points Redemption:

Redeem the RPs earned with the Simply Save card for an array of categories, including shopping, purchasing gift vouchers, etc., and you can even use it against your card’s statement balance. You can redeem the SimplySave card reward points as follows:

- Against gift vouchers by logging in to the [SBI card website](https://www.sbicard.com/) or via the SBI mobile app.
- Against the card’s outstanding amount through Internet banking.
- 1 Reward Point = Rs. 0.25, i.e, 4 Reward Points = Re. 1.

## SBI SimplySAVE Credit Card Fees & Charges

It’s really essential to be aware of the fees & charges associated with a credit card before you apply for it. The annual fee is not the only fee charged by credit cards; but there are a lot of other charges as well. These charges include interest rates, forex markup fees, cash advance fees, and many more. Refer to the following points to know the fees & charges on the SBI SimplySave Card:

- Joining & Annual fee: The annual as well as the joining fee for the SimlySAVE card is Rs. 499 + GST.
- Interest Rates: 3.75% per month (45% annually)
- Cash Advance Fee: 2.5% on the amount withdrawn, subject to a minimum of Rs. 500.
- Foreign Currency Mark-up: 3.5% of the transaction amount.

## SBI SimplySAVE Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

The process of getting a credit card is not as easy as it seems to some individuals. Every bank/card issuer has some eligibility criteria for every credit card that needs to be fulfilled to get approved for a particular card. The factors that influence your eligibility are age, your income, and your credit history. So, make sure to check for the eligibility criteria of a credit card before you apply for it. The following is the criteria for the Simply SAVE SBI Card:

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Criteria** | **Age** | **Income** |
| **Salaried** | 18 years – 65 years | Rs. 20,000 per month |
| **Self-employed** | 21 years – 65 years | Rs. 20,000 per month (as per ITR) |

## How to Apply for an SBI SimplySAVE RuPay Card?

One can apply for the Simply Save Card either online or offline. If you want to apply offline, then visit your closest SBI branch and ask the branch executives to provide you with the physical application form to apply for the SimplySave Card. Fill in the form carefully and submit it. To apply for this card online, you can click on the Apply Now button on your screen. You will be redirected to the official credit card application page, where you have to enter a few details and submit your application.

If you need any assistance to apply for the SBI SimplySave Card, you can contact the SBI Card customer care and request them to assist you for the same.

## SBI SimplySave Credit Card Activation & PIN Generation

- Generate your SimplySave SBI Card PIN via an SBI ATM nearest to you. Find the option to generate a PIN using OTP on the main menu and move accordingly.
- Another easy way to activate your card is Internet Banking. Log in /Sign up to your SBi Card internet banking account and look for an option for credit card activation/PIN generation.
- You may contact customer care and ask them to assist you in the PIN generation.

## SimplySAVE SBI Credit Card Limit

The SBI Card has not disclosed anything about the credit limit they provide with the Simply Save Credit Card. The limit can be different for different cardholders, depending on their past payment behavior and credit history. You may get a credit card that is lower than you expected or way higher than that. If the credit limit on your SimplySave SBI Credit Card is not sufficient, the credit limit can be increased by contacting customer care or by visiting the branch. But before applying for a higher limit, you should use the card responsible for at least six months so that your card issuer can trust you and provide you with a higher credit limit. You can also send an SMS INCR XXXX to 5676791, where XXXX denotes the last four digits of your credit card number.

## SBI SimplySAVE Credit Card Bill Payment

Making your credit card statement payment before the due date is one of the most essential steps that must be followed while using a credit card. Following are different ways in which you can make your SimplySave SBI Card bill payments:

- You can pay your Card bill through the SBI bill desk. Just enter your card details, mobile number, email address, and the amount to be paid, and the transaction will be done easily in seconds.
- If you have an account in another bank, you can make bill payments via NEFT. Enter your SBI SimplySave Card number as your account number and find the IFSC code online.
- The YONO app of SBI gives you another simple way to pay your credit card bill. Just register on the app and link your credit card to make payments easily through the app.
- You can also pay the bill through your nearest SBI ATM. Go to the ATM and find the option to pay credit card bills from the main menu. Fill in the required details and pay the bill.
- If you don’t prefer online options, you can visit your nearest SBI branch and pay the bill offline via cash or cheque. You can also drop the cheque at your interest ATM dropbox. But make sure to do it at least a week before the payment due date, as it takes a few days to process the bill payment in this case.

## SBI SimplySave RuPay Credit Card Review

Considering the shopping benefits it offers at a nominal annual fee of just Rs. 499, SimplySAVE SBI Card can be regarded as the best-suited card for first-time cardholders. Apart from shopping (select categories), the card earns you accelerated Reward Points on movie and dining spends as well. However, if you prefer online shopping over offline purchases, the [SBI SimplyCLICK Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/sbi-card/sbi-simplyclick-credit-card/) would be more suitable for you. So, make sure to analyze your spending habits before going further with any decision to get a credit card. Understand where you spend a lot and check whether this card is right for you. However, if you are new to credit cards and want a basic card with reward benefits, you can undoubtedly apply for this one.

This was our review of the SBI Simply Save Credit Card. Do let us know your thoughts on this entry-level shopping SBI credit card in the comment section. Also, if you have used the Simply Save card, let us know your experience with it.

# AU Bank LIT Credit Card

By [Uday Devgan](https://cardinsider.com/author/uday/ "Posts by Uday Devgan")

Updated On
12 June, 2025

The AU Small Finance LIT Credit Card is India’s First customizable Credit card in terms of features. This means that you can choose whatever feature you want on your card and just pay for the same and nothing else. AU Bank has launched a very innovative and only one of its kind credit card. Every other credit card company launches many credit cards with different features and benefits. There are many credit cards in India with different parts & features, and therefore, it becomes challenging and confusing for customers to choose the best card to fulfill their needs from so many cards. The customer often ends up having multiple cards and paying various fees every year. AU Bank took advantage of this and launched the first-ever feature customizing credit cards, the LIT credit card. The bank has launched many other cards with many great benefits, but the LIT card has unique features.

The LIT credit card comes with so many features and benefits. The cardholder can simply log on to the AU 0101 App, select the feature they want to use for the next 90 Days and just pay for it. The Card offers great ways to earn many reward points, like 1 Reward Point for every ₹100 spent on retail and 10X or 5X accelerated Reward Points on the feature that you select. On top of that, the card offers great cashback benefits on the specific feature you select. Not only this, the card offers free memberships to premium platforms like Zee5 and Amazon Prime. For travelers, the card provides excellent cashback offers and complimentary access to airport lounges. Select the feature you want, pay for it, and enjoy the benefits and privileges. Let’s explore more about this innovative card by AU with the detailed review below:

## AU Bank LIT Credit Card

[Apply Now](https://cardinsider.com/go/aubank)

![AU Bank LIT Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

- Surcharge waiver of 1% on all fuel transactions between Rs. 400 to Rs. 5,000.
- 5% and 2% cashback on retail spends on achieving the spend target.
- Additional 5% cashback for travel, dining and grocery expenses.
- 1 complimentary lounge access per quarter

## AU Bank LIT Credit Card Features & Benefits

The AU Small Finance Bank LIT Credit Card, as mentioned, is India’s First customizable credit card which allows you to choose the feature you want and just pay for it and nothing else. The card offers great benefits across lifestyle, Grocery, travel, and much more.

It offers almost every benefit of a credit card and allows its holders to choose one or more features/categories at a time. This enables the customers to get the benefit from the category they selected and also just pay for the feature that they are using rather than paying the full annual fees for the card. With the AU Bank LIT credit card, you have the power to choose your benefits and rewards. You can choose as many features as you want by paying for each one, and they are valid for 90 days. Currently, there are six benefit categories for cardholders to choose from –

### Airport Lounge Access

With this benefit, you can have a comfortable time during your travels and get benefits worth up to ₹1,200. The following are the prices associated with this feature, applicable for a 90-day period.

- Get one visit to domestic airport lounges in India – ₹399 for 90 days

### Memberships

Get access to some of the most popular and in-demand subscription services, and get benefits worth up to ₹758. The following are the prices associated with this feature, applicable for a 90-day period

- **3 Months Zee5 Membership – ₹99 for 90 days**. You must spend at least ₹5000 during the 90-day period, or you will be charged a Rs. 50 penalty.
- **3 Months Amazon Prime Membership – ₹199 for 90 days**. You must spend at least ₹10,000 during the 90-day period, or you will be charged a Rs. 100 penalty.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

Get 1% Fuel Surcharge Waiver – ₹49 for 90 days. The waiver is applicable on all transactions ranging between ₹400 to ₹5000.

You can choose whichever feature you want based on your needs, requirements, and spending habits. The pricing for each segment is valid for a 90-day period and is levied once you opt in for a specific feature. Each part chosen by you is on auto-renewal, and you will have to disable auto-renewal if you don’t want to continue with a particular feature after 90 days.

### Milestone Cashback

Get additional cashback on reaching the minimum spend amount and get benefits worth ₹1,500. The following are the prices associated with this feature, applicable for a 30-day period.

- Get **5% Additional Cashback – ₹299 for 30 days**. You will be eligible for the cashback on spending a minimum of ₹10,000, excluding fuel, cash advance, and rent spends. You can earn a maximum cashback of ₹500 per month.
- Get **2% Additional Cashback – Rs. 199 for 30 days**. You will be eligible for the cashback on spending a minimum of ₹15,000, excluding fuel, cash advance, and rent spends. You can earn a maximum cashback of ₹1,000 per month.

### Category Cashback

You can earn cashback on spends across different categories with this feature and get benefits worth up to₹1,500 per category.

- Get **5% Cashback on Grocery Spends – ₹299 for 90 days**. You can earn a maximum cashback of ₹1,000 per month.
- Get **5% Cashback on Apparel Spends – ₹199 for 90 days**. You can earn a maximum cashback of ₹500 per month.

### 5X/10X Rewards

You can earn bonuses up to 10x rewards on your online and offline spends with this feature.

#### For Online Spends

- Get **10x Reward Points on Online Spends – ₹299 for 90 days**. You can earn 10x reward points on your online spends, but rent, cash withdrawal, and fuel spends are not eligible.
- Get **5x Reward Points on Online Spends – ₹199 for 90 days**. You can earn 5x reward points on your online spends, but rent, cash withdrawal, and fuel spends are not eligible.

#### For Offline Spends

- Get **10x Reward Points on Offline Spends – ₹299 for 90 days**. You can earn 10x reward points on your offline spends, but rent, cash withdrawal, and fuel spends are not eligible.
- Get **5x Reward Points on Offline Spends – ₹199 for 90 days**. You can earn 5x reward points on your offline spends, but rent, cash withdrawal, and fuel spends are not eligible.

![AU Bank LIT Credit Card Features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## AU Bank LIT Credit Card Reward Points & Redemption

1 Reward Point for every ₹100 retail spends done on the Card. Cash withdrawal, EMI, rent, education, and fuel transactions will not be considered for earning Base Reward Points.

### Reward Points Redemption

The reward points earned with the AU Small Finance Bank LIT Credit Card can be redeemed for many benefits:

- Redeem against the purchase of Merchandise.
- For booking tickets for flights, hotels, buses, and movies.
- Use Points to recharge your mobile or DTH.
- Book Golf sessions at golf courses across India.
- Redeem your points for E-gift Vouchers.
- Convert your Reward Points to Air miles.

## How To Select The Category Of Your Choice?

- Open the [AU 0101 App](https://www.aubank.in/personal-banking/digital-banking/au-0101-mobile-banking-app) and go to the Credit Card Homepage, select ‘Manage Credit Card’ to get the list of available benefits, and choose the one that you want.
- Get the price of each category and choose the one that fits your budget and needs.
- Get to modify the benefit as per your choice and get what you want.
- You can manage and track your cashback and the reward points you earned, and also get to know how much is left to earn to complete the target.

## Eligibility Criteria

Certain conditions need to be fulfilled in order to be eligible to apply for an AU Bank LIT Credit Card. These criteria are mentioned below in the table:

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Criteria** | **Categories** |
| **Age** | Primary cardholder between the age of 21 years and 60 years. Add-on cardholder should be over 18 years old |
| **Residency Status** | Indian |

Other than this, the applicants should have a stable source of income, however, the minimum salary requirements are not disclosed clearly by the issuer.

## How to Apply For The AU Bank LIT Credit Card?

The AU Bank LIT Credit Card can be applied both online and offline. To apply online, you can follow a few simple steps as mentioned below:

- Click on ‘Apply Now,’
- Proceed further by filling in the required details.

To apply for this credit card through an offline process, all you have to do is visit the nearest bank branch, fill in the credit card application form, and attach the documents required.

## AU Bank LIT Credit Card Review

The AU Small Finance Bank LIT Credit Card has been specially designed for those who want to get each and every credit card benefit in just one card. The LIT card has removed the hassle of keeping multiple cards for different features by providing the choice to the cardholder to select one or more benefits and enjoy it in a limited timeframe, which provided huge independence to the customer. Not only this, the LIT card is completely lifetime free, which means that the cardholder does not need to pay the fees even when they are not using the card for their benefit. The card provides every other help that a credit card holder generally needs, like great reward points, tons of cashback, fuel surcharge waiver, lounge access, and much more.

This offering by the AU bank is one of its kind as the bank took advantage of the problem and launched India’s first customizable card, which allows users to get the features they need at a particular time. The ICICI Bank also offers a similar credit card named [ICICI Bank Mine Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/icici-bank/icici-bank-mine-credit-card/), which is an affordable card that allows holders to choose the rewards plan at their convenience. Now, this LIT Credit Card issued by AU Bank seems to be a strong competitor of the Mine Card.

## FAQs:

[Can I choose or select more than one category of benefit for the AU Small Finance Bank LIT Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/lit-credit-card/#chck0)


Yes, you can choose any one or even more than one category of benefits. Every category offered with the AU Small Finance Bank LIT Credit Card has a fixed period of 90 days or 3 months.


[What if I still have complimentary lounge access left unused, can I transfer them to the next period?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/lit-credit-card/#chck1)


No, you cannot transfer your unused lounge access. Every category has a fixed 90-day period, if you are unable to use the benefit within the time frame, then the reward or the benefit will expire and will be unusable.


[What is the fuel surcharge waiver I can avail with AU Small Finance Bank LIT Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/lit-credit-card/#chck2)


You can get 1% fuel surcharge waiver on all transactions between ₹400 to ₹5,000 with AU Small Finance Bank LIT Credit Card.


[Can I choose or get any other membership for the OTT platform or program?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/lit-credit-card/#chck3)


No, currently you can get a complimentary membership for the Amazon Prime and Zee5 with the AU Small Finance Bank LIT Credit Card. The Bank may offer other memberships in the future.

# AU Zenith+ Credit Card

By [Ankur Mittal](https://cardinsider.com/author/ankur/ "Posts by Ankur Mittal")

Updated On
15 July, 2025

The AU Zenith+ Metal Credit Card is AU Small Finance Bank’s most luxurious offering. It is perfect for people who frequently travel and make maximum credit card spending. You get ₹5000 luxury gift vouchers from Taj Hotels, Michael Kors, Tata Cliq, and other luxury brands as a welcome benefit. Cardholders can even opt for Reward Points worth ₹5,000 on card activation.

The card’s reward rate is decent: You can earn 2 RPs per ₹100 spent on dining, international, and travel and 1 RP per ₹100 spent elsewhere. You can even get a complimentary Taj Epicure Membership on spending ₹12 Lakhs in the year. Moreover, the card gives you 16 complimentary domestic and international lounge access, golf rounds, and BOGO movie tickets on booking with BookMyShow.

Another great feature is the low 0.99% forex markup fee on the card which is great for those who regularly make foreign currency spends with the card. Read on to learn more about the AU Bank Zenith+ Credit Card.

## Reward Earning Rate

- You can earn 1 RP per ₹100 spent with the card.
- Earn 2 RPs per ₹100 spent on travel, dining, and international spends.
- The value of 1 RP = ₹1. Cash advances, fuel, and EMI spending are not eligible for reward points.

## AU Bank Zenith+ Credit Card – Features

The following are the features and benefits associated with the AU Bank Zenith+ Credit Card–

### Welcome Benefit

Get welcome vouchers worth ₹5000 from Taj Hotels, Tata Cliq, Michael Kors, or other luxury brands. Customers can also opt for 5000 RPs as a welcome benefit.

You can use any of the following modes of activation to get the welcome benefit voucher –

- AU 0101 Mobile App/Net banking
- Call 1800 1200 1500
- Chatbot AURO

### Milestone Benefits

- Get a bonus of 1000 RPs on spending ₹75,000 or more in the month.
- Get a complimentary Taj Epicure Membership on spending ₹12 Lakhs in the card anniversary year. With the [Taj Epicure](https://www.tajhotels.com/en-in/epicureprogram) membership, you get the following benefits –

  - 25% off on Food and beverages – Takeaway and Dining
  - 20% off on Qmin App orders
  - 20% off on Spa Treatments at Jiva Spas
  - 20% off on Salon services at participating salons
  - 20% off on Food and Beverages – Banquets

### Renewal Benefit

Get 5,000 RPs whenever you pay the card membership, i.e., renewal fees from the second year onwards.

### Travel Benefits

- Get 16 complimentary international lounge access per annum, a maximum of 4 per quarter, with the Priority Pass membership.
- Get 16 complimentary domestic lounge access per annum, max four quarter, with the credit card.
- Get a complimentary Taj Epicure Membership on spending ₹12 Lakhs in the card anniversary year.
- Get complimentary meet and greet services with end-to-end VIP check-in four times per year, max once per quarter. You can book the service by calling the concierge at 022-42320298 or 1800 210 0298

### Golf Benefits

AU Zenith+ Credit Cardholders can enjoy up to 8 complimentary rounds of golf or lessons per calendar year, with a maximum of 2 complimentary rounds of golf or lessons per calendar quarter. Any unused complimentary rounds or lessons will not be carried forward to the next calendar quarter.

AU Zenith+ Credit Cardholders need to call the concierge desk at 1800 210 0298 (toll-free) or 022-42320298 to place the request. Cardholders will receive the booking confirmation via email. Check the [complete list of available golf courses](https://www.aubank.in/zenith-plus-tnc-au-zenith-plus-credit-card-golf-booking-terms-and-conditions.pdf) where free lessons/rounds are available with AU Zenith+ Metal Credit Card.

### Movie and Dining Benefits

- Get complimentary 16 Buy One Get One movie tickets per annum, a maximum of 4 per quarter. The maximum discount per booking is ₹500 when booked via BookMyShow. Complete [details regarding the BOGO offer](https://www.aubank.in/zenith-plus_tnc-book-my-show-terms-and-conditions.pdf) on Zenith+ Card are available online.
- Up to 30% discount at partner restaurants with the Silver Spoon dining program

### Insurance Benefits

The following insurance benefits are only available for the primary AU Zenith+ Credit Card.

- Air Accident Cover – Up to ₹2 Crore
- Card Liability Cover – Up to ₹15 Lakhs
- Credit Shield – Up to ₹15 Lakhs
- Purchase Protection – Up to ₹50,000
- Other travel-related covers – In the event of baggage delay, delay in flight, loss of passport, and plane hijack.

## Fees and Charges

The following are the fees and charges associated with the credit card –

- The joining and annual fee of the credit card is ₹4999 plus taxes. You can get the renewal fee waived on spending ₹8 Lakhs in the card anniversary year.
- The interest rate on the credit card is 1.99% per month or 23.8% per annum
- The card charges a [low forex markup fee](https://cardinsider.com/blog/all-about-foreign-currency-markup-fee-on-credit-cards/) of 0.99% of the transaction amount, which is great for individuals who regularly spend foreign currency with their cards.
- The cash advance fee on the card is 2.5% of the transaction amount or a minimum of ₹100

## Eligibility Criteria

The applicant must meet the following eligibility criteria –

- The applicant must be 21 to 60 years of age, and the add-on cardholder must be over 18 years old.
- The applicant must be a resident of India
- The applicant must have a net monthly income of ₹2.5 Lakhs

## AU Zenith+ Metal Credit Card Review

Like the Zenith Credit Card, AU Bank has launched the premium Zenith+ credit card with a joining fee of ₹4999 plus taxes. The cad has decent features and privileges for almost all spend categories and offers ₹5000 gift vouchers from luxury brands. You can get 2 RPs on international, dining, and travel spends, and 1 RP on spends made elsewhere.

Cardholders can get a complimentary Taj Epicure Membership as milestone benefits for those who travel frequently, complimentary domestic and international lounge access, complimentary golf rounds, and a low forex markup fee of 0.99%. The card also offers excellent movie and dining benefits to the cardholder. It is a great choice and offers various benefits similar to the Zenith credit card at a lower fee.

## FAQs:

[What is the joining/annual fee of the Zenith Plus credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/zenith-plus-credit-card/#chck0)


What is the joining/annual fee of the Zenith Plus credit card? The joining and the annual fee of the Zenith Plus credit card is ₹4999 plus GST and you can get the renewal fee waived on spending ₹8 Lakhs in the card anniversary year.


[What is the reward rate on the credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/zenith-plus-credit-card/#chck1)


You can earn 2 RPs per ₹100 spent on travel, international, and dining spends with the card, and 1 RP per ₹100 spent elsewhere.


[What are the movie and dining benefits offered by the card?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/zenith-plus-credit-card/#chck2)


With the card, you can get 16 complimentary BOGO movie tickets per annum, maximum 4 per quarter and you get a maximum discount of ₹500 per card. Also, you get 30% discount at partner restaurants with the Silver Spoon dining program.


[What is the forex markup fee charged by the credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/zenith-plus-credit-card/#chck3)


The card charges a low forex markup fee of 0.99% of the transaction amount, which is great for individuals who regularly spend foreign currency with their cards.


[Does the card offer any milestone benefit?](https://cardinsider.com/au-bank/zenith-plus-credit-card/#chck4)


You get a bonus of 1000 RPs on spending ₹75,000 or more in the month. Also, in the card anniversary year, you get a complimentary Taj hotel membership for ₹12 Lakhs.

# Myntra Kotak Credit Card

By [Ankur Mittal](https://cardinsider.com/author/ankur/ "Posts by Ankur Mittal")

Updated On
17 September, 2025

Myntra is among the top E-Commerce fashion companies in India, featuring apparel from some of the best global and Indian brands. Kotak Mahindra Bank and Myntra have partnered to launch their co-branded credit card. The Myntra Kotak credit card is exclusively designed for the younger generation who are fashion enthusiasts and love shopping on Myntra.

With this credit card, you can earn exclusive cashback on your Myntra purchases as well as on other partner brands. The card also offers a complimentary membership to Myntra Insider and a Rs. 500 worth of Myntra voucher as a welcome benefit. Other than this, you can enjoy complimentary lounge access, milestone benefits, and much more. Check out our Myntra Kotak credit card review to learn about the features and benefits offered by the card, its fees and charges, and other important information related to this co-branded card.

**_As of 10th July 2025, the Myntra Kotak Credit Card has been discontinued._**

## Myntra Kotak Credit Card

![Myntra Kotak Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

5% cashback on Swiggy, 2 complimentary PVR tickets on spends of Rs. 50,000 per quarter

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/myntra-kotak-credit-card/#)

- Myntra voucher worth Rs. 500 on making 1 transaction above Rs. 500 within 30 days of card issuance
- 7.5% discount on Myntra spends, capped at Rs. 750 per transaction
- 5% cashback on partner brands like Swiggy, PVR, Urban Company, etc., capped at Rs. 1000 per cycle
- Unlimited 1.25% cash back on other online and offline spends, with a few exclusions
- Complimentary Myntra Insider program membership
- 1 complimentary domestic lounge access per quarter
- 2 complimentary PVR tickets worth Rs. 250 on spends of Rs. 50,000 per quarter
- Renewal fee waiver on spends of Rs. 2 Lakhs in the anniversary year
- 1% fuel surcharge waiver on transactions between Rs. 500 to Rs. 3000

## Myntra Kotak Credit Card – Rewards and Benefits

### Welcome Benefit

You get a Myntra voucher worth Rs. 500 on activating your card and making 1 transaction above Rs. 500 within 30 days of getting the card. The voucher is sent to the cardholder via SMS or email within 30 days and can be used to get the discount.

### Myntra Insider Program Membership

The credit card offers a complimentary membership to the Myntra Insider program, offering exclusive offers and benefits to the cardholder.

- The cardholder is onboarded to the program within 7 days of credit card issuance.
- The cardholder is onboarded as a Select Member of the program and will need to spend Rs. 7000 minimum and place at least 5 orders in the year on Myntra to maintain the tier.
- The cardholder gets access to benefits like early access to sales and offers, free shipping, exclusive savings and offers, Supercoins, and much more.

### Airport Lounge Access

You can get 1 [complimentary domestic lounge access](https://cardinsider.com/credit-cards-free-domestic-airport-lounge-access-india/) per quarter, where you can enjoy privileges like comfortable seating, gourmet meals, newspapers and magazines, free WiFi, and widescreen TVs.

### Milestone Benefits

Get 2 complimentary PVR movie tickets, each worth Rs. 250, on spends of Rs. 50,000 or more in the calendar quarter. Wallet reloads, rent, and fuel spends are not considered towards the benefit.

### Renewal Fee Waiver

The annual fee can be waived off on spending Rs. 2 Lakhs or more in the card anniversary year.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

You get a 1% fuel surcharge waiver on transactions between Rs. 500 to Rs. 3000. You can get a maximum benefit of Rs. 3500 per year, but fuel spends are not eligible to earn cashback.

## Myntra Kotak Credit Card Cashback & Rewards

### Instant Discount on Myntra Spends

Get an instant 7.5% discount on all your spends on the Myntra website or mobile application. The discount is capped at Rs. 750 per transaction.

### Cashback on Preferred Partners

Get 5% cashback on your spends at preferred partners Swiggy, Swiggy Instamart, Cleartrip, Urban Company, and PVR. The cashback is capped at Rs. 1000 per month and is automatically credited to the card statement.

### Base Cashback

You can get an unlimited 1.25% cashback on all other spends made at offline and online merchants with the Kotak Myntra credit card.

There are a few exclusions on the base cashback –

- Cashback is not applicable on wallet reloads, rent, and fuel spends
- Transactions converted to EMIs are not eligible for cashback, and if you have received cashback, it will be reversed on converting the transaction to EMI.
- Myntra Kotak Credit Card UPI transactions will accrue a cashback of 1.25% only on transactions of Rs. 2000 or more.

### Reward Redemption

The cashback earned by the cardholder is automatically credited to the credit card statement.

## Myntra Kotak Credit Card – Fees and Charges

Here are the fees and charges associated with the Myntra Kotak Credit Card –

- The card charges a joining and annual fee of Rs. 500 plus taxes
- The card charges an interest rate of 3.7% per month and 44.4% per annum
- The foreign currency markup on the card is 3.5% of the transaction amount
- The ATM cash withdrawal fee on the card is 2.5% (a minimum of ₹500)
- The add-on card fee is Rs. 299, and the card replacement fee is Rs. 200

## Other Cards Similar to the Myntra Kotak Credit Card

### Flipkart Axis Bank Credit Card

The [Flipkart Axis Bank credit card](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/flipkart-axis-bank-credit-card/) is great for people who regularly make spends at Flipkart. You can get 5% cashback on Flipkart transactions and also on popular merchants like Uber, Swiggy, PVR, etc. The card also offers complimentary lounge access, a fuel surcharge waiver, and exclusive dining benefits with the Dining Delights program.

- Rs. 600 worth of welcome benefits with the card
- 5% cashback on Flipkart spends
- 4% cashback on preferred partners PVR, cult.fit, Uber, etc.
- 4 complimentary domestic lounge access

### Cashback SBI Credit Card

The [Cashback SBI card](https://cardinsider.com/sbi-card/cashback-sbi-credit-card/) is newly launched by the bank and offers cashback on almost all spends made by you. The card offers up to 5% cashback on online spends and 1% cashback on offline spends made by you. The annual fees of the card is Rs. 999 plus taxes, but you can get it waived on spending Rs. 2 Lakhs or more in the year.

- 5% cashback on online transactions
- 1% cashback on offline transactions
- Cashback is capped at a maximum of Rs. 5000 per month
- Spend-based annual fee waiver

## Myntra Kotak Credit Card Review by Card Insider

Myntra has always been one of the favorite portals for online shopping among youngsters and has the best clothing and apparel for everyone. Kotak Mahindra Bank and Myntra have launched their co-branded Myntra Kotak credit card, which is perfect for young millennials and fashionistas. With this card, you get unlimited discounts on your spends on Myntra.

Apart from this, you also earn cashback on spends made at partner brands and unlimited 1.25% cashback on other offline and online spends, excluding a few categories. Earlier, the card could be availed at a nominal charge of Rs. 500, which is now waived for a limited time. Customers can now get the Myntra Credit Card at zero joining charge. The annual fee shall also be waived for a period of three years. Also, you get complimentary lounge access, milestone benefits, Myntra Insider membership, and many other exciting privileges.

Head over to the comments section to tell us your thoughts regarding this new co-branded Kotak Myntra credit card and if you plan on applying for it.

## FAQs:

[How much discount do I get on spends at Myntra with the credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/myntra-kotak-credit-card/#chck0)


Cardholders get an unlimited discount of 7.5% on all spends made on the Myntra website and mobile application. The discount is capped at a maximum Rs. 750 per transaction.


[Which are the preferred partner brands on which I receive additional cashback?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/myntra-kotak-credit-card/#chck1)


You get 5% cashback on your spends at preferred partners Swiggy, Swiggy Instamart, Cleartrip, Urban Company, and PVR. The cashback is capped at Rs. 1000 per month and is automatically credited to the card statement.


[Do I get complimentary lounge access with the credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/myntra-kotak-credit-card/#chck2)


Yes, you get 1 complimentary domestic lounge access per quarter with the card. You can enjoy privileges like comfortable seating, gourmet meals, newspapers and magazines, free WiFi, and widescreen TVs.


[What are the milestone benefits on this credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/myntra-kotak-credit-card/#chck3)


You can get 2 complimentary PVR movie tickets, each worth Rs. 250, on spends of Rs. 50,000 or more in the calendar quarter, as the milestone benefit. Wallet reloads, rent, and fuel spends are not considered towards the benefit.


[What is the cash withdrawal fee on this credit card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/myntra-kotak-credit-card/#chck4)


The ATM cash withdrawal fee on the credit card is 2.5%, subject to a minimum amount of ₹500.

# Kotak 811 Credit Card

By [Rajat Gaur](https://cardinsider.com/author/rajat/ "Posts by Rajat Gaur")

Updated On
20 June, 2025

Kotak 811 Credit Card is an entry-level card recently launched by Kotak Mahindra Bank. This is a secured card issued against a Fixed Deposit. Kotak 811 Credit Card comes with a membership fee of Rs. 500 per annum, which is waived on an annual expenditure of a minimum of Rs. 50,000. The card offers a reward rate of 0.5% on online spends and 0.25% on offline transactions made with the card.

Apart from the reward rate, you are also entitled to a milestone benefit of Rs. 750 cashback on spending a minimum of Rs. 75,000 with the card in the preceding membership year. Other than this, the card has several other advantages, including insurance benefits, fuel surcharge waiver, etc. Read on to learn more about this entry-level offering by Kotak Bank.

## Kotak 811 Credit Card

![Kotak 811 Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

(4.4/5)


#### Joining Fee


Rs. 500 + GST


#### Renewal Fee


Rs. 500 + GST


#### Best Suited For

Shopping \|

#### Reward Type

Reward Points
\|

#### Welcome Benefits

500 Bonus Reward Points on expenditure of Rs. 5,000 within 45 days of card issuance.

[Rewards and Benefits](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#)

#### Movie & Dining

Option to get 4 PVR tickets on spending Rs. 75,000 in a year.


#### Rewards Rate

1 Reward Point per Rs. 100 on offline spends and 2 Reward Points per Rs. 100 on online spends.


#### Reward Redemption


Reward Points redeemable for mobile recharges, shopping vouchers, gift cards, movie and travel vouchers at Kotak Rewards portal.


#### Travel


N/A


#### Domestic Lounge Access


N/A


#### International Lounge Access


N/A


#### Golf

N/A


#### Insurance Benefits


Lost card cover worth Rs. 50,000 applicable 7 days prior to reporting the loss of card


[Fees & Charges](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#)

#### Spend-Based Waiver


Annual fee waived off on spending a minimum of Rs. 50,000 in the preceding year.


#### Rewards Redemption Fee

Nil


#### Foreign Currency Markup


3.5% of the transaction amount

#### Interest Rates

3.75% Per Month (45% Annually)


#### Fuel Surcharge

1% fuel surcharge waived off on transactions between Rs. 500 and Rs. 3,000 (max waiver capped at Rs. 3,500 per annum).


#### Cash Advance Charges


2.5% of the Cash Withdrawn (Minimum Charge of ₹500)

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#)

- 500 Bonus Reward Points as a welcome benefit on spending Rs. 5,000 with 45 days of card issuance.
- 2 Reward Points for every Rs. 100 spent online and 1 Reward Point for every Rs. 100 spent offline.
- 4 PVR tickets of a cashback of Rs. 750 on spending a minimum of Rs. 75,000 in a year.
- 1.8% surcharge waived off on online transactions at IRCTC and 2.5% surcharge waived off on rail ticket bookings at counters.
- 1% fuel surcharge waived off on transactions between Rs. 500 and Rs. 3,000 (max waiver capped at Rs. 3,500/year).
- Lost card insurance cover worth Rs. 50,000 against fraudulent usage of the card applicable 7 days pre-reporting the loss of card to the bank.
- Annual fee of Rs. 500 waived off on spending a minimum of Rs. 50,000 in the preceding anniversary year.

## Kotak 811 Credit Card Rewards

- The card earns you 1 Reward Point per Rs. 100 spent offline.
- You earn 2x Reward Points (i.e. 2 Reward Points per Rs. 100) on online spends.

### Reward Redemption

The Reward Points earned on the Kotak 811 Credit Card are redeemable for shopping vouchers, e-gift vouchers, movie vouchers, mobile recharges, and many other redemption categories. 1 Reward Point = Rs. 0.25.

## Kotak 811 Credit Card Features and Benefits

The Kotak 811 Credit Card is one of the basic credit cards offered by Kotak Bank. It comes with an affordable annual fee and is a great choice for individuals who just want a credit card with a decent reward rate to save more on their spends. Following are the detailed features and benefits of this card:

### Welcome Benefit

You get 500 Bonus Reward Points on spending a minimum of Rs. 5,000 within the first 45 days of card issuance.

### Milestone Benefit

If you spend a minimum of Rs. 75,000 with the card in a given membership year, you can choose from any of the following two milestone benefits-

- 4 complimentary PVR tickets.
- A cashback of Rs. 750.

### Railway Surcharge Waiver

- 1.8% surcharge is waived on rail ticket bookings via the IRCTC website/app.
- A 2.5% surcharge is waived on rail ticket bookings through railway counters.
- The maximum waiver amount is capped at Rs. 500 in a calendar year.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

- A 1% fuel surcharge will be waived at all filling stations across India for transactions between Rs. 500 and Rs. 3,000.
- A max capping of Rs. 3,500 per year is applicable on the fuel surcharge waiver.

### Insurance Benefit

Kotak 811 Credit Cardholders get complimentary insurance coverage worth Rs. 50,000 against the loss of their card. The cover is applicable from 7 days prior to the date of reporting the loss of the card to the bank.

## Eligibility Criteria for Kotak 811 Credit Card

To be eligible for Kotak 811 Credit Card, an applicant must satisfy the following eligibility criteria-

- the primary cardholder must be in the age bracket of 18 years and 65 years, and the add-on cardholder must be above 18 years of age.
- the primary and add-on cardholder(s) must be resident(s) of India.

## Conclusion

Kotak 811 Credit Card can be the card of choice for new-to-credit young professionals and millennials as it is an entry-level offering by the bank and does not have very strict eligibility criteria. The card is comparable to its secured variant- the [811 #DreamDifferent Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-dreamdifferent-credit-card/) in terms of the rewards and benefits offered. However, the secured variant comes with a zero annual fee; whereas this unsecured card charges an annual fee of Rs. 500. Along with offering a decent reward rate, the card provides its customers with a lot of other privileges, including complimentary movie tickets as a milestone benefit and many more. With a nominal annual fee and basic features & benefits, this card is an excellent choice for individuals who are new to the world of credit cards and want to start their journey with a basic one. If you have any further doubts regarding this card or its features, please ask us in the comments.

## FAQs:

[Is Kotak 811 Credit Card a secured card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#chck0)


No, this credit card is not a secured one. However, the bank also offers its secured variant which is named Kotak 811 #DreamDifferent Credit Card.


[Is this card lifetime free?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#chck1)


No, the Kotak 811 Credit Card is not lifetime free. It comes with an annual fee of Rs. 500.


[What is the value of 1 Reward Point that I earn using this card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#chck2)


For the Kotak 811 Credit Card, 1 Reward Point = Re. 0.25 for redemption against shopping, e-vouchers, and various other options.


[Do I get complimentary lounge access with the Kotak 811 Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#chck3)


No, you don't get complimentary airport lounge access with the Kotak 811 Credit Card.

# IndianOil Kotak Credit Card

By [Ankur Mittal](https://cardinsider.com/author/ankur/ "Posts by Ankur Mittal")

Updated On
11 September, 2025

Recently, Indian Oil and Kotak Mahindra Bank joined forces to launch their co-branded fuel credit card. The IndianOil Kotak credit card is a nice entry-level card perfect for those who are new to the credit scene and want great fuel-based benefits and other rewards.

IndianOil has more than 34,000 outlets across the country, and cardholders will be rewarded every time they refuel their vehicle at an Indian Oil fuel station. The card also offers reward points on grocery, dining, and other payments. These reward points can be redeemed for free fuel, cashback, or other products and vouchers at KotakRewards.

The IndianOil Kotak credit card can reward you with up to 60 liters of free fuel every year, along with a host of other benefits. Those who travel a lot can reduce the effect of high fuel prices through this card and can have a highly rewarding experience if they prefer IndianOil petrol pumps over other countries. Keep on reading to know more about the fuel credit card by Kotak Mahindra Bank and IndianOil.

## IndianOil Kotak Credit Card

## IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card Features and Benefits

As discussed above, the IndianOil Kotak Bank credit card is a [co-branded fuel card](https://cardinsider.com/best-fuel-credit-cards-india/) offering a nice reward rate on fuel as well as other transactions. Let’s know more about the card’s benefits and features in detail –

### Welcome Benefits

- You can get a welcome benefit of 1000 Reward Points on spending Rs. 500 or more within 30 days of getting the card.
- The benefit is only available to those who pay the joining fee for the credit card.

### Annual Fee Waiver

You can get the annual fee of the credit card waived on making spends of Rs. 50,000 or more in the card anniversary year.

### Reward Accrual

- Get 4% back as reward points on your fuel spends. You can earn 24 reward points on spending every Rs. 150 on fuel, and you can earn a maximum of 1200 reward points per month.
- Get 2% as reward points on dining and grocery spends. You can earn 12 reward points on spending every Rs. 150 on dining and grocery, and you can earn a maximum of 800 reward points every month.
- Get 0.5% as reward points on other spends. You will earn 3 reward points on spending every Rs. 150, and there is no maximum monthly capping on this.
- Also, you get 1000 reward points as your welcome benefit.

### Reward Redemption

- Cardholders can redeem their Reward Points as cashback at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.20. You must have a minimum of 2,000 Reward Points.
- You can redeem your reward points at Rs 0.25 against Kotak’s exclusive catalog at [Kotakrewards](https://kotakrewards.com/). You can purchase merchandise, products, travel and gift vouchers, mobile recharge, etc.
- You can convert your reward points into IndianOil XTRAREWARDS points only in multiples of 300 through Net or Mobile Banking. 300 RPs = 250 XTRAREWARDS points, where 1 XTRAREWARD point is worth Rs. 0.30. These points can be used to pay for fuel at IndianOil outlets.

### Concierge Services

- You get personal concierge services and assistance for gift delivery, restaurant bookings, flower delivery, car rental, limousine reservation, movie ticket booking, golf reservations, and much more.
- You can call 1800-26-78729 to avail of these concierge services.

### Add-on Card

- You can get an add-on card for free that has all the benefits of your primary card.
- You can set the spending limit on the add-on card as you wish.
- You can separately track the spending on every add-on card.

### Insurance Benefits

You get an insurance cover worth Rs. 2 Lakhs for personal accidents

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

You get a 1% fuel surcharge waiver on fuel transactions between Rs. 100 and Rs. 5000, with a maximum benefit of Rs. 100 every statement cycle.

### Contactless Payment

With Rupay Contactless technology, you can make transactions of up to Rs. 5,000 with your credit card without entering your PIN.

![IndianOil Kotak Credit Card Features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card Fees and Charges

Here are the several fees and charges associated with the IndianOil Kotak Bank credit card–

- The joining and renewal fee of the credit card is ₹449 plus taxes, which can be waived on spending ₹50,000 or more in the previous year.
- The interest rate charged by the card is 3.75% per month, equivalent to 45% per annum.
- The foreign currency markup fee on this credit card is 3.5%.
- There are no charges for obtaining an add-on card; however, a replacement card will incur a fee of ₹100.
- The cash advance charges are 2.5% of the amount withdrawn, subject to a minimum of ₹500.

## IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

You have to pass certain eligibility requirements in order to get approved for the credit card–

- Individuals must be a resident of India.
- Individuals must be between 18 and 65 years of age.
- Individuals must have a stable source of income and other necessary documents.

### Documents Required

You have to submit your ID, address, and income proof when applying for the credit card. Here is a list of essential documents that are required –

- ID Proof – PAN Card
- Address Proof – Aadhar Card, Electricity/Water Bills, Ration Card, Passport, etc.
- Income Proof – Latest salary slips, Form 16, latest bank statements, audited ITR.

## IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card Review

# IndusInd Bank Avios Visa Infinite Credit Card

By [Manan Singh](https://cardinsider.com/author/manan/ "Posts by Manan Singh")

When it comes to private sector financial institutions in the country, one of the first names that pops up in our minds is IndusInd Bank. The bank offers various banking services and has an admirable range of credit cards. The bank has done something unique to offer a reimagined travel experience to its cardholders, as it offers a multi-co-branded travel credit card for customers.

Those who frequently travel to foreign countries must have heard about both Qatar Airways and British Airways. Both these airlines operate some of the best-in-class flights, and their loyalty programs, the Qatar Airways Privilege Club and the British Airways Executive Club, have members throughout the world. With the Avios credit card, you earn Avios, which are the reward points of both airlines’ loyalty programs. It can also be used for exclusive perks and benefits on your travels, especially international flights. The Avios credit card is available in the Visa Infinite variant. When you apply for it, you can choose any one of the two airline loyalty programs and your preferred destination to earn maximum Avios points and other benefits and privileges.

The Avios Visa Infinite credit card offers Avios as reward points that can be used at both British Airways and Qatar Airways.

- 3X Avios/₹200 spent on other domestic and international spends, no matter which airline program you opt for.

- **For British Airways**, you can spend the Avios earned by you for reward flights, class upgrades, booking British Airways Holidays, booking flights with Cash + Miles, booking hotel stays, car rentals, travel experiences, and more.

## Travel Benefits

The IndusInd Avios Visa Infinite credit card offers the following travel benefits to cardholders.

### Airport Meet and Greet

Getting extra care when you are traveling, especially on long flights, is a great experience. The card offers two complimentary Airport Meet and Greet services every year at top airports for the cardholder. Both the primary cardholder and his companion get the service. The services include –

- Dedicated 1-to-1 service
- Meet and greet when you arrive at the airport terminal
- A guide to accompany you throughout the airport for proper security and seamless immigration
- Assistance with baggage and security
- Porter service for a maximum of 3 bags

### Priority Pass Program

The Avios credit card offers a complimentary [Priority Pass](https://cardinsider.com/blog/priority-pass-program-guide/) membership to the cardholder that offers access to international airport lounges worldwide. The cardholder gets two lounge access per quarter through the Priority Pass.

### Domestic Lounge Access

With the card, the cardholder gets two complimentary domestic lounge access per quarter at participating lounges. You get to relax at the lounge and enjoy gourmet meals, Free WiFi, entertainment, and more.

### Airline Benefits

For cardholders who choose the Qatar Airways Privilege Club program, they get an exclusive 10% discount on booking flights via the Qatar Airways app or website. Cardholders get the discount on all flights departing from India, and they need to use the code ‘IDSCB23’ to get the benefit.

### Insurance Benefits

The Avios credit card offers overseas medical insurance worth $25,000, and air accident insurance cover of ₹25 lakh to the cardholder. Other than this, IndusInd has partnered with Oriental Insurance Company to offer the following coverage –

- Lost baggage – ₹1 Lakh
- Delayed baggage – ₹25,000
- Loss of passport – ₹50,000
- Lost ticket or missed flight connection – ₹25,000

### Concierge Service

The Avios credit card offers [concierge service](https://cardinsider.com/blog/what-are-credit-card-concierge-services/) to make sure everything is planned properly for you during your travel, and you can have a nice time.

### Elderly Care Assist

While you are out traveling, the concierge service takes care of the elderly people in your family back in India. They help with home care, OPD appointments, healthcare packages, diagnostic test bookings, teleconsultation appointments, home quarantine, physiotherapy assistance, consultation with wellness experts, dieticians, nutritionists, etc.

### International Events

The concierge service will help you visit iconic events and plan the entire itinerary for you. They will arrange tickets for sold-out events and hospitality events. You can visit football matches, tennis tournaments, motor racing events, fashion events, music festivals and concerts, art exhibitions, etc.

### Other Services

The concierge services will help and assist you with other things, including –

- Pre-trip assistance and planning
- Gift and flower delivery assistance
- Hotel referral and reservation bookings
- Dining referrals and reservation bookings

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

You get a 1% fuel surcharge waiver on transactions between ₹500 and ₹10,000 at fuel stations across the country.

### Total Protect

The Total Protect program will protect you against fraudulent activity on your card up to your credit limit, and it also protects the add-on cards. You get protection against unauthorized transactions, counterfeit cards, and fraudulent use in case of a stolen card.

## IndusInd Bank Avios Credit Card Other Features

With the Avios credit card, the cardholder gets access to many unique experiences that take the overall travel experience to a whole new level. The luxury, relaxation, and comfort offered by both Qatar Airways and British Airways join forces with the convenience and trust offered by IndusInd Bank on the Avios Visa Infinite credit card.

With this credit card, your travels and journeys will surely be unforgettable and the best you can imagine. As it is a multi-brand card, you can choose to opt for either the Qatar Airways or British Airways program, and each of them has different welcome, renewal, and other benefits. Let’s know more about what the IndusInd Bank Avios credit card has to offer –

### Welcome Benefits

When you get the IndusInd Bank Avios Infinite credit card, you get access to world-class adventures and travel. The card onboards with a bonus of 20,000 Avios.

### Milestone Benefits

The Avios Visa Infinite credit card has amazing milestone benefits on offer for cardholders. No matter the airline program chosen by you, you get the following milestone benefits –

- Bonus 18,000 Avios on spending ₹8 Lakhs or more in the year
- Bonus 18,000 Avios more on a second spending of ₹8 Lakhs in the year
- The maximum amount of Avios you can earn in a year through milestone benefits is 36,000, and the bonus Avios is credited within 10 days after card statement generation once you achieve the milestone spend.

### Renewal Benefits

Bonus 5,000 Avios that are credited within 15 days of paying the renewal fee.

## IndusInd Bank Avios Visa Infinite Credit Card – Fees & Charges

The following are the fees associated with the credit card –

- The credit card comes at a joining fee of ₹10,000 and an annual fee of ₹5,000 that has to be paid after 12 months of card issuance.
- The interest rate on the card is 3% per month or 36% per annum.
- The cash advance fee on the credit card is waived.
- The forex markup fee on the card is 3.5% everywhere and 1.5% at preferred international destinations.

## Bottom Line

Frequent travelers and those who are looking for a nice travel credit card should consider the IndusInd Bank Avios Visa Infinite credit card. IndusInd Bank has partnered with both British Airways and Qatar Airways to launch this multi-brand credit card. This card works on the Visa Infinite network and offers Avios as reward points, which is the reward currency for both Qatar and British Airways’ loyalty programs.

The cardholder can earn up to 6X Avios/₹200 spent for POS transactions and earn 5X Avios/₹200 for British Airways and Qatar Airways bookings. If you regularly make foreign currency spends, then you can benefit from the low 1.5% forex markup fee at the preferred destinations. Moreover, the card is packed with travel benefits, including meet and greet service at airports, complimentary international as well as domestic lounge access, concierge services, discounts on flight bookings, and more.

Even though the card has a high joining fee and annual fee, it still offers many great privileges for people who travel frequently, especially on international flights through British Airways and Qatar Airways.

The IndusInd Bank Avios Credit Card has now become valuable for many ever since its revaluation in April 2025. This is a must-have card for those who often travel across the globe. Do let us know your thoughts on this multi-brand travel credit card by IndusInd Bank, and if you plan on applying for it.

IndusInd Bank has launched its first RuPay Credit Card. This unique card will offer you 2 Reward Points for every Rs. 100 spent on UPI transactions. This is mainly because of the increasing popularity of the RuPay network and the ease of linking it with the UPI application of your choice.

Introducing the Platinum RuPay Credit Card – a credit card that is free for life and is bound to pique the interest of both veteran and first-time cardholders. This card is particularly beneficial for those who frequently make transactions through their UPI apps, such as Google Pay, PhonePe, and Paytm.

## IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card Rewards

- You will get 2 Reward Points on every Rs. 100 spent with UPI.
- You shall receive 1 Reward Point for every non-UPI transaction of Rs. 100.
- You will also earn Rs. 0.70 for all eligible spends. This is valid for both UPI and non-UPI transactions.
- **You shall only earn Reward Points up to your given credit limit.**
- No Reward Points shall be awarded for utility bills, government services, insurance premiums, education fees, spends on fuel, and rental payments.

### Redemption of Reward Points

You can redeem your Reward Points earned with the Platinum RuPay Credit Card for the following.

- Earn instant discounts, cashback offers, and a lot more on popular brands with [IndusMoments](https://www.indusmoments.com/).
- Redeem your reward points for KrisFlyer miles. 400 Reward Points = 100 KrisFlyer miles.
- You can use your reward points to redeem their cash value and pay off your outstanding credit card. 1 Reward Point = Rs. 0.35.
