- Fill in your particulars and
- Submit your application for the new card.

## How to Link Your Platinum RuPay Credit Card With UPI?

Quickly link your Platinum RuPay Credit Card on UPI in a few steps given below.

- Open your UPI app\* BHIM, PhonePe, Paytm, or Google Pay.
- Select the option “Add Credit Card/Link Credit Card”
- Select Credit Card Issuer Bank (IndusInd Bank)
- Select your IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card
- Proceed to generate UPI PIN

## IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card Review

The IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card is most suited for people who make regular payments through UPI. Whether it’s groceries or dining, you can earn 2 Reward Points on every spend of Rs. 100 with the UPI application of your choice. Not only this, transactions not made through UPI shall also get 1 Reward Point for every Rs. 100 spent.

Although this card offers reasonable rewards for UPI payments, there are a few other benefits associated with it. This entry-level credit card is designed for beginners or first-time cardholders to build their [credit score](https://cardinsider.com/resources/what-is-credit-score-complete-guide/) and adjust to using a credit card.

IndusInd Bank, in collaboration with EazyDiner, a popular online dining reservation platform, has introduced one of the most rewarding dining credit cards in India. The EazyDiner IndusInd Signature Credit Card carries a joining and annual renewal fee of ₹2,999 and welcomes cardholders with 2,000 bonus EazyPoints, a Postcard Hotels stay voucher worth ₹7,500, and a complimentary EazyDiner Prime membership worth ₹3,550, which automatically gets renewed yearly.

Cardholders can enjoy dining benefits of up to ₹60,000 at premium and fine dining restaurants with EazyDiner. They will also receive a decent reward rate on every category spent and accelerated points for dining transactions made via the EazyDiner app. In addition to this, cardholders receive up to 50% extra discount on dining-related expenses via EazyDiner, 3X EazyPoints on all orders, and numerous other dining benefits.

## EazyDiner IndusInd Signature Credit Card Features and Benefits

The EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card is a co-branded card that offers exciting rewards rates and various benefits across multiple categories. Below are the detailed benefits & features of this credit card:

### Welcome Benefits

- Get 2,000 EazyPoints after the joining fee has been successfully paid.
- Get a complimentary Eazy Diner Prime membership for a year worth ₹3,550. After the annual fee is paid, the membership can be renewed annually.
- Postcard Hotels stay voucher worth ₹7,500. The offer is applicable for stays booked directly, either through the [Postcard Hotels](https://www.postcardresorts.com/) website or the reservations concierge at +91 79995 55222.

### Dining Benefits

Being a credit card targeted at people who dine out very often, the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card comes with several exclusive dining privileges as follows:

- Enjoy a 25% discount on your dining bills when you pay via Payeazy on EazyDiner.
- An additional 25% upto ₹1,000 off on paying bills using the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card via PayEazy on the EazyDiner app. The extra 25% off is capped at ₹2,000 each month without any spend-based conditions. Those who wish to access additional discounts of ₹5,000 can do so by making non-dining spends of ₹30,000 or more each month.
- Enjoy a free premium alcoholic beverage for each guest at selected restaurants nationwide. This offer is available at specific restaurants listed on the EazyDiner App. You can identify these restaurants by looking for the “Free alcoholic beverage” deal text on the restaurant listing page in the app.

### Renewal Benefits

The EazyDiner Prime membership is renewed annually upon payment of the annual fees. Cardholders shall also receive a Postcard Hotels voucher worth ₹7,500.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

With this card, you can get a 1% waiver on the surcharge applicable on fuel purchases between ₹500 and ₹3,000. This waiver is capped at ₹250 per statement cycle.

## EazyDiner IndusInd Credit Card Reward Points

The reward rate of the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card in different categories is mentioned below:

- You get **10 Reward Points/₹100 spent on hotels, travel, entertainment, and shopping**.
- You get **4 Reward Points/₹100 on all other eligible retail spend**.
- No Reward Points shall be awarded for dining spends.
- Other than the Reward points, you shall also earn 3X EazyPoints on making payments via the EazyDiner IndusInd Card.
- Reward Points shall only be earned up to the assigned credit limit of each cardholder.

**1 Reward Point = ₹0.10**

### Reward Redemption

The reward points accumulated via the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card are redeemable against dining-related spending on the EazyDiner app. You can earn Reward Points using the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card, and these points can be instantly redeemed against your dining bill. However, the instant redemption of Reward Points is only possible if you make the payment via PayEazy on the EazyDiner app. When making the payment, you have the option to choose the number of Reward Points you would like to redeem.

_Please note that the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card must be used to make the balance payment while redeeming the Reward Points. These reward points can not be redeemed against cash credit, and they expire one year after the accumulation date._

- You shall be redirected to the official application page.

## Bottom Line

While several credit cards offer high discounts and cashback on ordering food online, like the [Swiggy HDFC Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/swiggy-hdfc-bank-credit-card/), the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card is an excellent choice for those who frequently dine out.

The renewal fee appears to be relatively high, but considering all the benefits of this card, it is worth paying a fee of ₹2,999 for such an exclusive credit card. With this credit card, you earn accelerated rewards on travel spending, additional discounts of up to 50%, and many more benefits.

The HDFC [Regalia Gold](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/) Credit Card is also a lifestyle card that offers exciting dining benefits, but the EazyDiner co-branded card, issued by IndusInd Bank, remains the best option for foodies.

# Kotak 811 Credit Card

By [Rajat Gaur](https://cardinsider.com/author/rajat/ "Posts by Rajat Gaur")

Updated On
20 June, 2025

Kotak 811 Credit Card is an entry-level card recently launched by Kotak Mahindra Bank. This is a secured card issued against a Fixed Deposit. Kotak 811 Credit Card comes with a membership fee of Rs. 500 per annum, which is waived on an annual expenditure of a minimum of Rs. 50,000. The card offers a reward rate of 0.5% on online spends and 0.25% on offline transactions made with the card.

Apart from the reward rate, you are also entitled to a milestone benefit of Rs. 750 cashback on spending a minimum of Rs. 75,000 with the card in the preceding membership year. Other than this, the card has several other advantages, including insurance benefits, fuel surcharge waiver, etc. Read on to learn more about this entry-level offering by Kotak Bank.

## Kotak 811 Credit Card

![Kotak 811 Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

(4.4/5)


#### Joining Fee


Rs. 500 + GST


#### Renewal Fee


Rs. 500 + GST


#### Best Suited For

Shopping \|

#### Reward Type

Reward Points
\|

#### Welcome Benefits

500 Bonus Reward Points on expenditure of Rs. 5,000 within 45 days of card issuance.

[Rewards and Benefits](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#)

#### Movie & Dining

Option to get 4 PVR tickets on spending Rs. 75,000 in a year.


#### Rewards Rate

1 Reward Point per Rs. 100 on offline spends and 2 Reward Points per Rs. 100 on online spends.


#### Reward Redemption


Reward Points redeemable for mobile recharges, shopping vouchers, gift cards, movie and travel vouchers at Kotak Rewards portal.


#### Travel


N/A


#### Domestic Lounge Access


N/A


#### International Lounge Access


N/A


#### Golf

N/A


#### Insurance Benefits


Lost card cover worth Rs. 50,000 applicable 7 days prior to reporting the loss of card


[Fees & Charges](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#)

#### Spend-Based Waiver


Annual fee waived off on spending a minimum of Rs. 50,000 in the preceding year.


#### Rewards Redemption Fee

Nil


#### Foreign Currency Markup


3.5% of the transaction amount

#### Interest Rates

3.75% Per Month (45% Annually)


#### Fuel Surcharge

1% fuel surcharge waived off on transactions between Rs. 500 and Rs. 3,000 (max waiver capped at Rs. 3,500 per annum).


#### Cash Advance Charges


2.5% of the Cash Withdrawn (Minimum Charge of ₹500)

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#)

- 500 Bonus Reward Points as a welcome benefit on spending Rs. 5,000 with 45 days of card issuance.
- 2 Reward Points for every Rs. 100 spent online and 1 Reward Point for every Rs. 100 spent offline.
- 4 PVR tickets of a cashback of Rs. 750 on spending a minimum of Rs. 75,000 in a year.
- 1.8% surcharge waived off on online transactions at IRCTC and 2.5% surcharge waived off on rail ticket bookings at counters.
- 1% fuel surcharge waived off on transactions between Rs. 500 and Rs. 3,000 (max waiver capped at Rs. 3,500/year).
- Lost card insurance cover worth Rs. 50,000 against fraudulent usage of the card applicable 7 days pre-reporting the loss of card to the bank.
- Annual fee of Rs. 500 waived off on spending a minimum of Rs. 50,000 in the preceding anniversary year.

## Kotak 811 Credit Card Rewards

- The card earns you 1 Reward Point per Rs. 100 spent offline.
- You earn 2x Reward Points (i.e. 2 Reward Points per Rs. 100) on online spends.

### Reward Redemption

The Reward Points earned on the Kotak 811 Credit Card are redeemable for shopping vouchers, e-gift vouchers, movie vouchers, mobile recharges, and many other redemption categories. 1 Reward Point = Rs. 0.25.

## Kotak 811 Credit Card Features and Benefits

The Kotak 811 Credit Card is one of the basic credit cards offered by Kotak Bank. It comes with an affordable annual fee and is a great choice for individuals who just want a credit card with a decent reward rate to save more on their spends. Following are the detailed features and benefits of this card:

### Welcome Benefit

You get 500 Bonus Reward Points on spending a minimum of Rs. 5,000 within the first 45 days of card issuance.

### Milestone Benefit

If you spend a minimum of Rs. 75,000 with the card in a given membership year, you can choose from any of the following two milestone benefits-

- 4 complimentary PVR tickets.
- A cashback of Rs. 750.

### Railway Surcharge Waiver

- 1.8% surcharge is waived on rail ticket bookings via the IRCTC website/app.
- A 2.5% surcharge is waived on rail ticket bookings through railway counters.
- The maximum waiver amount is capped at Rs. 500 in a calendar year.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

- A 1% fuel surcharge will be waived at all filling stations across India for transactions between Rs. 500 and Rs. 3,000.
- A max capping of Rs. 3,500 per year is applicable on the fuel surcharge waiver.

### Insurance Benefit

Kotak 811 Credit Cardholders get complimentary insurance coverage worth Rs. 50,000 against the loss of their card. The cover is applicable from 7 days prior to the date of reporting the loss of the card to the bank.

## Eligibility Criteria for Kotak 811 Credit Card

To be eligible for Kotak 811 Credit Card, an applicant must satisfy the following eligibility criteria-

- the primary cardholder must be in the age bracket of 18 years and 65 years, and the add-on cardholder must be above 18 years of age.
- the primary and add-on cardholder(s) must be resident(s) of India.

## Conclusion

Kotak 811 Credit Card can be the card of choice for new-to-credit young professionals and millennials as it is an entry-level offering by the bank and does not have very strict eligibility criteria. The card is comparable to its secured variant- the [811 #DreamDifferent Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-dreamdifferent-credit-card/) in terms of the rewards and benefits offered. However, the secured variant comes with a zero annual fee; whereas this unsecured card charges an annual fee of Rs. 500. Along with offering a decent reward rate, the card provides its customers with a lot of other privileges, including complimentary movie tickets as a milestone benefit and many more. With a nominal annual fee and basic features & benefits, this card is an excellent choice for individuals who are new to the world of credit cards and want to start their journey with a basic one. If you have any further doubts regarding this card or its features, please ask us in the comments.

## FAQs:

[Is Kotak 811 Credit Card a secured card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#chck0)


No, this credit card is not a secured one. However, the bank also offers its secured variant which is named Kotak 811 #DreamDifferent Credit Card.


[Is this card lifetime free?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#chck1)


No, the Kotak 811 Credit Card is not lifetime free. It comes with an annual fee of Rs. 500.


[What is the value of 1 Reward Point that I earn using this card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#chck2)


For the Kotak 811 Credit Card, 1 Reward Point = Re. 0.25 for redemption against shopping, e-vouchers, and various other options.


[Do I get complimentary lounge access with the Kotak 811 Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/kotak-811-credit-card/#chck3)


No, you don't get complimentary airport lounge access with the Kotak 811 Credit Card.

# IndianOil Kotak Credit Card

By [Ankur Mittal](https://cardinsider.com/author/ankur/ "Posts by Ankur Mittal")

Updated On
11 September, 2025

Recently, Indian Oil and Kotak Mahindra Bank joined forces to launch their co-branded fuel credit card. The IndianOil Kotak credit card is a nice entry-level card perfect for those who are new to the credit scene and want great fuel-based benefits and other rewards.

IndianOil has more than 34,000 outlets across the country, and cardholders will be rewarded every time they refuel their vehicle at an Indian Oil fuel station. The card also offers reward points on grocery, dining, and other payments. These reward points can be redeemed for free fuel, cashback, or other products and vouchers at KotakRewards.

The IndianOil Kotak credit card can reward you with up to 60 liters of free fuel every year, along with a host of other benefits. Those who travel a lot can reduce the effect of high fuel prices through this card and can have a highly rewarding experience if they prefer IndianOil petrol pumps over other countries. Keep on reading to know more about the fuel credit card by Kotak Mahindra Bank and IndianOil.

## IndianOil Kotak Credit Card

![IndianOil Kotak Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

#### Renewal Fee


Rs. 449 + GST


#### Best Suited For

Fuel \|

#### Reward Type

Reward Points

Get 1000 Reward Points on spending Rs. 500 or more within 30 days of getting the card

[Rewards and Benefits](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/indianoil-kotak-credit-card/#)

#### Movie & Dining

Get 12 Reward Points on spending Rs. 150 on dining. You get 2% back as reward points on your dining spends.


#### Rewards Rate

Get 24 Reward Points on every Rs. 150 spent on fuel. Get 12 Reward Points on every Rs. 150 spent on grocery and dining. Get 3 Reward Points on every Rs. 150 spent on other transactions.


#### Reward Redemption


Reward Points can be redeemed as cashback or to buy merchandise, products, and vouchers on kotakrewards.com. You can redeem your Reward Points for IndianOil XTRAREWARDS as well.


#### Travel


N/A


#### Domestic Lounge Access


N/A


#### International Lounge Access


N/A


#### Golf

N/A


#### Insurance Benefits


Insurance cover worth Rs. 2 Lakhs for personal accident

#### Spend-Based Waiver


Annual fee is waived on spends of Rs. 50,000 or above


#### Rewards Redemption Fee

Nil


#### Foreign Currency Markup


3.5%

#### Interest Rates

3.75% Per Month or 45% Per Annum


#### Fuel Surcharge

1% Waiver on Fuel Spends Between ₹100 and ₹5,000 Only at IndianOil Pumps (Maximum ₹100 Each Statement Cycle)


#### Cash Advance Charges


2.5% of the Cash Withdrawn (Minimum Charge of ₹500)

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/kotak/indianoil-kotak-credit-card/#)

- Welcome Benefit of 1000 Reward Points on spending Rs. 500 inside 30 days
- 24 Reward Points on spending Rs. 150 on fuel at IndianOil outlets
- 12 Reward Points on spending Rs. 150 on dining and grocery
- 3 Reward Points on spending Rs. 150 on everything else
- Annual fee waiver on spends of Rs. 50,000 or more in the previous year
- 1% fuel surcharge waiver with a maximum of Rs. 100 per month
- Insurance cover of Rs. 2 Lakhs for personal accident
- Rupay Contactless payment up to transactions of Rs. 5000

## IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card Features and Benefits

As discussed above, the IndianOil Kotak Bank credit card is a [co-branded fuel card](https://cardinsider.com/best-fuel-credit-cards-india/) offering a nice reward rate on fuel as well as other transactions. Let’s know more about the card’s benefits and features in detail –

### Welcome Benefits

- You can get a welcome benefit of 1000 Reward Points on spending Rs. 500 or more within 30 days of getting the card.
- The benefit is only available to those who pay the joining fee for the credit card.

### Annual Fee Waiver

You can get the annual fee of the credit card waived on making spends of Rs. 50,000 or more in the card anniversary year.

### Reward Accrual

- Get 4% back as reward points on your fuel spends. You can earn 24 reward points on spending every Rs. 150 on fuel, and you can earn a maximum of 1200 reward points per month.
- Get 2% as reward points on dining and grocery spends. You can earn 12 reward points on spending every Rs. 150 on dining and grocery, and you can earn a maximum of 800 reward points every month.
- Get 0.5% as reward points on other spends. You will earn 3 reward points on spending every Rs. 150, and there is no maximum monthly capping on this.
- Also, you get 1000 reward points as your welcome benefit.

### Reward Redemption

- Cardholders can redeem their Reward Points as cashback at the rate of 1 RP = ₹0.20. You must have a minimum of 2,000 Reward Points.
- You can redeem your reward points at Rs 0.25 against Kotak’s exclusive catalog at [Kotakrewards](https://kotakrewards.com/). You can purchase merchandise, products, travel and gift vouchers, mobile recharge, etc.
- You can convert your reward points into IndianOil XTRAREWARDS points only in multiples of 300 through Net or Mobile Banking. 300 RPs = 250 XTRAREWARDS points, where 1 XTRAREWARD point is worth Rs. 0.30. These points can be used to pay for fuel at IndianOil outlets.

### Concierge Services

- You get personal concierge services and assistance for gift delivery, restaurant bookings, flower delivery, car rental, limousine reservation, movie ticket booking, golf reservations, and much more.
- You can call 1800-26-78729 to avail of these concierge services.

### Add-on Card

- You can get an add-on card for free that has all the benefits of your primary card.
- You can set the spending limit on the add-on card as you wish.
- You can separately track the spending on every add-on card.

### Insurance Benefits

You get an insurance cover worth Rs. 2 Lakhs for personal accidents

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

You get a 1% fuel surcharge waiver on fuel transactions between Rs. 100 and Rs. 5000, with a maximum benefit of Rs. 100 every statement cycle.

### Contactless Payment

With Rupay Contactless technology, you can make transactions of up to Rs. 5,000 with your credit card without entering your PIN.

![IndianOil Kotak Credit Card Features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card Fees and Charges

Here are the several fees and charges associated with the IndianOil Kotak Bank credit card–

- The joining and renewal fee of the credit card is ₹449 plus taxes, which can be waived on spending ₹50,000 or more in the previous year.
- The interest rate charged by the card is 3.75% per month, equivalent to 45% per annum.
- The foreign currency markup fee on this credit card is 3.5%.
- There are no charges for obtaining an add-on card; however, a replacement card will incur a fee of ₹100.
- The cash advance charges are 2.5% of the amount withdrawn, subject to a minimum of ₹500.

## IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

You have to pass certain eligibility requirements in order to get approved for the credit card–

- Individuals must be a resident of India.
- Individuals must be between 18 and 65 years of age.
- Individuals must have a stable source of income and other necessary documents.

### Documents Required

You have to submit your ID, address, and income proof when applying for the credit card. Here is a list of essential documents that are required –

- ID Proof – PAN Card
- Address Proof – Aadhar Card, Electricity/Water Bills, Ration Card, Passport, etc.
- Income Proof – Latest salary slips, Form 16, latest bank statements, audited ITR.

## Comparison with Other Co-Branded Fuel Credit Cards

Let’s compare the IndianOil Kotak Bank credit card with two similar co-branded fuel cards – IndianOil HDFC and IndianOil Axis Bank Credit Card.

|     |     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Features** | **IndianOil Kotak Credit Card** | **IndianOil Axis Bank Credit Card** | **IndianOil HDFC Bank Credit Card** |
| **Joining/Annual Fee** | Rs. 449 plus taxes | Rs. 500 plus taxes | Rs. 500 plus taxes |
| **Annual Fee Waiver** | On spending Rs. 50,000 or more in the year | On spending Rs. 50,000 or more in the year | On spending Rs. 50,000 or more in the year |
| **Reward Type** | 1000 reward points on spending Rs. 500 within 30 days of getting the card | 100% cashback up to Rs. 250 on fuel spends within 30 days of card activation | NA |
| **Rewards on Fuel Spends** | 24 reward points on every spend of Rs. 150 | 20 Edge points on every Rs. 100 spent | 5% of purchases as fuel points. You get a maximum of 250 fuel points in the first 6 months and max 150 fuel points after 6 months. |
| **Rewards on Other Spends** | 12 Reward points on Rs. 150 spent on grocery and dining. 3 reward points on every Rs. 150 spent on other categories | 5 Edge points on every Rs. 100 spent on online shopping 1 Edge point on Rs. 100 spent on other categories | 5% as fuel points on bills and grocery payments up to max 100 points per month. 1 fuel point on every Rs. 150 spent on other categories |
| **Other Benefits** | Concierge service and assistance Personal accident insurance worth Rs. 2 Lakhs | IndianOil XTRAREWARDS membership | 10% discount on BookMyShow movie tickets and a 15% discount at partner restaurants with the Dining Delights Program through EazyDiner |

## IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card Review

![Kotak Bank IndianOil Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

For those who love to travel with their vehicle and refuel it regularly, the IndianOil Kotak Bank credit card is a great choice. You get a nice welcome benefit and accelerated reward points on fuel spends at IndianOil outlets. The reward points can be redeemed as cashback on Kotak Rewards or converted to IndianOil XTRAREWARDS points.

The card even offers insurance cover and concierge services to the cardholder. The maximum capping on reward points is a downside, but still, the card has many positive points that outweigh this. You should surely consider applying for the IndianOil Kotak Bank Credit Card if you are new to credit and want a fuel-based card. You have a chance to earn 60 litres of free fuel every year with the card.

# IndusInd Bank Avios Visa Infinite Credit Card

By [Manan Singh](https://cardinsider.com/author/manan/ "Posts by Manan Singh")

Updated On
6 September, 2025

When it comes to private sector financial institutions in the country, one of the first names that pops up in our minds is IndusInd Bank. The bank offers various banking services and has an admirable range of credit cards. The bank has done something unique to offer a reimagined travel experience to its cardholders, as it offers a multi-co-branded travel credit card for customers.

Those who frequently travel to foreign countries must have heard about both Qatar Airways and British Airways. Both these airlines operate some of the best-in-class flights, and their loyalty programs, the Qatar Airways Privilege Club and the British Airways Executive Club, have members throughout the world. With the Avios credit card, you earn Avios, which are the reward points of both airlines’ loyalty programs. It can also be used for exclusive perks and benefits on your travels, especially international flights. The Avios credit card is available in the Visa Infinite variant. When you apply for it, you can choose any one of the two airline loyalty programs and your preferred destination to earn maximum Avios points and other benefits and privileges.

- Travel-related insurance benefits and 1% fuel surcharge waiver.

## Reward Earning as Avios

The Avios Visa Infinite credit card offers Avios as reward points that can be used at both British Airways and Qatar Airways.

- 6X Avios/₹200 for Point of Sale transactions at preferred destinations.
- 5X Avios/₹200 spent on the Qatar Airways and British Airways website or mobile app.
- 3X Avios/₹200 spent on other domestic and international spends, no matter which airline program you opt for.
- 1X Avios/₹200 for utilities, government, and insurance spends.
- No points shall be earned for fuel transactions.

_Preferred destinations can be selected while applying for the card and can be changed at every renewal. Check the complete list of preferred destinations available_ [_here_](https://www.indusind.bank.in/content/dam/indusind-corporate/avios-visa-infinite-credit-card/Destination-Country-List.pdf) _._

### Reward Redemption

The Avios earned by you can be redeemed against a host of different options at Qatar Airways or British Airways.

- **For Qatar Airways**, you can spend the Avios earned by you for award flights and upgrades, to buy gifts, or transfer Avios at the Qatar Duty Free, for partner airlines award flights, to book flights with Cash + Avios, and more.
- **For British Airways**, you can spend the Avios earned by you for reward flights, class upgrades, booking British Airways Holidays, booking flights with Cash + Miles, booking hotel stays, car rentals, travel experiences, and more.

![IndusInd Bank Avios Visa Infinite Credit Card Benefits](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## Travel Benefits

The IndusInd Avios Visa Infinite credit card offers the following travel benefits to cardholders.

### Airport Meet and Greet

Getting extra care when you are traveling, especially on long flights, is a great experience. The card offers two complimentary Airport Meet and Greet services every year at top airports for the cardholder. Both the primary cardholder and his companion get the service. The services include –

- Dedicated 1-to-1 service
- Meet and greet when you arrive at the airport terminal
- A guide to accompany you throughout the airport for proper security and seamless immigration
- Assistance with baggage and security
- Porter service for a maximum of 3 bags

### Priority Pass Program

The Avios credit card offers a complimentary [Priority Pass](https://cardinsider.com/blog/priority-pass-program-guide/) membership to the cardholder that offers access to international airport lounges worldwide. The cardholder gets two lounge access per quarter through the Priority Pass.

### Domestic Lounge Access

With the card, the cardholder gets two complimentary domestic lounge access per quarter at participating lounges. You get to relax at the lounge and enjoy gourmet meals, Free WiFi, entertainment, and more.

### Airline Benefits

For cardholders who choose the Qatar Airways Privilege Club program, they get an exclusive 10% discount on booking flights via the Qatar Airways app or website. Cardholders get the discount on all flights departing from India, and they need to use the code ‘IDSCB23’ to get the benefit.

### Insurance Benefits

The Avios credit card offers overseas medical insurance worth $25,000, and air accident insurance cover of ₹25 lakh to the cardholder. Other than this, IndusInd has partnered with Oriental Insurance Company to offer the following coverage –

- Lost baggage – ₹1 Lakh
- Delayed baggage – ₹25,000
- Loss of passport – ₹50,000
- Lost ticket or missed flight connection – ₹25,000

### Concierge Service

The Avios credit card offers [concierge service](https://cardinsider.com/blog/what-are-credit-card-concierge-services/) to make sure everything is planned properly for you during your travel, and you can have a nice time.

### Elderly Care Assist

While you are out traveling, the concierge service takes care of the elderly people in your family back in India. They help with home care, OPD appointments, healthcare packages, diagnostic test bookings, teleconsultation appointments, home quarantine, physiotherapy assistance, consultation with wellness experts, dieticians, nutritionists, etc.

### International Events

The concierge service will help you visit iconic events and plan the entire itinerary for you. They will arrange tickets for sold-out events and hospitality events. You can visit football matches, tennis tournaments, motor racing events, fashion events, music festivals and concerts, art exhibitions, etc.

### Other Services

The concierge services will help and assist you with other things, including –

- Pre-trip assistance and planning
- Gift and flower delivery assistance
- Hotel referral and reservation bookings
- Dining referrals and reservation bookings

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

You get a 1% fuel surcharge waiver on transactions between ₹500 and ₹10,000 at fuel stations across the country.

### Total Protect

The Total Protect program will protect you against fraudulent activity on your card up to your credit limit, and it also protects the add-on cards. You get protection against unauthorized transactions, counterfeit cards, and fraudulent use in case of a stolen card.

## IndusInd Bank Avios Credit Card Other Features

With the Avios credit card, the cardholder gets access to many unique experiences that take the overall travel experience to a whole new level. The luxury, relaxation, and comfort offered by both Qatar Airways and British Airways join forces with the convenience and trust offered by IndusInd Bank on the Avios Visa Infinite credit card.

With this credit card, your travels and journeys will surely be unforgettable and the best you can imagine. As it is a multi-brand card, you can choose to opt for either the Qatar Airways or British Airways program, and each of them has different welcome, renewal, and other benefits. Let’s know more about what the IndusInd Bank Avios credit card has to offer –

### Welcome Benefits

When you get the IndusInd Bank Avios Infinite credit card, you get access to world-class adventures and travel. The card onboards with a bonus of 20,000 Avios.

### Milestone Benefits

The Avios Visa Infinite credit card has amazing milestone benefits on offer for cardholders. No matter the airline program chosen by you, you get the following milestone benefits –

- Bonus 18,000 Avios on spending ₹8 Lakhs or more in the year
- Bonus 18,000 Avios more on a second spending of ₹8 Lakhs in the year
- The maximum amount of Avios you can earn in a year through milestone benefits is 36,000, and the bonus Avios is credited within 10 days after card statement generation once you achieve the milestone spend.

### Renewal Benefits

Bonus 5,000 Avios that are credited within 15 days of paying the renewal fee.

## IndusInd Bank Avios Visa Infinite Credit Card – Fees & Charges

The following are the fees associated with the credit card –

- The credit card comes at a joining fee of ₹10,000 and an annual fee of ₹5,000 that has to be paid after 12 months of card issuance.
- The interest rate on the card is 3% per month or 36% per annum.
- The cash advance fee on the credit card is waived.
- The forex markup fee on the card is 3.5% everywhere and 1.5% at preferred international destinations.

## Bottom Line

Frequent travelers and those who are looking for a nice travel credit card should consider the IndusInd Bank Avios Visa Infinite credit card. IndusInd Bank has partnered with both British Airways and Qatar Airways to launch this multi-brand credit card. This card works on the Visa Infinite network and offers Avios as reward points, which is the reward currency for both Qatar and British Airways’ loyalty programs.

The cardholder can earn up to 6X Avios/₹200 spent for POS transactions and earn 5X Avios/₹200 for British Airways and Qatar Airways bookings. If you regularly make foreign currency spends, then you can benefit from the low 1.5% forex markup fee at the preferred destinations. Moreover, the card is packed with travel benefits, including meet and greet service at airports, complimentary international as well as domestic lounge access, concierge services, discounts on flight bookings, and more.

Even though the card has a high joining fee and annual fee, it still offers many great privileges for people who travel frequently, especially on international flights through British Airways and Qatar Airways.

The IndusInd Bank Avios Credit Card has now become valuable for many ever since its revaluation in April 2025. This is a must-have card for those who often travel across the globe. Do let us know your thoughts on this multi-brand travel credit card by IndusInd Bank, and if you plan on applying for it.

IndusInd Bank has launched its first RuPay Credit Card. This unique card will offer you 2 Reward Points for every Rs. 100 spent on UPI transactions. There has been a steady rise in banks offering [RuPay Credit Cards](https://cardinsider.com/rupay-credit-cards-for-upi-payments-in-india/). This is mainly because of the increasing popularity of the RuPay network and the ease of linking it with the UPI application of your choice.

Introducing the Platinum RuPay Credit Card – a credit card that is free for life and is bound to pique the interest of both veteran and first-time cardholders. This card is particularly beneficial for those who frequently make transactions through their UPI apps, such as Google Pay, PhonePe, and Paytm.

Earn rewards on every spend with the IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card. Read on for benefits and review.

## IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card Rewards

- You will get 2 Reward Points on every Rs. 100 spent with UPI.
- You shall receive 1 Reward Point for every non-UPI transaction of Rs. 100.
- You will also earn Rs. 0.70 for all eligible spends. This is valid for both UPI and non-UPI transactions.
- **You shall only earn Reward Points up to your given credit limit.**
- No Reward Points shall be awarded for utility bills, government services, insurance premiums, education fees, spends on fuel, and rental payments.

### Redemption of Reward Points

You can redeem your Reward Points earned with the Platinum RuPay Credit Card for the following.

- Earn instant discounts, cashback offers, and a lot more on popular brands with [IndusMoments](https://www.indusmoments.com/).
- Redeem your reward points for KrisFlyer miles. 400 Reward Points = 100 KrisFlyer miles.
- You can use your reward points to redeem their cash value and pay off your outstanding credit card. 1 Reward Point = Rs. 0.35.

## How to Apply for an IndusInd Platinum RuPay Credit Card?

The application process for IndusInd Platinum RuPay Credit Card is fairly easy. You just have to follow these steps.

- Click on Apply Now,
- You shall then be redirected to the official bank website,
- Fill in your particulars and
- Submit your application for the new card.

## How to Link Your Platinum RuPay Credit Card With UPI?

Quickly link your Platinum RuPay Credit Card on UPI in a few steps given below.

- Open your UPI app\* BHIM, PhonePe, Paytm, or Google Pay.
- Select the option “Add Credit Card/Link Credit Card”
- Select Credit Card Issuer Bank (IndusInd Bank)
- Select your IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card
- Proceed to generate UPI PIN

## IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card Review

The IndusInd Bank Platinum RuPay Credit Card is most suited for people who make regular payments through UPI. Whether it’s groceries or dining, you can earn 2 Reward Points on every spend of Rs. 100 with the UPI application of your choice. Not only this, transactions not made through UPI shall also get 1 Reward Point for every Rs. 100 spent.

Although this card offers reasonable rewards for UPI payments, there are a few other benefits associated with it. This entry-level credit card is designed for beginners or first-time cardholders to build their [credit score](https://cardinsider.com/resources/what-is-credit-score-complete-guide/) and adjust to using a credit card.

IndusInd Bank, in collaboration with EazyDiner, a popular online dining reservation platform, has introduced one of the most rewarding dining credit cards in India. The EazyDiner IndusInd Signature Credit Card carries a joining and annual renewal fee of ₹2,999 and welcomes cardholders with 2,000 bonus EazyPoints, a Postcard Hotels stay voucher worth ₹7,500, and a complimentary EazyDiner Prime membership worth ₹3,550, which automatically gets renewed yearly.

Cardholders can enjoy dining benefits of up to ₹60,000 at premium and fine dining restaurants with EazyDiner. They will also receive a decent reward rate on every category spent and accelerated points for dining transactions made via the EazyDiner app. In addition to this, cardholders receive up to 50% extra discount on dining-related expenses via EazyDiner, 3X EazyPoints on all orders, and numerous other dining benefits.

## EazyDiner IndusInd Signature Credit Card Features and Benefits

The EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card is a co-branded card that offers exciting rewards rates and various benefits across multiple categories. Below are the detailed benefits & features of this credit card:

### Welcome Benefits

- Get 2,000 EazyPoints after the joining fee has been successfully paid.
- Get a complimentary Eazy Diner Prime membership for a year worth ₹3,550. After the annual fee is paid, the membership can be renewed annually.
- Postcard Hotels stay voucher worth ₹7,500. The offer is applicable for stays booked directly, either through the [Postcard Hotels](https://www.postcardresorts.com/) website or the reservations concierge at +91 79995 55222.

### Dining Benefits

Being a credit card targeted at people who dine out very often, the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card comes with several exclusive dining privileges as follows:

- Enjoy a 25% discount on your dining bills when you pay via Payeazy on EazyDiner.
- An additional 25% upto ₹1,000 off on paying bills using the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card via PayEazy on the EazyDiner app. The extra 25% off is capped at ₹2,000 each month without any spend-based conditions. Those who wish to access additional discounts of ₹5,000 can do so by making non-dining spends of ₹30,000 or more each month.
- Enjoy a free premium alcoholic beverage for each guest at selected restaurants nationwide. This offer is available at specific restaurants listed on the EazyDiner App. You can identify these restaurants by looking for the “Free alcoholic beverage” deal text on the restaurant listing page in the app.

### Renewal Benefits

The EazyDiner Prime membership is renewed annually upon payment of the annual fees. Cardholders shall also receive a Postcard Hotels voucher worth ₹7,500.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

With this card, you can get a 1% waiver on the surcharge applicable on fuel purchases between ₹500 and ₹3,000. This waiver is capped at ₹250 per statement cycle.

## EazyDiner IndusInd Credit Card Reward Points

The reward rate of the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card in different categories is mentioned below:

- You get **10 Reward Points/₹100 spent on hotels, travel, entertainment, and shopping**.
- You get **4 Reward Points/₹100 on all other eligible retail spend**.
- No Reward Points shall be awarded for dining spends.
- Other than the Reward points, you shall also earn 3X EazyPoints on making payments via the EazyDiner IndusInd Card.
- Reward Points shall only be earned up to the assigned credit limit of each cardholder.

**1 Reward Point = ₹0.10**

### Reward Redemption

The reward points accumulated via the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card are redeemable against dining-related spending on the EazyDiner app. You can earn Reward Points using the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card, and these points can be instantly redeemed against your dining bill. However, the instant redemption of Reward Points is only possible if you make the payment via PayEazy on the EazyDiner app. When making the payment, you have the option to choose the number of Reward Points you would like to redeem.

_Please note that the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card must be used to make the balance payment while redeeming the Reward Points. These reward points can not be redeemed against cash credit, and they expire one year after the accumulation date._

## EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card Fees and Charges

The following are the essential charges applicable to the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Card:

- The joining and renewal fee applicable to the card is ₹2,999 plus applicable taxes.
- The finance charges applicable to the IndusInd Bank EazyDiner Credit Card are 3.83% per month.
- The foreign currency markup fee applicable to the card is 3.5% of the transaction amount.
- The cash advance fee applicable on the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Card is 2.5% of the amount withdrawn (minimum of ₹300).

## Eligibility for EazyDiner IndusInd Signature Credit Card

The eligibility criteria to get approved for the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card have been mentioned below:

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Particular** | **Criteria** |
| **Age** | Above 21 |
| **Income** | Stable Income as a Salaried or Self-Employed Individual |
| **Credit Score** | Decent Credit Score (750 or Above) |

## How to Apply for the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card?

You can apply for the IndusInd Bank EazyDiner Credit Card online or offline. If you prefer offline methods, simply visit a bank branch in your city and fill in the application form. If you prefer online methods, follow the steps below to apply for the card:

- Click on the Apply Now option.
- You shall be redirected to the official application page.
- Fill in your particulars.
- Please proceed to apply as instructed on the website.

## Bottom Line

While several credit cards offer high discounts and cashback on ordering food online, like the [Swiggy HDFC Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/swiggy-hdfc-bank-credit-card/), the EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card is an excellent choice for those who frequently dine out.

![IndusInd EazyDiner Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

The renewal fee appears to be relatively high, but considering all the benefits of this card, it is worth paying a fee of ₹2,999 for such an exclusive credit card. The EazyDiner Prime membership you get for free with this credit card is worth ₹3,550. With this credit card, you earn accelerated rewards on travel spending, additional discounts of up to 50%, and many more benefits.

The HDFC [Regalia Gold](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/) Credit Card is also a lifestyle card that offers exciting dining benefits, but the EazyDiner co-branded card, issued by IndusInd Bank, remains the best option for foodies.

_Those looking for a dining credit card with similar benefits and no joining or annual charges should consider the_ [_EazyDiner IndusInd Platinum_](https://cardinsider.com/indusind-bank/eazydiner-indusind-bank-platinum-credit-card/) _Credit Card. This is our EazyDiner IndusInd Bank Credit Card review. Drop your views on the card in the comments below._

# IDFC FIRST Millennia Credit Card

By [Ankur Mittal](https://cardinsider.com/author/ankur/ "Posts by Ankur Mittal")

Updated On
28 July, 2025

If you are looking for an entry-level card, IDFC’s FIRST Millennia credit card is the right choice. This is a reward points-based credit card with a decent rewards program. It offers up to 10x non-expiring Reward points, which can be redeemed at any point in time. This card provides reward points for online as well as offline spending. Moreover, if someone is fond of traveling via trains, then this card offers complimentary Railway Lounge access four times a quarter, making your traveling experience a lot more luxurious.

Other card benefits include a discount of up to 25% on movies, up to 20% off on dining, [complimentary roadside assistance](https://cardinsider.com/best-credit-cards-for-complimentary-roadside-assistance/), and many more. Plus, it is a lifetime free credit card, i.e., no annual fee credit card, which means you don’t have to pay a single penny to get and use this card. Read on to learn more about the IDFC FIRST Millennia Credit Card:

#### Pros

- With this card, you get complimentary railway lounge access, which is highly beneficial for domestic travelers who prefer to travel by railway.
- This card has a great advantage as it is a lifetime free card.

#### Cons

- You won’t be provided with any domestic or international airport lounge access with the IDFC First Millennia Credit Card.
- There is a reward redemption fee of ₹99 with this card.

## IDFC First Millennia Credit Card Rewards

- The card offers **10X Reward Points** after achieving **incremental spends of ₹20,000** or more in a month. You get **3X Reward Points until a spend of ₹20,000** is reached.
- **1X Reward Points for insurance and utility spending**.
- Earn **50 Reward Points/₹150 for hotels and 20 Reward Points/₹150 for flight bookings** via the IDFC Travel & Shop portal. A maximum of 8,000 accelerated Reward Points can be earned each month.
- These RPs are redeemable at any point in time as they don’t expire and get accumulated for future use.
- You cannot earn reward points on the purchase of fuel or Insurance policies, EMI, or Cash Withdrawals.

Note: 1X= 1 Reward Point/₹150 spent.

### Reward Redemption

- Redemption of reward points earned using this credit card is simply a breeze. You can redeem the reward points against various categories & products. You have to pay a low amount of ₹99 plus taxes for reward redemption.
- The Reward points are being calculated as follows-


1 RP= ₹0.25

## IDFC First Millennia Card Features and Benefits

The IDFC First Millennia Credit Card is targeted at young individuals who love to spend on shopping, entertainment, dining, etc. The following are some of the important features and advantages of this credit card:

### Welcome Benefits

- IDFC First Millennia credit card comes with a host of welcome benefits for the buyer. You get an e-gift voucher worth ₹500 for spending ₹5,000 or more within 30 days of issuance of this credit card.
- You also get a cashback of 5% (maximum ₹1,000) on your first EMI transaction if done within the first month of card issuance.

### Movie Offers

IDFC First Millennia credit card offers a 25% discount on movie tickets (up to ₹100) on the District app once every month so that you can watch your favorite heroes in action on big screens.

### Dining Benefits

You can avail of up to 20% discount at more than 1,500 leading restaurants across India and relish your favorite delicacies without any worries.

### Free Railway Lounge Access

If you are a frequent train traveler, we have great news for you. You can enjoy [complimentary access to Railway Lounges](https://cardinsider.com/best-credit-card-for-complimentary-railway-lounge-access/) up to 4 times per quarter with the IDFC FIRST Millennia Credit Card. These lounges offer a luxurious and comfortable experience similar to that of high-end hotels. You can now wait for your rides without any hassle and enjoy a sense of luxury.

Here are some of the IRCTC Executive Lounges you can access with your IDFC FIRST Millennia Credit Card-

- Agra Cantt Railway Station
- Ahmedabad Kalupur Railway Station
- Chennai Central Railway Station
- Old Delhi Railway Station
- Kolkata (Sealdah) Railway Station
- New Delhi Railway Station
- Varanasi Cantt Railway Station
- Jaipur Railway Station

You must present your valid card along with your train ticket to avail of this offer.

### Insurance Benefits

- If you are a user of this card, then you are entitled to get a Personal Accident Cover worth ₹2 Lakhs, which will be active in case of an accident or similar mishap.
- Also, if you lose this card, your card will be equipped with a complimentary lost card liability cover of ₹25,000. Both these are applicable only if this card is being used or is active.

For the issuance of the IDFC First Millennia Credit Card, there are certain basic eligibility criteria that need to be fulfilled-

- The applicant should be at least 21 years old.
- Annual income should be more than ₹3 Lakh.
- The applicant should have a decent credit score with no payment defaults (Preferably above 700).

## IDFC First Millennia Credit Card Review

Along with being a highly affordable card, the IDFC FIRST Millennia credit card is one of the most trending reward cards in the market one can opt for. It has a unique reward point system where the reward points don’t expire and are carried forward. Besides this, it offers a host of benefits like travel, lifestyle, insurance covers, fuel surcharge charge waiver, etc. This card is best suited for people who are searching for a lifetime free credit card with no joining fee and want reward points on every online and offline spend. You won’t regret buying this smart and value-for-money credit card. ## FAQs:

[Where can I view and redeem my accrued Reward Points ?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/idfc-first-millennia-card/#chck0)


You can view and redeem your accrued Reward Points from IDFC First App / Net banking by clicking on HAVE tab -> Then select CARDS tab. Here you can view your accrued Reward points and redeem them by clicking on REDEEM NOW button.


[Will I earn Reward Point on every purchase or spend I do ?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/idfc-first-millennia-card/#chck1)


Yes, you will earn a reward on every purchase or spend except Cash Withdrawals, Fuel, EMI and Insurance purchase transactions.


[Is there any capping or threshold limit on the Reward Points earned ?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/idfc-first-millennia-card/#chck2)


No, there is no limit or capping on the Reward Points you earn. You will earn Reward Points on every purchase.


[Can I use my Reward Points whenever I want ?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/idfc-first-millennia-card/#chck3)


Yes. With IDFC First Millennia credit card, you can use your Reward Points whenever you want as they won’t get expired and would be accumulated for future use if you don’t use them.


[Does this credit card provides Travel and Dine-In benefits to its users ?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/idfc-first-millennia-card/#chck4)


Yes, this card provides both Travel and Dine-In benefits: **TRAVEL:** You will get complimentary Railway Lounge access four times in a quarter. **DINE-IN:** You can avail of up to 20% discount at more than 1500 leading restaurants Pan India.


[Does this credit card provides Cash Withdrawals from the ATM ?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/idfc-first-millennia-card/#chck5)


Yes. With IDFC First Millennia credit card, you can enjoy interest-free cash withdrawals from the ATM for 48 days.

# IDFC First Bank Mayura Credit Card

By [Kartik Kanwar](https://cardinsider.com/author/kartik/ "Posts by Kartik Kanwar")

Updated On
1 September, 2025

_Mayura Credit Card is IDFC First Bank’s latest premium card. It is suited for the premium segment and can be considered the costlier and more rewarding version of its counterpart, the_ [_Ashva_](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/ashva-credit-card/) _Card. It weighs 17 grams and displays a magnificent peacock design. With the heavyweight ornate design, this card truly offers a super premium feel and look._

The Mayura Credit Card is ideal for travelers, offering access to international and domestic airport lounges. It even offers one free guest visit to domestic airport lounges. Those who travel globally and make international transactions will be pleased to know that this card offers zero Foreign Exchange charges, making it particularly suitable for foreign transactions. Additionally, those who opt for this card can earn up to 3.33% rewards for international transactions.

Other than travel benefits, cardholders can earn up to 10X Reward Points on making incremental spends of ₹20,000 or more. Additionally, this card also offers rewards for rent, education, insurance, and utilities. Most cards exclude these categories for reward earning. Keep reading to learn more about this card.

IDFC Mayura Credit Card is available on the Mastercard World and Visa Infinite network variants. The joining fee shall be charged upfront. Upon successful payment, the Mayura Credit Card will be dispatched.

## IDFC First Bank Mayura Credit Card

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/mayura-credit-card/#)

- The Mayura Credit Card is a premium credit card that comes at a fee of ₹5,999.
- This card offers 10X Reward Points on incremental spends of ₹20,000 or above.
- Cardholders can access both international and domestic airport lounges with this card.
- Availing this card is relatively easy; one can also apply for this on a card-to-card basis.

[Pros/Cons](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/mayura-credit-card/#)

#### Pros

- This is a highly beneficial credit card for those who often travel and are seeking a decent card with airport lounge access.
- There is a Trip Cancellation Cover worth ₹50,000 with the Mayura Card.

#### Cons

- The reward rate could have been better, but it’s the same as that of the Ashva Credit Card.
- There are no worthwhile renewal benefits or spend-based annual fee waivers with this card. Making it rewarding only for the first year.

## Welcome Benefits

Until August 31, 2025, cardholders must have completed four transactions of ₹1,000 or more within 60 days of card issuance to receive ₹1,000 cashback per transaction. The cashback was capped at a maximum of ₹4,000.

From 1st September 2025, the joining benefits have been revised and will now be awarded across three statement cycles:

- Earn 4,000 Reward Points with the payment of the joining fee.
- Earn 4,000 Reward Points on spends over ₹20,000 in the second statement cycle.
- Earn another 4,000 Reward Points on spends over ₹20,000 in the third statement cycle as well.
- This revised structure applies to cards generated on or after September 1, 2025.

_The joining fee payment benefit on the Mayura Credit Card is offered only once per customer. If you have previously held this card, you will not be eligible to receive this benefit again._

## Reward Point Earning

- **10X Reward Points/₹150** on **incremental spends above ₹20,000** in a monthly statement cycle.
- **5X/₹150 Reward Points** on **spends up to ₹20,000** in a monthly statement cycle.
- With the Mayura Credit Card, you can earn rewards even in restricted categories. Cardholders are eligible to earn **3X/₹150 Reward Points on rent, government, wallet load, and education, and 1X/₹150 Reward Points on insurance and utilities.**
- The Reward Points earned with the Mayura Credit Card do not expire.

Which categories are exempt from reward point earning?

- Fuel
- Transactions Converted into EMI
- Fees or Charges
- Loan on Card
- Balance Transfer
- Cash Withdrawal Transactions

**_Value of One Reward Point = ₹0.50 for Travel Bookings Via IDFC Travel & Shop, ₹0.25 Everywhere Else_**

### Accelerated Reward Points for Travel Bookings

IDFC Mayura Credit Cardholders can earn accelerated Reward Points on travel bookings made via the IDFC Travel & Shop portal on the IDFC Mobile Banking app. These points are earned over and above the base reward rate. A maximum of 25,000 Bonus Reward Points can be earned each month.

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Redemption** | **Bonus Reward Points** | **Reward Rate** |
| **Hotels** | 50 Bonus Reward Points/₹150 | 16.66% |
| **Flights** | 30 Bonus Reward Points/₹150 | 10% |

Cardholders can use their points to cover up to 70% of the total booking value, including taxes and fees. The remaining amount must be paid using the Mayura Credit Card, which is linked to the Reward Points being used for hotel or flight bookings through the “Travel & Shop” section of the IDFC FIRST Bank Mobile App.

Please note that you can redeem a maximum of 300,000 Reward Points each year.

## “Buy One Get One” Free on Movies

Mayura cardholders can get a “Buy One Get One” free offer on the second movie ticket they book on District by Zomato. The maximum discount for each movie ticket is ₹500, and this offer can be availed twice each month. This benefit could help save up to ₹1,000 per month and ₹12,000 per year.

## Free Trip Cancellation Cover

One of the most advantageous benefits offered with the Mayura Credit Card is the **Cancel For Any Reason (CFAR) Insurance**. Cardholders who have made non-refundable flight/hotel bookings with their Mayura Cards can avail of the Cancel For Any Reason (CFAR) Insurance for reimbursement of up to ₹50,000 twice a year. This cover is available to both primary and add-on credit cardholders who, for any reason, wish to cancel their booking 24 hours before the scheduled time. Whether in any unforeseeable circumstance or emergency, you can easily cancel your plans without losing money on trip bookings with the Mayura Credit Card. Check the complete claims term [here](https://www.idfcfirstbank.com/content/dam/idfcfirstbank/pdf/Mayura/docs/CFAR.pdf).

![IDFC Mayura Credit Card Features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## Free Airport Lounge Access

Those who wish to access airport lounges with a Mayura Credit Card would need to spend  ₹20,000 in the current month to be eligible for it in the next.

### Domestic Airports

Four complimentary airport lounge visits each quarter across India. This would mean that cardholders can visit airport lounges across the nation sixteen times a year. Above this, the Mayura Credit Card also offers one additional visit for guests at domestic airports. This is an excellent benefit in case there is a family member or a close one without a credit card with complimentary airport lounge access.

### International Airports

Mayura Credit Card offers four free international lounge accesses each quarter, a total of sixteen of which are available each year.

## Complimentary Railway Lounge Access

Those who often travel by Indian railways will be glad to know that this card offers four complimentary railway lounge accesses across the country. This would allow cardholders to rest and rejuvenate at railway lounges. The maximum number of railway lounge access visits allowed is 16 each year. Starting on 20th February 2025, this benefit shall be unlocked after spending ₹20,000 or more in the previous month.

## Flat ₹5,000 Off on Postcard Hotels & Resorts

With your Mayura Credit Card, you can enjoy a flat discount of ₹5,000 on stays at The Postcard’s luxury hotels and resorts in India on a minimum booking of ₹25,000. This offer can be used once every calendar quarter by applying the **promo code IDFCVIP**, and is valid until 10th February 2027. Check complete details [here](https://www.idfcfirstbank.com/content/dam/idfcfirstbank/pdf/Postcard-Hotel-Ashva-and-Mayura.pdf).

![Postcard Hotels Mayura Discount Offer](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## Annual Benefit

Spend a minimum of ₹8 Lakhs in the previous card anniversary year to get 7,500 Reward Points (worth up to ₹3,750), or spend a minimum of ₹15 Lakhs in the previous card anniversary year to get 15,000 Reward Points (worth up to ₹7,500).

_Keep in mind that cardholders can only avail themselves of just one of these benefits._

## Mayura Credit Card Review

The Mayura Credit Card comes with a hefty fee of ₹5,999. Although it offers welcome benefits worth 12,000 Reward Points, they don’t fully compensate for the fees, which would discourage many from applying for the card. The card would be most suitable for those with high spending who can take advantage of its various benefits, such as airport/railway lounge access, golfing privileges, and buy one get one free offer on movies. One of the main aspects of spending and earning rewards with the Mayura Credit Card is that one has to spend at least ₹20,000 each month to be eligible for a higher reward rate.

If you travel abroad often and make international purchases, you should definitely opt for this card. It provides plenty of airport lounge access and charges zero foreign exchange charges on global purchases.

![IDFC Mayura Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## FAQs:

[What movie offers are available with the Mayura Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/mayura-credit-card/#chck0)


Cardholders get ₹500 off on the purchase of a second movie ticket made through District by Zomato. This benefit can be availed twice each month, offering savings of ₹1,000 each month.


[Why do I need to pay the Mayura Credit Card fee before I receive the card?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/mayura-credit-card/#chck1)


Usually, the joining fee is attached to the first credit card statement, but with the IDFC Mayura and Ashva Credit Card, you must pay the joining fee upfront to avoid any unnecessary card dispatch.


[Can I apply for the Mayura Credit Card on a C2C basis?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/mayura-credit-card/#chck2)


Yes, the Mayura Credit Card can be applied for using the card 2 card method. Simply provide your other bank card number, card issuer details, and limit when applying for the Mayura Credit Card. This makes the application approval process extremely easy.


[What is the joining fee for the Mayura Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/mayura-credit-card/#chck3)


The Mayura Credit Card comes at a joining fee of ₹5,999 + GST.


[Does this card offer any renewal benefits?](https://cardinsider.com/idfc-first-bank/mayura-credit-card/#chck4)


No, there aren't any renewal benefits, although cardholders can get annual milestone benefits on reaching designated spends of ₹8 and ₹15 lakh.

For the very first time, IDFC First Bank has launched a co-branded credit card. IDFC First Bank launched two cards in collaboration with Hindustan Petroleum Corporation Limited. The IDFC Bank HPCL First Power Plus Credit Card helps you save up to ₹18,500 annually, which roughly translates to around 175+ liters of fuel, equivalent to free fuel.

This credit card offers numerous benefits beyond fuel savings at HPCL, including a welcome gift worth ₹1,500. The joining fee is ₹499. This card offers impressive lifestyle privileges, including complimentary domestic airport lounge access, up to 25% off movie tickets, complimentary roadside assistance, and more.

IDFC Bank HPCL First Power Plus Credit Card also helps you avail of fantastic deals and discounts across a wide range of popular partnered brands. The interest rate for these cards ranges from 0.75% to 3.5% per month, equivalent to 9% to 42% annually. ## IDFC HPCL FIRST Power Credit Card – Features and Benefits

Here are the main key features and benefits provided by the IDFC First Bank –

### Welcome Benefits

- You will get ₹500 gift voucher on your 1st HPCL fuel transaction above ₹500
- Get up to 5% cashback (up to ₹1,000 capped) on the transaction value of the first EMI.

### Movie and Dining

Up to 25% off on movie tickets up to ₹100 once per month on District by Zomato.

### Domestic Lounge Access

Complimentary domestic airport lounge access once every quarter on a minimum monthly spending of ₹20,000.

### UPI

The IDFC First Bank HPCL Power Plus Credit Card is now available on the [RuPay](https://www.rupay.co.in/) network. If you opt for the RuPay version of the First Power Plus Credit Card, you will be able to link your card to any UPI application of your choice, such as Google Pay, PhonePe, Cred, and Paytm. When you make UPI transactions using this card, you will earn 3X Reward Points across all categories except Fuel, Insurance, and EMI. There is no limit to the number of UPI Reward Points you can earn.

### Insurance Benefits

- The benefit of a personal accident loan is ₹2 lakh.
- Card liability is covered for ₹25,000 on lost cards.
- Complimentary roadside assistance worth ₹1,399 four times a year.

## Rewards Rate With IDFC First Power Plus Credit Card

- 30X Reward Points on HPCL Fuel up to 2,400 Reward Points (₹600) in a statement cycle. Check the official [IDFC First savings maximizer](https://www.idfcfirstbank.com/content/dam/idfcfirstbank/pdf/credit-card/Savings-Maximiser-FIRST-Powerplus-001.pdf) document.
- Save up to 5% as rewards on grocery and utility expenses of up to ₹2,000 per month.
- You can save up to 5% as a reward on IDFC FIRST FASTag recharge (max cap ₹1,000 per month). For IDFC FIRST FASTag recharge, you will get 30 rewards for every ₹150 spent.
- Get up to 3X rewards on all other retail transactions. Here, 3X rewards are equal to 3 reward points for every ₹150 spent.
- Rewards are not applicable to HPCL fuel and LPG expenses beyond the maximum monthly capped amount.

_(1 Reward Point = ₹0.25)_

IDFC Bank HPCL First Power Plus Credit Card is one of the first credit cards by IDFC First Bank to come into existence in collaboration with HPCL. One of the main benefits of this card is that it rewards customers for regular refuels based on their usage.

This card is well-suited for fuel use, but it also offers other benefits, proving that it can help save money through everyday shopping. For frequent travelers and people who refill their tanks, this credit card is a perfect approach to saving money. This card not only saves fuel but also offers numerous benefits for movie rentals and even rental cars. The IDFC HPCL Credit Card also offers a luxurious lifestyle benefit of complimentary lounge access at no extra cost.

# RBL Platinum Delight Credit Card

By [Bharti Bisht](https://cardinsider.com/author/bharti/ "Posts by Bharti Bisht")

Updated On
1 July, 2025

The RBL Platinum Delight credit card is a rewards card that comes with exciting reward offers on a variety of purchases. It is best suited for people looking for a credit card that allows them to save on all their expenses. With a joining fee of Rs. 1,000, you get 4,000 reward points as a welcome gift. The card gives you an opportunity to earn bonus Reward Points every month on the basis of your monthly spends. With your RBL Platinum Delight credit card, you can earn 2 Reward Points on every Rs. 100 spent on all purchases and 2x Reward Points on weekends to make them even more delightful.

These reward points can be redeemed against a variety of options through their dedicated website for rewards. Moreover, you can make the card absolutely free for yourself by getting the annual fee waived on the basis of your spends. To know more details about the RBL Platinum Delight credit card, its benefits, and fees & charges, keep reading:

## RBL Platinum Delight Credit Card Features

### Welcome Benefits

- The RBL Platinum Delight Credit Card provides you with a welcome benefit of 4,000 Reward Points if you swipe your credit card for a valid retail spend within 30 days of receiving it.
- The offer can be availed after paying the membership fee of Rs. 1,000.
- The information regarding the welcome bonus will be communicated via SMS or mail at your registered phone number or email ID within 60 days of card issuance.

### RBL Platinum Delight Credit Card Rewards

- For every Rs. 100 spent, you get 2 Reward Points on all purchases on weekdays.
- You earn 2x Reward points, i.e., 4 Reward Points for every Rs. 100 spent on weekends (Saturdays & Sundays).
- The maximum limit capped for reward accumulation in a month is 1,000 reward points.
- No Reward Points will be earned for fuel, railways, utilities, insurance, wallet reloads, rentals, and miscellaneous payments.

### Rewards Redemption

- The Reward Points you earn can be redeemed through the dedicated website – [RBL Rewards](https://www.rblrewards.com/).
- 1 Reward Point = 0.25 for redemption against hotel/flight bookings, mobile recharges shopping vouchers, and other lifestyle products.
- To redeem the reward points of RBL Bank Credit Cards, you are charged a fee of Rs. 99 + GST on every redemption request.

### Milestone Benefits

- You get 1,000 bonus points every month on swiping your card 5 times for transactions worth Rs. 1,000 each month.
- The offer is not applicable on fuel transactions made using your credit card in a calendar month.
- The information will be communicated within 30 days of crossing the threshold transaction limit of a calendar month.

### Spend-Based Annual Fee Waiver

The renewal fee of Rs. 1000 is waived if you spend more than Rs. 2 lakh in the previous year.

### Fuel Surcharge Waiver

You get a fuel surcharge waiver of 1% on all your fuel transactions capped at a maximum limit of Rs. 150 per month. Fuel transactions range from Rs 400 to Rs 5,000.

## RBL Platinum Delight Credit Card Review

If you want to save on all your purchases, then the RBL Platinum Delight credit card would be a great choice for you. It comes with exclusive reward offers and other spend-based benefits, such as renewal fee waivers, monthly bonus reward points, etc. What makes it even more advantageous is it offers you 2x Reward Points on weekends, and there is a high chance that you will spend more on weekends than on weekdays. These reward points can be redeemed against various options through their dedicated website with a nominal redemption fee. So, you can go with the card if you make purchases more often on the weekends and if you are looking for a card to let you save more.

## FAQs:

[1\. Where can I use the reward points I earn through my RBL Platinum credit card ?](https://cardinsider.com/rbl-bank/rbl-platinum-delight-credit-card/#chck0)


You can use these reward points for hotel/flight bookings, mobile recharges, shopping vouchers, and several other options available at RBL Rewards.


[2\. How can I get my renewal fee waived off for this card ?](https://cardinsider.com/rbl-bank/rbl-platinum-delight-credit-card/#chck1)


The renewal fee for your RBL Platinum Delight credit card is waived off if you spend over Rs. 1.5 lacs in the previous year.


[3\. Does the RBL Platinum Delight credit card provide Zero Liability Protection ?](https://cardinsider.com/rbl-bank/rbl-platinum-delight-credit-card/#chck2)


No, it doesn’t provide zero liability protection for a lost/stolen card.


[4\. What is the foreign currency markup fee on this card ?](https://cardinsider.com/rbl-bank/rbl-platinum-delight-credit-card/#chck3)


It can be up to 3.5% on all foreign currency spends.


[5\. How many Rewards Points are equal to 1 Rupee ?](https://cardinsider.com/rbl-bank/rbl-platinum-delight-credit-card/#chck4)


For the RBL Platinum Delight credit card, 1 Reward Point = Rs. 0.25, i.e., 4 Reward Points are equal to 1 Rupee.

# HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card

By [Divya Shokeen](https://cardinsider.com/author/divya/ "Posts by Divya Shokeen")

Updated On
18 September, 2025

The HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card is an entry-level credit card offered by HSBC Bank. It comes with no joining fee and an annual fee. It welcomes its customers with exclusive benefits. With this card, you earn 2 Reward Points on every ₹150 you spend. Moreover, you get exciting benefits from the partner brands, including the ‘Buy 1 Get 1 Free’ offer on BookMyShow. This is a must-have card for periodic HSBC discounts and offers on various brands. The fuel surcharge waiver and zero liability protection against a lost card are also among the key features of the card. To know more about the HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card, keep reading:

## HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card

![HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card Features

The Visa Platinum Credit Card offered by HSBC is an entry-level offering by the bank, and it is among the most popular [lifetime free credit cards in India](https://cardinsider.com/best-lifetime-free-credit-cards-india/). With a zero annual fee, it provides you with a decent reward rate and several other benefits across a few more categories, including entertainment, lifestyle, etc. The detailed features and benefits of this card are given below:

### Movie & Dining benefits

You get a “Buy 1 Get 1” offer on booking movie tickets on Saturdays through [BookMyShow](https://in.bookmyshow.com/). The maximum discount limit is capped at ₹250.

## HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card Rewards

The reward rates offered by the HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card in different categories are mentioned below:

- You earn 2 Reward Points on every spend of ₹150.
- No Reward Points shall be earned on transactions related to utilities, tax payments, real estate services, non-financial institutions, money transfers, jewelry purchases, insurance, fuel spends, payments to financial institutions, e-wallet loading, bail and bond payments, as well as education and government-related transactions.

### Reward Redemption

- You can redeem your reward points under the HSBC Credit Cards Rewards Programme for gift vouchers, cosmetics, home appliances, donations, etc.
- On the HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card, you can easily convert your reward points into AirMiles (British Airways/Singapore Airlines).

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Airlines/Hotel** | **Conversion Ratio** |
| Maharaja Club | 2 Reward Points = 1 Maharaja Club Point |
| Singapore Airlines (KrisFlyer) | 2 Reward Points = 1 KrisFlyer Mile |

| Air Asia | 1 Reward Point = 2 Asia Miles |

![Visa Platinum HSBC Credit Card Features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## HSBC Bank Visa Platinum Credit Card Fees & Charges

The Visa Platinum Credit Card by HSBC has some important fees and charges associated with it, and the most important charges of this card are as follows:

- The HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card has no joining/annual fee; i.e., it is a free lifetime credit card.
- The interest rate on this credit card is 3.75% per month, which is charged on the outstanding amount of your card after the payment due date.
- The cash advance fee on the Visa Platinum Credit Card is 2.5% of the amount withdrawn, subject to a minimum fee of ₹500.
- The foreign currency markup fee on this card is 3.5% of the total amount that has been spent in foreign currency transactions.

## HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

In order to get approved for the Visa Platinum Credit Card by HSBC, you will have to follow certain eligibility criteria, which are mentioned below:

- The age of the applicant should lie between 18 years and 65 years.
- The applicant should have a stable source of income with a minimum monthly income of ₹4 lakhs.
- This card is issued to salaried individuals only. Self-employed people can apply only if they have a savings/current account in HSBC.
- The applicant should be an Indian resident.
- The applicant should be from one of these cities: Bengaluru, New Delhi, Gurugram, Hyderabad, Mumbai, Chennai, Noida, Kolkata, Pune, Chandigarh, Jaipur and Ahmedabad.

## How to Apply for The HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card?

If you fulfill the above-mentioned eligibility criteria, you can apply for the card online as well as offline at your convenience. To apply offline, visit your nearest HBC branch (if there is one in your city) with the necessary documents and apply for the card by filling out the physical application form. To apply online, follow the steps given below:

- Click on ‘Apply Now.’
- Start your application by entering your name & mobile number and proceed further.
- Enter your Aadhaar-linked mobile number.

## Conclusion

Recently, HSBC has revalued the Visa Platinum Credit Card, keeping in mind the requirements of those who want to have a lifetime free credit card with maximum savings opportunities. Now it is available on the Visa Signature variant, offering even more benefits. Even after being a card with no joining fee, the card provides its customers with some exclusive welcome benefits, which is one of the exceptional features of the card.

Moreover, you can enjoy accelerated reward points once you reach a total spend of ₹4 lakhs. Being a no-annual-fee credit card with decent benefits, it gives direct competition to some other popular cards like the [ICICI Platinum Chip Credit card](https://cardinsider.com/icici-bank/icici-bank-platinum-chip-credit-card/). Keeping all these advantages of this card in mind, the card can be considered an excellent choice for people who are looking for an affordable card with multiple exciting benefits. This was our review of the HSBC Visa Platinum Credit Card. Make sure to let us know your views on this in the comment section below!

# HSBC Live+ Credit Card – Cashback on Groceries

By [Rajat Gaur](https://cardinsider.com/author/rajat/ "Posts by Rajat Gaur")

Updated On
8 September, 2025

HSBC Cashback Credit Card has now been rebranded as Live+ Credit Card. This card can be the best choice for those fond of shopping online, as it gives you a high cashback of 10% on grocery, food delivery, and dining spends and 1.5% on all other spends. HSBC Cashback Credit Card welcomes you with exclusive benefits, such as a gift voucher from [Amazon](https://www.amazon.in/). Since it’s a cashback card, you don’t have to worry about redeeming rewards like you have to do with reward points-based cards. The cashback is automatically credited back within 45 days of the card statement date.

You also get complimentary domestic lounge access with this credit card. Keep on reading more to learn more about the HSBC Live+ Credit Card.
