## HSBC Live+ Credit Card Features and Rewards

### Welcome Benefit

On making a transaction worth ₹20,000 within the first 30 days of card issuance, cardholders would get ₹1,000 cashback as a welcome gift.

### HSBC Live+ Credit Card Cashback

The Cashback Credit Card by HSBC rewards you in the form of cashback. This cashback is credited back within 45 days of the card statement date. You get:

- 10% accelerated cashback on dining, grocery, and food delivery spends. The maximum cap is ₹1,000 per statement cycle.
- 1.5% unlimited cashback on all other transactions.
- No cash back on the following categories: Fuel, e-wallets, payment of property management fees, rental commissions, rental payments, or any such payments made through MCCs 6513, 7012, 7349, education and government-related, insurance, jewelry & antique items, gambling, tolls, and bridge fees, collection agencies, charity, money transfers, and wholesale clubs.

### Dining & Movie Benefit

- Purchase 2 movie tickets and get 1 free while booking through [BookMyShow](https://in.bookmyshow.com/) on Saturdays. You can book movie tickets for any day of the week.
- The maximum price of the free movie ticket is ₹250.
- Get 15% off on EazyDiner with this credit card.

### Complimentary Domestic Lounge Access

The HSBC Cashback Credit Card gives you four free visits to domestic lounges across India (1 each quarter).

![HSBC Live+ Credit Card Features](https://cardinsider.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/HSBC-Live-Credit-Card-Features.webp)

## HSBC Live+ Credit Card Eligibility

To be eligible for this credit card, you must fulfill the following criteria:

- Must be an Indian citizen.
- Age between 18 and 65 years.
- Should be earning a minimum of ₹4,00,000 per annum (for salaried individuals).
- Must be a resident of Chennai, Gurgaon, Delhi National Capital Region (NCR), Pune, Noida, Hyderabad, Mumbai, Bangalore, Kochi, Coimbatore, Jaipur, Chandigarh, Ahmedabad or Kolkata.

## How to Apply for an HSBC Live+ Credit Card?

You can apply for HSBC credit cards with Card Insider. Simply click on the Apply Now button appearing on your screen. Alternatively, you can call HSBC phone banking, and they will get to you as soon as possible. You can also visit your nearest HSBC branch with the required documents and apply for the card offline.

## Customer Care

If you need any assistance or need to report the loss of a card, you may contact [HSBC’s customer care](https://cardinsider.com/hsbc-bank/credit-card-customer-care-number-email-id/) at 18002673456 or 18001212208. These toll-free numbers are functional for general inquiries from 6:30 a.m. to 8:40 p.m. However, you can report the loss of a card 24×7 on any of these numbers.

## HSBC Live+ Credit Card Review

If online shopping accounts for a large majority of your credit card spends, this card is certainly worth considering. Also, you don’t have to worry about reward redemption, as the cashback amount is automatically credited back within 45 days of the statement date. This makes things easier as you don’t have to be tied to specific brands for reward redemption.

This [cashback credit card](https://cardinsider.com/best-cashback-credit-cards-india/) is especially suited for individuals who frequently make spends on dining, grocery and food delivery as you would get 10% cashback on them. Great cashback, complimentary domestic lounge access, and a welcome benefit worth ₹1,000 are the main features of this card. Please let us know your opinion of this card in the comment section. Also, if you’re an existing cardholder, feel free to share your experience.

## FAQs:

[When do I get the cashback?](https://cardinsider.com/hsbc-bank/hsbc-cashback-credit-card/#chck0)


The cashback amount is automatically credited back within 45 days of the card statement date.


[Do I get zero liability protection with this card?](https://cardinsider.com/hsbc-bank/hsbc-cashback-credit-card/#chck1)


Yes, you get an insurance cover of up to Rs. 3,00,000 against any unauthorized use of the card up to 24 hours prior to reporting the loss of card.


[Do I get any reward points with this card?](https://cardinsider.com/hsbc-bank/hsbc-cashback-credit-card/#chck2)


No, this is not a reward points based card. You only get cashback on your online/offline spends credited within 45 days of the card's statement date.


[Does this card support contactless payments?](https://cardinsider.com/hsbc-bank/hsbc-cashback-credit-card/#chck3)


Yes, this card supports contactless payments with Visa Paywave. No PIN is required for transactions of up to Rs. 5,000


[Can I get an add-on card?](https://cardinsider.com/hsbc-bank/hsbc-cashback-credit-card/#chck4)


Yes, you can get add-on card(s) and no fee is chargeable for the same.


[Do I get cashback on EMI transactions?](https://cardinsider.com/hsbc-bank/hsbc-cashback-credit-card/#chck5)


Yes, you do get cashback on EMI transactions as well.


[Is there an upper limit to the cashback that I can earn in a given month?](https://cardinsider.com/hsbc-bank/hsbc-cashback-credit-card/#chck6)


Yes, the cashback for the 10% category is capped at ₹1,000 per month, while the cashback earned on the 1.5% category is unlimited.

# Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card

By [Kartik Kanwar](https://cardinsider.com/author/kartik/ "Posts by Kartik Kanwar")

Updated On
18 June, 2025

Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card offers you exciting rewards on your purchases. The card carries an annual charge of Rs. 250, which can be waived by spending Rs. 500, making it lifetime free and welcomes you with exclusive offers worth more than the joining fee itself. For every transaction done with the card, you get 1 reward point and 5 reward points per Rs. 150 you spend on dining and fuel purchases. The points earned are easily redeemable against various products and vouchers of your choice.

Besides that, you can also get up to 100% cashback on dining spends within ninety days of your card issuance. To learn more about the card, keep reading.

## Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card

![Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

1 reward point per Rs 150 you spend on all categories and 5 reward points per Rs 150 you spend on dining and fuel transactions


#### Reward Redemption

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/standard-chartered/standard-chartered-platinum-rewards-credit-card/#)

- 5X Reward Points on every Rs 150 spent on dining and fuel transactions.
- Earn 1 reward point per Rs 150 you spend on all other categories.
- Renewal Fee Waiver on spend of Rs. 500 in the first 30 days.

## Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card Features and Benefits

The Platinum Rewards Credit Card issued by Standard Chartered Bank is an [entry-level credit card](https://cardinsider.com/best-credit-cards-in-india/) having a decent reward rate along with several basic features that are listed further in this article:

### Lifetime Free

By spending Rs. 500 in the first 30 days of card issuance, you can avail this card as LTF.

## Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card Reward Rate

- Get 1 reward point per Rs. 150 you spend on all categories.
- You get 5 reward points per Rs. 150 you spend on dining and fuel transactions with no monthly capped limit on your purchases with a platinum rewards card.

### Reward Redemption

- The Reward Points earned are redeemable through net banking or at the 360 rewards portal.
- Reward points are also redeemable by calling [Standard Chartered Bank customer care](https://cardinsider.com/standard-chartered/credit-card-customer-care-number/).
- Redeem earned points against retail items and gift vouchers (at a rate of 1 RP = Re. 0.25).

## Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card Fees and Charges

Following are the important fees and charges associated with the StanChart Platinum Rewards Credit Card:

- The joining & renewal fee of the Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Card is Rs. 250. Lifetime free if you spend Rs. 500 within the first 30 days of card issuance.
- The interest charges applicable on this credit card are 3.75% monthly.
- 3.5% of the foreign currency transaction amount is applicable as the forex markup fee on this card.
- The cash advance fee of 3% of the transacted amount subject to a minimum of Rs. 300 is applicable on cash advances.

![Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card Features](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

The following are the criteria that need to be fulfilled by the applicant to be eligible for the Standard Chartered Bank Platinum Rewards Credit Card:

- The applicant should be between 21 – 65 years of age.
- The additional cardholder’s minimum age is subject to 18 years.
- Salaried or Self-employed applicants should have a minimum Gross Monthly Income of Rs 20,000/-

## Documents Required

The table given below summarizes the types of documents that are acceptable when applying for the card:

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Document Proof** | **Documents acceptable** |
| Identity Proof | PAN Card, Ration Card, Aadhaar card, Voter’s ID, etc. |
| Address Proof | Passport, Driving License, Utility Bills, Aadhaar card, Ration card, etc. |
| Income Proof | Latest bank statements or salary slips, Latest ITR. |

## How To Apply For The Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card?

If you wish to apply for the Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card, then there are both offline and online methods that you can follow. For the offline procedure, you need to visit your nearest SC branch, if it is available in the city where you live, fill in the application form, and submit all the necessary documents. However, if you wish to apply online, then the following steps will help you to apply online through Bank’s website:

- Click on Apply Now.
- Fill in your details.
- Proceed with the application.

## Conclusion

The Standard Chartered Platinum Reward credit card would be a good choice for you in case you are a person with no credit background and looking to apply for one. This card gives you a great reward rate on spending on all categories, and you also get up to 5x reward points per 150 spent on dining and fuel. This card carries a nominal renewal fee of Rs  250, on which a spend-based waiver is provided if you spend Rs. 500 in the first 30 days of card issuance. But, if you are a high-end customer, this card might not be a great choice for you since this card does not provide you with enough rewards and benefits.

Another basic credit card that you must look for is the [American Express SmartEarn Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/american-express/american-express-smartearn-credit-card/), which is an affordable credit card with great deals. This was the Standard Chartered Platinum Rewards Credit Card review. Make sure to drop your take on the card in the comments below.

# YES Bank RESERV Credit Card

By [Rajat Gaur](https://cardinsider.com/author/rajat/ "Posts by Rajat Gaur")

Updated On
8 September, 2025

The RESERV Credit Card, earlier known as the First Exclusive credit card, is a high-end offering by Yes Bank. It can be considered a direct competitor to [HDFC Regalia Gold](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/hdfc-bank-regalia-gold-credit-card/) Credit Card. The card comes with complimentary access to both international and domestic airport lounges. Apart from that, what makes this card such a lucrative offering in the premium segment is one of the lowest foreign currency markups by industry standards (1.75%). But that’s not it; since it is a premium card, you also get many other benefits, like complimentary rounds of golf and movie ticket bookings at a discounted rate. Read the article for further information about this card.

## YES Bank RESERV Credit Card

![Yes Bank First Exclusive/RESERV Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

(4.5/5)


#### Joining Fee


₹2,499 + GST


#### Renewal Fee


₹2,499 + GST


#### Best Suited For

Travel \| Shopping \|

#### Reward Type

Reward Points
\|

#### Welcome Benefits

N/A

[Rewards and Benefits](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-bank-first-exclusive-credit-card/#)

#### Movie & Dining

N/A


#### Rewards Rate

24 Reward Points for every Rs. 200 spent online. 12 Reward Points for every Rs. 200 spent on offline shopping and 6 RPs on select categories


#### Reward Redemption


Reward Points Can be Redeemed on the YesRewardz Portal Against Multiple Options


#### Travel


N/A


#### Domestic Lounge Access


12 Domestic Lounge Visits Every Year (3 Per Quarter) on Spending ₹1 Lakh or More in the Previous Quarter


#### International Lounge Access


6 Lounge Visits Outside India Each Year


#### Golf

4 Complimentary Rounds of Golf With Green Fee Waived and 1 Complimentary Golf Lesson Per Year


#### Insurance Benefits


Insurance coverage is worth Rs. 3 crore if the cardholder's life is lost due to an air accident, and medical insurance is worth Rs. 50 lakh in case of emergency overseas hospitalization and a Credit Shield cover (up to Rs. 10 lakh) against outstanding card dues in the unfortunate event of the primary cardholder's death.


[Fees & Charges](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-bank-first-exclusive-credit-card/#)

#### Spend-Based Waiver


The Joining Fee is Waived on Spending Rs. 40,000 Within 30 Days of Card Issuance. and the Renewal Fee is Waived on Annual Spends of Rs. 3 Lakh or More.


#### Rewards Redemption Fee

Rs. 100 + GST


#### Foreign Currency Markup


1.75% (plus applicable taxes)

#### Interest Rates

2.99% per month (35.88% annually)


#### Fuel Surcharge

1% fuel surcharge is waived on all fuel purchases at all fuel stations across India for transactions between Rs. 400 and Rs. 5000 (waiver capped at Rs. 1000 per month).


#### Cash Advance Charges


2.5% Or Rs. 500 (Whichever is Higher)

[Product Details](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-bank-first-exclusive-credit-card/#)

- 24 Reward Points for every ₹200 spent on online shopping.
- 12 Reward Points for every ₹200 spent offline.
- 6 Reward Points on every spend of ₹200 on select categories like insurance, education, government, and wallet payments.

## Yes Bank RESERV Credit Card Features and Benefits

The RESERV Credit Card by YES Bank is a well-known credit card in the Indian market. It comes with a number of exciting benefits across various categories. Here are some of the most attractive features and benefits of this credit card:

### Travel Benefits

Travel benefits with this credit card include:

- Travel offers on the Yes Privileges portal.
- 12 domestic lounge access (3 per quarter) at select airports across India. This is also available for add-on credit card holders.
- 6 complimentary lounge access outside India for primary and add-on cardholders.
- Reward Points are transferable to partner airlines’ AirMiles.

_Effective 1st April 2025, access to domestic airport lounges will be based on spending criteria._

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Lounge Access Quarter** | **Spend Threshold** |
| Jan – March | ₹1 Lakh |
| April – Jun | ₹1 Lakh |
| July – Sept | ₹1 Lakh |
| Oct – Dec | ₹1 Lakh |

### Insurance Benefits

A credit shield cover worth ₹15 lakh against the card’s balance amount in case of accidental death of the primary cardholder.

### Lower Foreign Currency Markup

At 1.75%, the foreign currency markup for this credit is one of the lowest in the market.

### Renewal Benefit

Get 8,000 Reward Points on payment of renewal charges, which are applicable from the second year.

### Fuel Surcharge

The convenience fee on purchases (between ₹400 and ₹5,000) is waived at all refill stations across India (the maximum benefit under this waiver is capped at ₹1,000 per month).

## YES First Exclusive/RESERV Credit Card Rewards

As it is a Reward Points-based credit card, you get Reward Points on transactions made with the card. You get:

- 24 Reward Points for every Rs. 200 spent on online shopping.
- 12 Reward Points for every Rs. 200 spent on offline shopping.
- Select category transactions shall earn 6 Reward Points per Rs. 200 spent. These include insurance, education, and government payments.
- A maximum of 36,000 Reward Points can be earned in a statement cycle.
- Yes Bank Reward Points are valid for 3 years from the accumulation date.
- Reward Points will not be awarded for fuel transactions, cash withdrawals, rental/wallet payments, and transactions converted into EMI.

### Rewards Redemption

Yes Bank Reward Points can be redeemed for purchasing various products, e-vouchers, travel bookings, and movie tickets on the Yes Rewardz portal.

![YES RESERV Credit Card Benefits](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20800%20419'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

## Yes Bank First Exclusive/RESERV Credit Card Eligibility

The eligibility criteria that need to be fulfilled by the applicants in order to get approved for the YES Bank First Exclusive Credit Card are as follows:

- Must be a resident of India,
- Must be between the age of 21 and 60 years,
- Should have a minimum monthly income of ₹2 lakh or annual income of ₹18 lakh or above.

## How to Apply for Yes RESERV Credit Card?

YES Bank provides you with online as well as offline methods to apply for the RESERV Credit Card. To apply online, you can click on the Apply Now button appearing on your screen. Fill in a few details, and you will have submitted your application for the Yes Bank First Exclusive/Reserv Credit Card. If you don’t prefer online methods, you always have the option to visit your nearest Yes Bank branch. You will need identity proof, address proof, and a passport-size photo of yours along with proof of income such as salary slip(s) in case of salaried employees and ITR documents for self-employed applicants.

_Those who already hold a Yes Bank Credit Card shall not be eligible for the RESERVE Card._

## Yes Bank RESERV Credit Card Review

If you frequently fly internationally, this credit card is worth considering. It offers six complimentary lounge accesses at airport lounges outside of India, which is a significant benefit. One unique aspect is that free international lounge access is also available for add-on cardholders. However, while this card may seem attractive at first glance, there are several drawbacks, such as lounge access being based on spending requirements.

There are alternative cards to consider, including the IDFC Ashva, HDFC Diners Club Privilege, and [Axis Horizon Credit Card](https://cardinsider.com/axis-bank/axis-bank-horizon-credit-card/). Despite its limitations, the Yes Reserv Credit Card may still be a good choice for high-net-worth individuals. We’d love to hear your thoughts on this card in the comments section.

## FAQs:

[Can I use the reward points whenever I want?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-bank-first-exclusive-credit-card/#chck0)


The reward points earned are valid for 3 years from the date of accumulation and expire thereafter.


[How many complimentary lounge access do I get outside India?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-bank-first-exclusive-credit-card/#chck1)


You get 6 lounge access at airports outside India.


[Does the bank offer zero liability protection in case I lose my card?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-bank-first-exclusive-credit-card/#chck2)


Yes, you are not liable for any fraudulent transaction made using your card, post the time of reporting the loss of your card. However, for any transaction, before you have reported the loss of card to the bank’s customer care, the liability of all the transactions made using your card lies solely with you.


[What is the Yes Rewardz portal?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-bank-first-exclusive-credit-card/#chck3)


Yes Rewardz is Yes Bank’s reward redemption portal where you can redeem your reward points for exclusive products, shopping vouchers, and hotel/flight/movie ticket bookings.

# YES Elite+ Credit Card

By [Bharti Bisht](https://cardinsider.com/author/bharti/ "Posts by Bharti Bisht")

Updated On
8 September, 2025

The YES Premia Credit Card, now known as the Yes Bank Elite Credit Card, is a premium card aiming at providing its cardholders with premium benefits & offers across various categories. It is issued at a joining fee of ₹999, on which you can get a waiver if you reach the total eligible spends of ₹20,000 within thirty days of card activation. Coming to its reward offers, you earn 12 Reward Points/₹200 spent for online shopping and 6 Reward Points/₹200 you spend offline. Moreover, you get a 25% discount on movies on BookMyShow with this card.

The YES Elite+ Credit Card, offered by Yes Bank, is a mid-segment card that offers a range of exclusive benefits in various categories such as travel, insurance, movies, dining, rewards, and more. One of its perks is free access to domestic and international lounges. Additionally, this card also allows you to save on foreign currency transactions with its low foreign currency markup fee of just 2.75%. Refer to the detailed information provided for more information about the benefits of this card.

## YES Elite+ Credit Card

![YES Premia/Elite+ Credit Card](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E)

#### Rewards Rate

12 RPs for every Rs. 200 spent online. 6 RPs per Rs. 200 you spend offline. 4 RPs on select categories like insurance, education, and rental payments.


#### Reward Redemption


RPs Earned Are Redeemable Through Yes Rewardz (1 RP = Re. 0.25 Against Hotel/Flight Bookings)


#### Travel


N/A


#### Domestic Lounge Access


2 complimentary lounge access per quarter.


#### International Lounge Access


3 Free Visits in a Year

Joining Fee Waiver - Spend ₹20,000 within 30 Days of Card Issuance. Annual Fee Waiver on Spends of ₹2 Lakh or More


#### Rewards Redemption Fee

₹100 + GST


#### Foreign Currency Markup


2.75% on all foreign currency transactions.

3.99% Per Month (47.88% Annually)


#### Fuel Surcharge

1% fuel surcharge waiver on fuel transactions between Rs. 400 and Rs. 5,000.


#### Cash Advance Charges


2.5% or ₹500 (Whichever is Higher)

## YES Premia/ELITE+ Credit Card Rewards

- You earn **12 Reward Points/₹200 for online shopping.** There is a maximum cap for these Reward Points, which is at 12,000 RPs per statement cycle.
- You earn **6 Reward Points/₹200 for offline shopping.**
- Get **4 Reward Points per ₹200** you spend on utilities, government services, education, and insurance payments. Reward earnings on utility payments are capped at 300 RPs per statement cycle.
- **Rewards Points are valid for a period of 36 months.**
- A **1% charge** will apply to **utility transactions over ₹15,000/month** and on all **rental and wallet transactions**
- No Reward Points shall be earned for fuel transactions, rental payments, wallet transactions, EMI spends, and cash withdrawals.

### Rewards Redemption

- The Reward Points earned are redeemable through [Yes Rewardz](https://credit.yesrewardz.com/) for movie/flight/hotel bookings.
- 1 RP = ₹0.25 when redeeming against hotel/flight bookings.

### Rewards Subscription Plan

You can also opt for a Rewards Subscription Plan which would allow you to earn accelerated Reward Points on specified categories. _Additional YES Rewardz Points per statement cycle will be capped at 75,000 per customer._

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Subscription Plan** | **Fee (One Year)** | **RPs** |
| **First** | ₹1,500 | 3X YES Rewardz Points (Offline or Select Category Spends) |
| **Second** | ₹2,500 | 5X YES Rewardz Points (Travel, Dining, Fashion and Healthcare) |

## Joining and Renewal Fee Waiver

On **spending ₹20,000** in the first 30 days of card activation, you can get the **joining fee** of the card **waived**. The **annual renewal fee can also be waived by spending ₹2 lakh** or above in a year.

## Free Domestic Airport Lounge Access

You get 2 complimentary domestic lounge access per calendar quarter (for primary cardholders only). This means that you can **access domestic lounges eight times a year** without having to pay any additional charges. Starting **April 1st, 2024**, access to domestic airport lounges will be **based on spending criteria.**

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Lounge Access Quarter** | **Required Spending** |
| April – June | ₹10,000 |
| July – September | ₹35,000 |

## Complimentary Airport Lounge Access Outside India

3 complimentary airport lounge access outside India per year with the Yes Elite Plus Credit Card.

## Movie Benefits

Get a 25% discount on movies on booking via BookMyShow (website and app) using your YES Elite+ Credit Card. You can avail of a maximum benefit of ₹250 in a calendar month with this offer. This [benefit on movie ticket bookings](https://cardinsider.com/best-credit-cards-for-movie-tickets-in-india/) would allow you to save up to ₹3,000 in a year with the Elite Credit Card.

## Golf Benefits

You get 4 green fee waivers at selected golf courses across India. Enjoy 1 complimentary lesson for golf every calendar month at selected golf courses all over India.

## Insurance Benefits

You get a credit shield cover worth ₹3 lakhs, a lost card liability of ₹3 lakh along with a Purchase Protection Plan (Up to ₹50,000).

## Fuel Surcharge Waiver

Get a **1% waiver on the fuel surcharge** for fuel transactions above ₹400 and below ₹5,000. The **maximum benefit of ₹250** every month can be availed through the fuel surcharge waiver.

## Low Foreign Currency Markup Fee

The YES Premia/ELITE Credit Card comes with a **low foreign currency markup fee of 2.75%**, allowing you to save on your foreign currency spends.

## Dining Benefits

Enjoy discounts of up to 25% with the Yes Bank Dining Fiesta program at over 1,000 select restaurants.

## YES Premia/ELITE Credit Card Eligibility Criteria

When applying for the Yes ELITE+ Credit Card, certain criteria need to be fulfilled by the applicant to be eligible for the card. The details of the same has been mentioned below:

- To be eligible for the YES ELITE Credit Card, the applicant should be between 21 years & 60 years of age.
- Applicants can be either self-employed or salaried.
- The applicant’s income should be a minimum of ₹1 lakhs per month or an Income Tax Return of 9 lakhs or more.

## How to Apply for YES ELITE+ Credit Card?

Follow the steps given below to apply for the card online, and if you want to apply for the card offline, visit the bank branch:

- Click on ‘Apply Now.’
- You shall be redirected to the application page.
- Proceed by filling out the application form.
- Submit your application.

## YES ELITE Credit Card Review

Issued by YES Bank, the YES ELITE+ Credit Card, also known as the Premia/Elite Card, can be considered an ideal credit card as it has all the key features that one might be looking for in a credit card. It is very much comparable with the [Tata Neu Infinity](https://cardinsider.com/hdfc-bank/tata-neu-infinity-hdfc-credit-card/) Credit Card, which comes with similar privileges. Along with providing exclusive reward offers, the ELITE Card comes with exciting travel benefits; discount offers on movies, golf benefits, fuel surcharge waiver, joining/renewal fee waiver, and many more features.

The YES Premia/ELITE+/Elite Credit Card has numerous benefits, one of which is its low foreign currency markup fee of just 2.75%. This fee is much lower than other cards, making it an ideal choice for those who frequently make international transactions. In addition to the low markup fee, the card also offers free international and domestic lounge access, further enhancing its value. With all these benefits in mind, it can be concluded that the YES Premia/ELITE+ Credit Card is an excellent option for individuals seeking exclusive benefits at a low joining fee of Rs. 999 + Applicable Taxes. _If you’re looking for a card with these features, the YES ELITE Credit Card is definitely worth considering._

## FAQs:

[Can I get the Yes Elite+ Credit Card against a Fixed Deposit?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-premia-credit-card/#chck0)


Yes, the Elite+ Credit Card can also be opted for as a secured credit card i.e. against a fixed deposit.


[Does the card offer complimentary international lounge access?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-premia-credit-card/#chck1)


Yes, 3 complimentary international lounge access are offered along with the Yes Elite+ Credit Card. After these 3 complimentary visits, you will be charged.


[How can I apply online for the YES Elite+ Credit Card?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-premia-credit-card/#chck2)


Simply click on Apply Now to apply for the Yes Bank Elite Plus Credit Card. You would need to submit a few details about yourself such as your PAN Card, employment details, etc. Check eligibility before applying. It's also best to keep all the necessary documents handy.


[What is the foreign currency markup fee applicable with this card?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-premia-credit-card/#chck3)


The foreign currency markup fee of 2.75% on all foreign currency transactions is applicable on the YES Premia Credit Card.


[Can I get the joining fee waived?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-premia-credit-card/#chck4)


If you spend ₹20,000 or more within the first 30 days after receiving your YES Elite+ Credit Card you can get the joining fee waived.


[What is the Reward redemption fee applicable on this card?](https://cardinsider.com/yes-bank/yes-premia-credit-card/#chck5)


The reward redemption fee of ₹100 on every redemption request is charged on the YES Premia credit card.
